atlas/modules/domain_module.py191 lines
"""
Domain / website investigation module.
Independent lookups run concurrently; per-host rate limiting still applies.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from typing import Any, Dict

from ..sources.cert_transparency import search_crtsh
from ..sources.dns_lookup import get_dns_records
from ..sources.geoip import lookup_ip_geo
from ..sources.provider_hints import infer_providers
from ..sources.rdap import query_rdap_domain
from ..sources.wayback import query_wayback
from ..sources.web_presence import basic_web_info
from ..utils.helpers import (
    confidence_label, is_offline, is_public_ip, make_progress, resolve_hostname, timestamp,
)

logger = logging.getLogger("atlas.modules.domain")

LEGAL_NOTES = [
    "Domain RDAP, DNS, and Certificate Transparency data are public by design.",
    "Observing a public website's headers and title is generally permitted passive research.",
    "Do not attempt to bypass authentication, scrape behind logins, or test for vulnerabilities without authorisation.",
    "UK Computer Misuse Act 1990 prohibits unauthorised access. This tool only requests publicly reachable resources.",
    "If the domain relates to an individual (e.g. personal site), treat any personal data under UK GDPR / DPA 2018 principles.",
]


def _conf(score: float, note: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {"level": confidence_label(score), "score": score, "note": note}


def _resolve_and_geo(domain: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    ips = resolve_hostname(domain)
    out: Dict[str, Any] = {"ips": ips, "geo": None}
    public = [ip for ip in ips if is_public_ip(ip)]
    if public:
        out["geo"] = lookup_ip_geo(public[0])
        out["geo_ip"] = public[0]
    return out


def investigate_domain(domain: str, progress_callback=None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    progress = make_progress(progress_callback, logger)

    report: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "target": domain, "target_type": "DOMAIN",
        "started_at": timestamp(), "completed_at": None,
        "sections": {}, "confidence_summary": {}, "sources_used": [],
        "limitations": [], "legal_notes": list(LEGAL_NOTES), "key_findings": [],
    }

    progress("Running RDAP, DNS, resolution, Certificate Transparency and HTTP checks in parallel...")
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=6, thread_name_prefix="atlas") as pool:
        f_rdap = pool.submit(query_rdap_domain, domain)
        f_dns = pool.submit(get_dns_records, domain)
        f_res = pool.submit(_resolve_and_geo, domain)
        f_ct = pool.submit(search_crtsh, domain)
        f_web = pool.submit(basic_web_info, domain)
        f_way = pool.submit(query_wayback, domain)

        def done(name: str, fut):
            try:
                value = fut.result()
            except Exception as e:  # one failing source must not sink the report
                logger.exception("%s lookup crashed", name)
                value = {"success": False, "errors": [f"{name} crashed: {e}"]}
            progress(f"{name} complete")
            return value

        rdap = done("RDAP", f_rdap)
        dns = done("DNS", f_dns)
        res = done("Resolution/geo", f_res)
        ct = done("Certificate Transparency", f_ct)
        web = done("HTTP", f_web)
        way = done("Web history", f_way)

    # --- RDAP
    report["sections"]["registration"] = rdap
    if rdap.get("success"):
        report["sources_used"].append(rdap.get("source") or "RDAP")
        report["confidence_summary"]["registration"] = _conf(0.80, "RDAP registration data")
    else:
        report["confidence_summary"]["registration"] = _conf(
            0.25, "RDAP failed – domain may be new, restricted, or service unavailable")
        report["limitations"].append("Domain registration (RDAP) data incomplete.")

    # --- DNS
    report["sections"]["dns"] = dns
    if dns.get("success"):
        report["sources_used"].append(f"DNS ({dns.get('method')})")
        report["confidence_summary"]["dns"] = _conf(0.90, "Live DNS resolution")
    else:
        report["confidence_summary"]["dns"] = _conf(0.2, "DNS lookup failed")

    # --- Resolution + geo
    ips = res.get("ips", [])
    report["sections"]["resolved_ips"] = {"ips": ips, "count": len(ips)}
    non_public = [ip for ip in ips if not is_public_ip(ip)]
    if non_public:
        report["limitations"].append(
            f"Domain resolves to non-public address(es) ({', '.join(non_public[:3])}); "
            "these were not queried or contacted."
        )
    geo = res.get("geo")
    if geo:
        report["sections"]["primary_ip_geo"] = geo
        if geo.get("success"):
            report["sources_used"].append(geo.get("source") or "GeoIP")

    # --- Certificate Transparency
    report["sections"]["certificate_transparency"] = ct
    if ct.get("success"):
        report["sources_used"].append("crt.sh")
        report["confidence_summary"]["subdomains_ct"] = _conf(
            0.75, f"Found {len(ct.get('unique_names', []))} unique names in CT logs")
    else:
        report["confidence_summary"]["subdomains_ct"] = _conf(0.2, "CT search failed or returned no data")

    # --- Web presence
    report["sections"]["web_presence"] = web
    if web.get("success"):
        report["sources_used"].append(web.get("final_url") or domain)
        report["confidence_summary"]["web"] = _conf(0.70, "Public HTTP response observed")
    else:
        report["limitations"].append("No HTTP response could be obtained from the domain.")

    # --- Web history (Internet Archive)
    report["sections"]["web_history"] = way
    if way.get("success"):
        report["sources_used"].append("web.archive.org (CDX API)")
        report["confidence_summary"]["web_history"] = _conf(
            0.7 if way.get("captures_found") else 0.4,
            "Internet Archive capture dates (shows the site existed then, not who ran it)")
    elif not is_offline():
        report["limitations"].append("Internet Archive history could not be retrieved.")

    # --- Related domains (certificate co-listing) and provider inference (DNS)
    related = ct.get("related_domains") or []
    if related:
        report["sections"]["related_domains"] = {
            "domains": related[:15], "note": ct.get("related_domains_note"), "confidence": "LOW",
            "source": "crt.sh (shared certificates; multi-tenant CDN certificates excluded)"}
    prov = infer_providers((dns.get("records") or {}) if dns.get("success") else {})
    if prov["email"] or prov["dns"] or prov["sending_services"]:
        report["sections"]["provider_inference"] = prov

    # ---- key findings (each with its source)
    kf = report["key_findings"]
    summ = rdap.get("raw_summary") or {}
    for ev in summ.get("events") or []:
        if isinstance(ev, dict) and ev.get("eventAction") in ("registration", "expiration", "last changed"):
            kf.append({"finding": f"Domain {ev['eventAction']}: {ev.get('eventDate')}", "source": rdap.get("source"),
                       "confidence": "HIGH"})
    if summ.get("nameservers"):
        kf.append({"finding": f"Name servers: {', '.join(n for n in summ['nameservers'] if n)}",
                   "source": rdap.get("source"), "confidence": "HIGH"})
    if ips:
        kf.append({"finding": f"Resolves to: {', '.join(ips[:6])}", "source": "DNS resolution", "confidence": "HIGH"})
    if ct.get("success"):
        kf.append({"finding": f"{len(ct.get('unique_names', []))} related host name(s) seen in Certificate Transparency logs",
                   "source": "crt.sh", "confidence": "MEDIUM"})
    if way.get("captures_found"):
        kf.append({"finding": f"Internet Archive has captures from {way['first_capture']} to {way['last_capture']} "
                              f"({way['months_with_captures']} months with captures)",
                   "source": way.get("first_snapshot_url"), "confidence": "MEDIUM"})
    elif way.get("success"):
        kf.append({"finding": "The Internet Archive has no captures of this domain (new, private or excluded site)",
                   "source": "web.archive.org (CDX API)", "confidence": "MEDIUM"})
    if related:
        kf.append({"finding": "Possibly related domains on shared certificates: " + ", ".join(r["domain"] for r in related[:6]),
                   "source": "crt.sh", "confidence": "LOW"})
    if prov["email"] or prov["dns"]:
        kf.append({"finding": "Provider inference – email: " + (", ".join(prov["email"]) or "unknown") +
                              "; DNS: " + (", ".join(prov["dns"]) or "unknown"),
                   "source": "DNS records", "confidence": "MEDIUM"})
    if web.get("success"):
        kf.append({"finding": f"Website responded HTTP {web.get('status_code')}"
                              f"{'; title: ' + web['title'] if web.get('title') else ''}",
                   "source": web.get("final_url"), "confidence": "HIGH"})
    if is_offline():
        report["limitations"].append("Offline mode: no network lookups were performed.")

    report["completed_at"] = timestamp()
    return report
atlas/modules/ip_module.py192 lines
"""
IP address investigation module.
Passive only.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import ipaddress
import logging
from typing import Any, Dict

from ..sources.geoip import lookup_ip_geo, parse_asn
from ..sources.rdap import query_rdap_ip
from ..sources.reputation import check_ip_reputation
from ..utils.helpers import confidence_label, is_offline, make_progress, timestamp
from ..utils.network_safety import ip_category, parse_ip

logger = logging.getLogger("atlas.modules.ip")

LEGAL_NOTES = [
    "Querying public RDAP, DNS, and free geolocation services for an IP address is generally considered passive public research.",
    "Do not attempt to map an IP to a specific individual or private residence without lawful authority.",
    "Geolocation data is approximate and must not be treated as precise personal location information.",
    "Under UK GDPR / DPA 2018, IP addresses can be personal data in certain contexts. Processing must have a lawful basis if personal data is involved.",
    "This tool performs only passive lookups and does not attempt unauthorised access (Computer Misuse Act 1990).",
]


HOSTING_KEYWORDS = (
    "hosting", "cloud", "data center", "datacenter", "datacentre", "colo", "vps", "server", "amazon", "aws",
    "google", "microsoft", "azure", "digitalocean", "ovh", "hetzner", "linode", "akamai", "cloudflare",
    "fastly", "oracle", "alibaba", "vultr", "leaseweb", "contabo",
)

UNCERTAINTY = (
    "An IP address is not proof of a person's identity or residence: it identifies a network endpoint that may be "
    "shared (NAT, mobile carriers, VPNs, proxies, CDNs, corporate gateways) and may be reassigned over time."
)


def _hosting_indicators(rdap, geo) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    reasons = []
    gdata = (geo or {}).get("data") or {}
    if gdata.get("is_hosting"):
        reasons.append("Geolocation provider flags the address as hosting / data-centre.")
    if gdata.get("is_proxy"):
        reasons.append("Geolocation provider flags the address as proxy / VPN / relay.")
    text = " ".join(str(x or "") for x in (
        (rdap or {}).get("raw_summary", {}).get("name"), gdata.get("organisation"), gdata.get("isp"),
        gdata.get("as_name"), gdata.get("hostname"))).lower()
    hits = sorted({k for k in HOSTING_KEYWORDS if k in text})
    if hits:
        reasons.append(f"Network/organisation name contains hosting-related terms: {', '.join(hits)}.")
    return {"likely_hosting_or_datacentre": bool(reasons), "indicators": reasons,
            "confidence": "LOW" if reasons else "VERY LOW / UNVERIFIED",
            "note": "Heuristic only; absence of indicators does not mean the address is residential."}


def _conf(score: float, note: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {"level": confidence_label(score), "score": score, "note": note}


def investigate_ip(ip: str, progress_callback=None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Full passive investigation of a public IP address."""
    progress = make_progress(progress_callback, logger)

    report: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "target": ip, "target_type": "IP",
        "started_at": timestamp(), "completed_at": None,
        "sections": {}, "confidence_summary": {}, "sources_used": [],
        "limitations": [], "legal_notes": list(LEGAL_NOTES), "key_findings": [],
    }

    progress("Determining IP version and basic properties...")
    try:
        cat = ip_category(ip)
        addr = parse_ip(ip)
    except ValueError:
        report["sections"]["basic"] = {"error": "Invalid IP address"}
        report["completed_at"] = timestamp()
        return report

    ip = cat["normalised"]
    report["target"] = ip
    report["sections"]["basic"] = {
        "ip_version": f"IPv{addr.version}", "category": cat["category"], "is_global": cat["is_global"],
        "explanation": cat["explanation"], "is_private": addr.is_private, "is_loopback": addr.is_loopback,
        "is_reserved": addr.is_reserved, "is_multicast": addr.is_multicast,
        "is_link_local": addr.is_link_local, "notes": [UNCERTAINTY],
    }
    report["key_findings"].append({"finding": f"{ip} is {cat['category']}: {cat['explanation']}",
                                   "source": "Python ipaddress (local analysis)", "confidence": "HIGH"})

    # Non-global addresses mean nothing to public registries; don't send them out.
    if not cat["is_global"]:
        report["sections"]["basic"]["notes"].append(
            "No external lookups were performed because public registry, geolocation and reputation data "
            "would be meaningless for this address.")
        report["limitations"].append(f"{cat['category'].title()} address – external lookups skipped.")
        report["completed_at"] = timestamp()
        return report
    if is_offline():
        report["limitations"].append("Offline mode: no registry, geolocation or reputation lookups were made.")

    progress("Querying public RDAP registries for network registration...")
    rdap = query_rdap_ip(ip)
    report["sections"]["rdap"] = rdap
    if rdap.get("success"):
        report["sources_used"].append(rdap.get("source") or "RDAP")
        report["confidence_summary"]["network_registration"] = _conf(
            0.85, "RDAP data from regional internet registry")
    else:
        report["confidence_summary"]["network_registration"] = _conf(
            0.2, "RDAP lookup failed or incomplete")
        report["limitations"].append("Could not retrieve RDAP registration data.")

    progress("Looking up approximate geolocation and ISP information (cross-checking providers)...")
    geo = lookup_ip_geo(ip)
    report["sections"]["geolocation"] = geo
    cc = geo.get("cross_check") or {}
    if geo.get("success"):
        report["sources_used"].append(geo.get("source") or "GeoIP")
        if cc.get("country_agreement") is True:
            score, note = 0.6, ("Two independent providers agree on the country"
                                + ("" if cc.get("city_agreement") else "; city/region differ or are unconfirmed")
                                + ". Network location only – never street-level or personal.")
        elif cc.get("country_agreement") is False:
            score, note = 0.15, "Providers DISAGREE on the country – treat the location as unreliable."
            report["limitations"].append("Geolocation providers disagree on the country; the location is unreliable.")
        else:
            score, note = 0.4, "Single provider only – approximate network location, could not be cross-checked."
        report["confidence_summary"]["geolocation"] = _conf(score, note)
    else:
        report["confidence_summary"]["geolocation"] = _conf(0.1, "Geolocation lookup failed")

    # accuracy cross-check: registry (RDAP) country vs geolocation country
    reg_country = ((rdap or {}).get("raw_summary") or {}).get("country")
    geo_cc = ((geo or {}).get("data") or {}).get("country_code")
    checks: Dict[str, Any] = {"geolocation_providers": cc.get("providers", []),
                              "geolocation_country_agreement": cc.get("country_agreement"),
                              "registry_country": reg_country, "geolocation_country": geo_cc}
    if reg_country and geo_cc:
        checks["registry_vs_geolocation"] = "match" if reg_country.upper() == str(geo_cc).upper() else "differs"
        if checks["registry_vs_geolocation"] == "differs":
            checks["explanation"] = ("The registry country reflects where the network operator is registered; the "
                                     "geolocation country estimates where the address is used. They often differ for "
                                     "multinational, hosting and CDN networks and are not an error by themselves.")
    report["sections"]["accuracy_checks"] = checks

    progress("Checking selected public reputation / blocklist sources...")
    rep = check_ip_reputation(ip)
    report["sections"]["reputation"] = rep
    if rep.get("success"):
        report["sources_used"].extend(rep.get("sources_checked", []))
        findings = [f for f in rep.get("findings", []) if f.get("source") != "Manual recommendation"]
        listed = [f for f in findings if f.get("listed") is True]
        unknown = [f for f in findings if f.get("listed") is None]
        if listed:
            report["confidence_summary"]["reputation"] = _conf(
                0.7, f"{len(listed)} positive listing(s) among checked sources")
        elif unknown:
            report["confidence_summary"]["reputation"] = _conf(
                0.3, f"No listings, but {len(unknown)} source(s) gave no usable answer (refused / rate-limited)")
        else:
            report["confidence_summary"]["reputation"] = _conf(0.55, "No listings among checked public RBLs")
    else:
        report["limitations"].append("Blocklist checks could not be completed.")

    # ---- derived findings
    summ = (rdap or {}).get("raw_summary") or {}
    if rdap.get("success") and summ.get("name"):
        report["key_findings"].append({
            "finding": f"Registered network: {summ.get('name')} ({summ.get('start_address')} – {summ.get('end_address')}), "
                       f"registry country {summ.get('country') or 'n/a'}.",
            "source": rdap.get("source"), "confidence": "HIGH"})
    gdata = geo.get("data") or {}
    asn = parse_asn(gdata.get("as"), gdata.get("organisation"))
    if geo.get("success"):
        report["key_findings"].append({
            "finding": f"Network operator (ISP/organisation): {gdata.get('isp') or gdata.get('organisation') or 'unknown'}"
                       f"{'; ' + asn if asn else ''}.",
            "source": geo.get("source"), "confidence": "LOW"})
    hosting = _hosting_indicators(rdap, geo)
    report["sections"]["hosting_indicators"] = hosting
    if hosting["likely_hosting_or_datacentre"]:
        report["key_findings"].append({"finding": "Indicators suggest a hosting / data-centre / relay network.",
                                       "source": "ATLAS heuristic", "confidence": "LOW"})
    report["sections"]["uncertainty"] = {"warning": UNCERTAINTY}

    report["completed_at"] = timestamp()
    return report
atlas/modules/org_module.py275 lines
"""
Organisation / company investigation module.
Focuses on public, official information only.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import re
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from urllib.parse import quote_plus, urlparse

from ..sources import page_metadata as pm
from ..sources import wikidata
from ..sources.cert_transparency import search_crtsh_org
from ..sources.web_presence import basic_web_info
from ..utils.helpers import confidence_label, is_offline, is_valid_domain, make_progress, timestamp

logger = logging.getLogger("atlas.modules.org")


LEGAL_SUFFIXES = frozenset({"inc", "ltd", "limited", "llc", "llp", "plc", "gmbh", "corp", "corporation", "co",
                            "company", "group", "holdings", "sa", "ag", "bv", "nv", "pty", "lp"})
CONNECTORS = frozenset({"of", "the", "and", "for", "&"})
CANDIDATE_TLDS = (".com", ".co.uk", ".org", ".net")
MAX_CANDIDATES = 8


def name_tokens(name: str) -> Tuple[List[str], List[str]]:
    """(core tokens incl. connectors, significant tokens without connectors) with legal suffixes removed."""
    toks = [t for t in re.findall(r"[a-z0-9]+", name.lower()) if t not in LEGAL_SUFFIXES]
    return toks, [t for t in toks if t not in CONNECTORS]


def candidate_domains(name: str) -> List[str]:
    """Plausible official domains for an organisation name (verified later, never trusted)."""
    core, sig = name_tokens(name)
    if not core:
        return []
    stems = []
    for stem in ("".join(core), "-".join(core), "".join(sig)):
        if stem and stem not in stems:
            stems.append(stem)
    out: List[str] = []
    for stem in stems[:2]:
        for tld in CANDIDATE_TLDS:
            d = stem + tld
            if is_valid_domain(d) and d not in out:
                out.append(d)
    return out[:MAX_CANDIDATES]


GENERIC_WORDS = frozenset({"welcome", "to", "home", "homepage", "official", "site", "website", "page", "online",
                           "web", "the", "and", "a", "an"})


def _segment_tokens(text: str) -> List[str]:
    return [t for t in re.findall(r"[a-z0-9]+", text.lower())
            if t not in LEGAL_SUFFIXES and t not in CONNECTORS and t not in GENERIC_WORDS]


def verify_site_name(name: str, web: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    """
    Does the responding site identify itself as this organisation?
    Looks ONLY at the site's own title / site name / main heading, one segment at a time
    (segments are separated by | – — : or ' - '). A single distinctive word must make up the WHOLE
    segment ("Example Corp – Home" matches "Example Corp"; "Example Widgets" does not); names with
    two or more distinctive words may appear as a contiguous phrase.
    """
    core, sig = name_tokens(name)
    all_tokens = re.findall(r"[a-z0-9]+", name.lower())
    if not sig:
        return False, "no distinctive words in the name to verify"
    collapsed_forms = {"".join(sig), "".join(core), "".join(all_tokens)}
    for label, value in (("page title", web.get("title")), ("site name", web.get("site_name")),
                         ("main heading", web.get("h1"))):
        if not value:
            continue
        for seg in re.split(r"\s*[|–—:·•]\s*|\s+-\s+", value):
            toks = _segment_tokens(seg)
            hit = toks == sig or "".join(toks) in collapsed_forms
            if not hit and len(sig) >= 2:
                hit = any(toks[i:i + len(sig)] == sig for i in range(len(toks) - len(sig) + 1))
            if hit:
                return True, f"{label} contains the organisation's name"
    return False, "the site's title/name does not contain the organisation's name"


def investigate_organisation(name: str, progress_callback=None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    progress = make_progress(progress_callback, logger)

    report = {
        "target": name,
        "target_type": "ORGANISATION",
        "started_at": timestamp(),
        "completed_at": None,
        "sections": {},
        "confidence_summary": {},
        "sources_used": [],
        "limitations": [],
        "legal_notes": [],
        "search_hints": [], "key_findings": [],
    }

    progress("Performing passive public discovery for organisation...")

    # 1. Try to find an official website via simple heuristics + DuckDuckGo HTML (lightweight)
    # We avoid heavy scraping. Provide clear search URLs for the analyst.
    encoded = quote_plus(name)
    report["search_hints"] = [
        {
            "description": "General web search",
            "url": f"https://duckduckgo.com/?q={encoded}",
        },
        {
            "description": "Official site heuristic search",
            "url": f"https://duckduckgo.com/?q={encoded}+official+website",
        },
        {
            "description": "UK Companies House (if UK entity)",
            "url": f"https://find-and-update.company-information.service.gov.uk/search?q={encoded}",
        },
        {
            "description": "OpenCorporates (multi-jurisdiction)",
            "url": f"https://opencorporates.com/companies?q={encoded}",
        },
        {
            "description": "News coverage",
            "url": f"https://news.google.com/search?q={encoded}",
        },
    ]

    # 1b. Wikidata: community-recorded official website (a far better lead than guessing)
    wd_site: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    if not is_offline():
        progress("Looking the organisation up in Wikidata (official website, disambiguation)...")
        wd = wikidata.lookup(name, "org")
        report["sections"]["wikidata"] = {k: wd.get(k) for k in ("found", "ambiguous", "errors", "source", "queried_at")}
        report["sections"]["wikidata"]["candidates"] = [
            {k: c[k] for k in ("qid", "label", "description", "official_website", "url")} for c in wd["candidates"]]
        if wd.get("ambiguous"):
            report["limitations"].append(
                "Several organisations share this name in Wikidata; ATLAS did not pick one. See the candidates in "
                "sections.wikidata and confirm the right one with --source <official URL>.")
        elif wd.get("selected") and wd["selected"].get("official_website"):
            sel = wd["selected"]
            wd_site = {"domain": (urlparse(sel["official_website"]).hostname or "").removeprefix("www."),
                       "url": sel["official_website"], "qid": sel["qid"], "description": sel["description"]}
            report["sources_used"].append(sel["url"])

    # 2. Test plausible official domains and VERIFY each against the site's own name
    progress("Testing plausible official domains and verifying them against the organisation's name...")
    cleaned = [] if wd_site else candidate_domains(name)
    if wd_site:
        cleaned = [wd_site["domain"]]

    def probe(domain: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        target = wd_site["url"] if (wd_site and domain == wd_site["domain"]) else domain   # exact recorded URL
        return {"domain": domain, "web": basic_web_info(target)}

    found_sites: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    seen_hosts = set()
    if cleaned:
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4, thread_name_prefix="atlas-org") as pool:
            probes = list(pool.map(probe, cleaned))
        for pr in probes:
            web = pr["web"]
            if not (web.get("success") and web.get("status_code") and web["status_code"] < 400):
                continue
            final = web.get("final_url") or pr["domain"]
            host = (urlparse(final).hostname or pr["domain"]).lower()
            host = host[4:] if host.startswith("www.") else host
            if host in seen_hosts:                       # several candidates redirecting to one site
                continue
            seen_hosts.add(host)
            matched, basis = verify_site_name(name, web)
            from_wd = bool(wd_site and pr["domain"] == wd_site["domain"])
            found_sites.append({
                "origin": "Wikidata official website (P856)" if from_wd else "guessed from the name",
                "domain": pr["domain"], "title": web.get("title"), "site_name": web.get("site_name"),
                "final_url": final, "server": web.get("server"), "name_match": matched, "match_basis": basis,
            })
            report["sources_used"].append(final)
        found_sites.sort(key=lambda x: not x["name_match"])      # verified-by-name first

    matched_sites = [x for x in found_sites if x["name_match"]]
    report["sections"]["possible_official_sites"] = {
        "candidates_tested": cleaned,
        "responsive_sites": found_sites,
        "name_verified_sites": [x["domain"] for x in matched_sites],
        "note": ("Candidates are guesses from the name. 'name_match' means the site's own title/name contains the "
                 "organisation's name; that makes it a stronger lead but is NOT confirmation that it is the official site."),
    }

    wd_verified = [x for x in matched_sites if str(x.get("origin", "")).startswith("Wikidata")]
    if wd_verified:
        report["confidence_summary"]["web_presence"] = {
            "level": confidence_label(0.7), "score": 0.7,
            "note": "Wikidata records this as the organisation's official website AND the site's own name matches"}
    elif matched_sites:
        report["confidence_summary"]["web_presence"] = {
            "level": confidence_label(0.4), "score": 0.4,
            "note": "A guessed site's own title/name matches the organisation – still unconfirmed; verify manually"}
    elif found_sites:
        report["confidence_summary"]["web_presence"] = {
            "level": confidence_label(0.2), "score": 0.2,
            "note": "Candidate domains responded but none identifies itself as this organisation – likely unrelated sites"}
    else:
        report["confidence_summary"]["web_presence"] = {
            "level": confidence_label(0.15), "score": 0.15,
            "note": "No candidate domains responded; use the search hints below"}

    # 2b. Domains seen on certificates issued to this organisation name (leads only)
    if not is_offline():
        progress("Searching certificate-transparency logs for domains certified to this organisation name...")
        org_ct = search_crtsh_org(name)
        report["sections"]["certificate_organisation_domains"] = org_ct
        if org_ct.get("success") and org_ct.get("domains"):
            report["sources_used"].append("crt.sh (O= search)")
            report["key_findings"].append({
                "finding": "Domains on certificates issued to this organisation name (unverified leads): " +
                           ", ".join(d["domain"] for d in org_ct["domains"][:8]),
                "source": "crt.sh", "confidence": "LOW"})

    # 3. Placeholder for registry data – we point the user to official sources
    report["sections"]["business_registries"] = {
        "note": (
            "ATLAS does not scrape government registries automatically in order to "
            "respect terms of service and rate limits. Use the official search links provided."
        ),
        "recommended": [
            "UK: Companies House – https://find-and-update.company-information.service.gov.uk/",
            "US: SEC EDGAR, state business search portals",
            "Multi-jurisdiction: OpenCorporates, GLEIF (for LEIs)",
        ],
    }

    report["sections"]["social_and_news"] = {
        "note": "Search the provided news and general web links. Official social accounts are best confirmed from the organisation's own website.",
        "caution": "Do not treat unofficial social profiles as verified without cross-checking.",
    }

    report["limitations"].extend([
        "Organisation investigations are inherently less automated than IP/domain lookups.",
        "Many official registries require interactive use or have strict ToS against bulk scraping.",
        "Always verify any candidate website or registration data manually.",
    ])

    report["legal_notes"] = [
        "Collecting publicly available information about companies is generally permitted.",
        "Do not attempt to access non-public internal systems or employee personal data.",
        "If the organisation is a sole trader or the data includes living individuals, UK GDPR / DPA 2018 may apply.",
        "Respect robots.txt and website terms when performing any further manual research.",
    ]

    for site in found_sites:
        if site["name_match"] and str(site.get("origin", "")).startswith("Wikidata"):
            text = (f"Official website (per Wikidata, name verified on the site): {site['domain']} – {site['match_basis']}")
            conf = "MEDIUM"
        elif site["name_match"]:
            text = (f"Likely official website: {site['domain']} – {site['match_basis']} "
                    "(guessed from the name; NOT confirmed as official)")
            conf = "LOW"
        else:
            text = (f"Domain {site['domain']} responded but {site['match_basis']}; probably unrelated "
                    "(excluded from image and location lookups)")
            conf = "LOW"
        report["key_findings"].append({"finding": text, "source": site.get("final_url") or site["domain"],
                                       "confidence": conf})
    if not found_sites:
        report["key_findings"].append({"finding": "No official website could be identified automatically.",
                                       "source": "ATLAS heuristic", "confidence": "LOW"})
    report["completed_at"] = timestamp()
    return report
atlas/modules/person_module.py291 lines
"""
Public figure / person investigation module.

Strictly limited to publicly published PROFESSIONAL information. It does not build
dossiers on private individuals, never looks for addresses/phones/private accounts,
never reads a Person's address/home/birthplace fields or social-profile (sameAs) links,
and refuses to merge information when the identity is ambiguous.

Sources: (1) Wikipedia (public MediaWiki endpoint, for notable people) and
(2) official/public pages the *user supplies* with --source. ATLAS has no search-engine
access, so published interviews cannot be discovered automatically; search links are given.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
from urllib.parse import quote_plus, urlparse

from ..sources import page_metadata as pm
from ..sources import wikidata, wikimedia
from ..sources.web_pages import fetch_page
from ..utils.helpers import confidence_label, is_offline, make_progress, timestamp
from ..utils.source_reliability import rate_domain

logger = logging.getLogger("atlas.modules.person")

MAX_SOURCES = 5

STRICT_BOUNDARY = (
    "ATLAS only assists with publicly available information relevant to a person's professional "
    "or public identity. It will not search for or report private addresses, personal phone numbers, "
    "private email accounts, passwords, family details, or non-public social media, and it does not "
    "build dossiers on private individuals."
)


def _names(value: Any, limit: int = 6) -> List[str]:
    items = value if isinstance(value, list) else [value]
    out = []
    for it in items[:limit]:
        t = pm.text_of(it, 150)
        if t and t not in out:
            out.append(t)
    return out


def _analyse_source(url: str, name: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Fetch one user-supplied page and extract professional facts about *name*."""
    info = fetch_page(url)
    host = (urlparse(info.get("final_url") or url).hostname or "")
    rating, why = rate_domain(host)
    rec: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "url": info.get("final_url") or url, "host": host, "reliability": rating,
        "reliability_note": why, "accessed_at": info.get("accessed_at"),
        "fetched": bool(info.get("ok")), "name_match": "none", "title": info.get("page_title"),
        "errors": info.get("errors", []),
    }
    if not info.get("ok"):
        return rec

    persons = [n for n in info.get("jsonld", []) if "person" in {t.lower() for t in pm.node_types(n)}]
    exact = [n for n in persons if pm.tokens(pm.text_of(n.get("name"))) == pm.tokens(name)]
    other = [n for n in persons if n not in exact]
    doc = info.get("doc", {})
    in_text = pm.name_in_text(name, " ".join([doc.get("title", ""), *doc.get("h1", [])]))

    if exact:
        node = exact[0]
        rec["name_match"] = "structured_exact"
        rec["job_title"] = pm.text_of(node.get("jobTitle"), 150) or None
        rec["employer"] = _names(node.get("worksFor"))
        rec["affiliations"] = _names(node.get("affiliation"))
        rec["education"] = _names(node.get("alumniOf"))
        rec["awards"] = _names(node.get("award"))
        rec["description"] = pm.text_of(node.get("description"), 400) or None
    elif in_text:
        rec["name_match"] = "page_text"
        rec["description"] = pm.meta_first(doc.get("meta", {}), "og:description", "twitter:description")[:400] or None
    elif other:
        rec["name_match"] = "different_person"
        rec["note"] = "The page's structured data describes a different person; it was ignored."
    else:
        rec["note"] = "The page does not demonstrably mention this person; it was ignored."
    return rec


def investigate_person(name: str, progress_callback=None, sources: Optional[List[str]] = None,
                       use_wikipedia: bool = True) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    progress = make_progress(progress_callback, logger)
    sources = list(sources or [])[:MAX_SOURCES]

    report: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "target": name, "target_type": "PERSON",
        "started_at": timestamp(), "completed_at": None,
        "sections": {}, "confidence_summary": {}, "sources_used": [],
        "limitations": [], "legal_notes": [], "search_hints": [], "key_findings": [],
        "strict_boundary": STRICT_BOUNDARY,
    }
    professional: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "identity": {"status": "not_determined", "explanation": ""},
        "biography": None, "wikipedia": None, "sources": [], "employment": [],
        "affiliations": [], "education": [], "achievements": [],
        "published_interviews": {
            "automated": False,
            "note": "ATLAS has no search-engine access, so published interviews are not discovered "
                    "automatically. Use the search links below and verify each result manually."},
    }
    report["professional"] = professional

    encoded = quote_plus(name)
    report["search_hints"] = [
        {"description": "Professional / public web presence", "url": f"https://duckduckgo.com/?q={encoded}"},
        {"description": "News coverage and published interviews", "url": f"https://news.google.com/search?q={encoded}+interview"},
        {"description": "Academic / publication search (if applicable)", "url": f"https://scholar.google.com/scholar?q={encoded}"},
        {"description": "Official biography heuristic", "url": f"https://duckduckgo.com/?q={encoded}+official+biography"},
    ]
    report["sections"]["scope"] = {
        "permitted": ["Public professional profiles and biographies", "Published interviews and articles",
                      "Public professional achievements and roles",
                      "Official organisational pages that mention the person",
                      "Officially published professional (not residential) locations"],
        "not_permitted_by_atlas": ["Private residential addresses", "Personal telephone numbers",
                                   "Private email addresses or credentials", "Non-public or personal social media",
                                   "Family information", "Live location tracking", "Inferring location from photos",
                                   "Any attempt to bypass access controls"],
    }

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- Wikipedia
    ambiguous = False
    if use_wikipedia and not is_offline():
        progress("Checking Wikipedia (public MediaWiki endpoint) for a matching article...")
        wiki = wikimedia.lookup_person(name, with_licence=False)
        professional["wikipedia"] = wiki
        if wiki.get("ambiguous"):
            ambiguous = True
        elif wiki.get("found") and wiki.get("name_match"):
            report["sources_used"].append(wiki.get("page_url") or "Wikipedia")
            professional["biography"] = {
                "text": wiki.get("extract"), "description": wiki.get("description"),
                "source": wiki.get("page_url"), "date_accessed": wiki.get("queried_at"),
                "reliability": "MEDIUM", "licence": "Text from Wikipedia, CC BY-SA 4.0 – attribute and share alike.",
            }
        elif wiki.get("found"):
            report["limitations"].append(
                f"Wikipedia redirected to a differently named article ('{wiki.get('title')}'); it was not used.")
    elif is_offline():
        report["limitations"].append("Offline mode: Wikipedia and source pages were not fetched.")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- Wikidata (disambiguation + official site)
    wd_candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    wd_selected: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    if use_wikipedia and not is_offline():
        progress("Checking Wikidata for people with this name (disambiguation, official website)...")
        wd = wikidata.lookup(name, "person")
        wd_candidates = [c for c in wd["candidates"] if c["exact_label"] and c["is_human"]]
        professional["wikidata"] = {
            "found": wd["found"], "ambiguous": wd["ambiguous"], "errors": wd["errors"], "source": wd["source"],
            "queried_at": wd["queried_at"],
            "candidates": [{k: c[k] for k in ("qid", "label", "description", "occupations", "employers", "url")}
                           for c in wd_candidates]}
        if wd["ambiguous"]:
            ambiguous = True
        elif wd.get("selected"):
            wd_selected = wd["selected"]
            report["sources_used"].append(wd_selected["url"])
            professional["wikidata_profile"] = {
                "qid": wd_selected["qid"], "description": wd_selected["description"],
                "occupations": wd_selected["occupations"], "employers": wd_selected["employers"],
                "official_website": wd_selected["official_website"], "url": wd_selected["url"],
                "reliability": "MEDIUM", "note": "Community-edited; one matching person found for this exact name."}
            site = wd_selected.get("official_website")
            if site and site not in sources and len(sources) < MAX_SOURCES:
                sources.append(site)                       # fetched below like a user-supplied page

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- user-supplied sources
    for url in sources:
        progress(f"Reading official/public source: {url}")
        rec = _analyse_source(url, name)
        professional["sources"].append(rec)
        if rec["fetched"]:
            report["sources_used"].append(rec["url"])

    matched = [r for r in professional["sources"] if r["name_match"] in ("structured_exact", "page_text")]

    # employment / affiliations / education / achievements with provenance
    def collect(field: str, dest: str, label: str) -> None:
        for r in matched:
            vals = r.get(field) or []
            vals = [vals] if isinstance(vals, str) else vals
            for v in vals:
                professional[dest].append({label: v, "source": r["url"], "source_reliability": r["reliability"],
                                           "date_accessed": r["accessed_at"]})

    for r in matched:
        if r.get("job_title") or r.get("employer"):
            professional["employment"].append({
                "job_title": r.get("job_title"), "employer": r.get("employer"), "source": r["url"],
                "source_reliability": r["reliability"], "date_accessed": r["accessed_at"]})
    wdp = professional.get("wikidata_profile")
    if wdp and (wdp["occupations"] or wdp["employers"]):
        professional["employment"].append({
            "job_title": ", ".join(wdp["occupations"]) or None, "employer": wdp["employers"],
            "source": wdp["url"], "source_reliability": "MEDIUM", "date_accessed": professional["wikidata"]["queried_at"]})
    collect("affiliations", "affiliations", "organisation")
    collect("education", "education", "institution")
    collect("awards", "achievements", "achievement")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- identity assessment
    employers = {e.lower() for r in matched if r["name_match"] == "structured_exact" for e in (r.get("employer") or [])}
    if ambiguous:
        listing = "; ".join(f"{c['label']} – {c['description'] or 'no description'} ({c['url']})" for c in wd_candidates[:5])
        professional["identity"] = {
            "status": "ambiguous",
            "explanation": "Several people share this name (Wikipedia/Wikidata list more than one). ATLAS cannot "
                           "confidently match one person and has not combined information about different people. "
                           + (f"Candidates: {listing}. " if listing else "")
                           + "Supply an official page for the right person with --source <URL> to continue."}
        report["key_findings"].append({"finding": "Name is ambiguous – candidates: " + (listing or "see Wikipedia disambiguation page"),
                                       "source": "Wikidata / Wikipedia", "confidence": "MEDIUM"})
    elif len(employers) > 1:
        professional["identity"] = {
            "status": "possible_multiple_people",
            "explanation": "Sources name different employers for this name; they may be different people. "
                           "Findings are listed per source and have NOT been merged."}
    elif matched or professional["biography"] or professional.get("wikidata_profile"):
        n_ind = len({r["host"] for r in matched}) + (1 if (professional["biography"] or professional.get("wikidata_profile")) else 0)
        professional["identity"] = {
            "status": "name_match_only",
            "explanation": (f"{n_ind} independent source(s) match the name. ATLAS cannot verify that they all refer to "
                            "the same individual, so identity confidence is capped below HIGH.")}
    else:
        professional["identity"] = {
            "status": "no_information_located",
            "explanation": "No public professional information could be matched automatically. ATLAS does not build "
                           "dossiers on private individuals; supply an official page with --source <URL> if the "
                           "person is a public figure."}

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- confidence
    score = 0.10
    st = professional["identity"]["status"]
    if st == "name_match_only":
        score = 0.35
        if professional["biography"] or professional.get("wikidata_profile"):
            score += 0.15
        official = [r for r in matched if r["reliability"] == "HIGH" and r["name_match"] == "structured_exact"]
        if official:
            score += 0.20
        if len({r["host"] for r in matched}) >= 2:
            score += 0.10
        score = min(score, 0.80)                       # never HIGH automatically
    elif st in ("ambiguous", "possible_multiple_people"):
        score = 0.15
    report["confidence_summary"]["overall"] = {
        "level": confidence_label(score), "score": round(score, 2),
        "note": professional["identity"]["explanation"]}

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- key findings
    kf = report["key_findings"]
    if wdp:
        kf.append({"finding": f"Wikidata: {wdp['description'] or 'matching person found'}"
                              + (f"; official website recorded: {wdp['official_website']}" if wdp["official_website"] else ""),
                   "source": wdp["url"], "confidence": "MEDIUM"})
    kf.append({"finding": f"Identity assessment: {st.replace('_', ' ')}.", "source": "ATLAS analysis",
               "confidence": confidence_label(score)})
    if professional["biography"] and st != "ambiguous":
        kf.append({"finding": f"Biography summary available: {professional['biography'].get('description') or 'see report'}.",
                   "source": professional["biography"]["source"], "confidence": "MEDIUM"})
    for e in professional["employment"]:
        text = " at ".join(x for x in (e.get("job_title"), ", ".join(e.get("employer") or [])) if x)
        if text:
            kf.append({"finding": f"Published role: {text}.", "source": e["source"],
                       "confidence": "MEDIUM" if e["source_reliability"] != "HIGH" else "HIGH"})
    report["limitations"] += [
        "Automated person research is deliberately limited; results are leads to verify, not a dossier.",
        "Identity matching is by name only unless an official page links the facts together.",
        "Published interviews cannot be discovered automatically (no search-engine access).",
        "Public figures still have privacy rights; purpose and proportionality matter.",
    ]
    report["legal_notes"] = [
        "Under UK GDPR and the Data Protection Act 2018, information relating to an identifiable living individual is personal data.",
        "Even public information may be personal data and need a lawful basis, a stated purpose and proportionate handling.",
        "Special category data (health, politics, religion, etc.) has additional protections and is not sought by ATLAS.",
        "Using publicly available information does not by itself make an investigation lawful; legality depends on purpose, "
        "the information, how it is processed and the circumstances.",
        "The Computer Misuse Act 1990 prohibits unauthorised access to computer material; ATLAS only requests public pages.",
        "This is not formal legal advice.",
    ]
    report["completed_at"] = timestamp()
    return report
atlas/modules/username_module.py226 lines
"""
Username presence module ("which public platforms have an account with this exact username?").

Boundaries (also stated in every report): existence of a public profile page only; no profile
content is read; no logins; no username variants; robots.txt honoured; a shared username does NOT
show that two accounts belong to the same person.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from ..sources.username_sources import (
    MANUAL_LINKS, PLATFORMS, Platform, _fill, check_platform, load_platforms, selftest_platform, validate_username,
)
from ..utils.helpers import is_offline, make_progress, timestamp

logger = logging.getLogger("atlas.modules.username")

STRICT_BOUNDARY = (
    "ATLAS only checks whether a public profile page exists for the EXACT username on a curated list of "
    "mainstream platforms that permit automated access. It does not read profile content, log in, bypass "
    "access controls, try spelling variants, or link accounts to a real-world person."
)

IDENTITY_WARNING = (
    "A username that exists on several platforms does NOT show that the accounts belong to the same person: "
    "usernames are frequently reused by unrelated people. Treat every result as a lead to verify, never as "
    "identification. Wrongly attributing accounts to a person can cause real harm."
)

LEGAL_NOTES = [
    "A username can be personal data under UK GDPR / DPA 2018 when it relates to an identifiable person; you need a lawful basis and a specific purpose.",
    "Suitable purposes include checking your own handle or brand for impersonation, authorised security assessments and "
    "proportionate public-interest research. Do not use this to locate, monitor or harass an individual.",
    "Platform terms of service may restrict automated checks; ATLAS honours robots.txt and queries one public URL per platform, but you remain responsible for compliance.",
    "Using public information does not by itself make an investigation lawful; legality depends on purpose, the information, how it is processed and the circumstances.",
    "This is not formal legal advice.",
]

_ORDER = {"found": 0, "unreliable": 1, "inconclusive": 2, "error": 3, "skipped_robots": 4,
          "skipped_format": 5, "not_found": 6, "offline": 7}


def investigate_username(
    username: str,
    progress_callback=None,
    stop_at_first: bool = False,
    platforms: Optional[List[Platform]] = None,
    max_workers: int = 8,
    platforms_path: Optional[str] = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    progress = make_progress(progress_callback, logger)
    load_warnings: List[str] = []
    list_source = "built-in list"
    if platforms is None:
        if platforms_path:
            platforms, load_warnings = load_platforms(platforms_path)
            list_source = f"custom list ({platforms_path})"
        else:
            platforms = PLATFORMS
    platforms = list(platforms)
    username = (username or "").strip().lstrip("@")

    report: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "target": username, "target_type": "USERNAME",
        "started_at": timestamp(), "completed_at": None,
        "sections": {}, "confidence_summary": {}, "sources_used": [],
        "limitations": [], "legal_notes": list(LEGAL_NOTES), "key_findings": [],
        "strict_boundary": STRICT_BOUNDARY, "search_hints": [],
    }
    accounts: Dict[str, Any] = {"found": [], "unreliable": [], "inconclusive": [], "skipped": [],
                                "not_found_count": 0, "checked": 0, "stop_at_first": stop_at_first,
                                "platform_list": list_source}
    report["limitations"] += load_warnings
    report["accounts"] = accounts

    if not platforms:
        report["error"] = "No usable platforms to check" + (f" ({'; '.join(load_warnings)})" if load_warnings else "")
        report["completed_at"] = timestamp()
        return report
    ok, why = validate_username(username)
    if not ok:
        report["error"] = f"Invalid username: {why}"
        report["completed_at"] = timestamp()
        return report

    # manual links: printed for the user, never fetched
    report["search_hints"] = [
        {"description": f"{name} (blocks automated access – open manually if you have a legitimate need)",
         "url": _fill(tpl, username)} for name, tpl in MANUAL_LINKS]

    if is_offline():
        report["limitations"].append("Offline mode: no platforms were queried.")
        accounts["status"] = "offline"
        report["completed_at"] = timestamp()
        return report

    progress(f"Checking {len(platforms)} public platforms for the exact username '{username}' "
             f"(robots.txt honoured, one request each)...")
    results: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    stopped_early = False
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=max_workers, thread_name_prefix="atlas-user") as pool:
        futures = {pool.submit(check_platform, p, username): p for p in platforms}
        for fut in as_completed(futures):
            p = futures[fut]
            try:
                r = fut.result()
            except Exception as e:                          # one platform failing must not sink the scan
                logger.exception("%s check crashed", p.name)
                r = {"platform": p.name, "category": p.category, "status": "error",
                     "detail": f"check crashed: {e}", "profile_url": None, "http_status": None,
                     "checked_at": timestamp(), "control_check": None}
            results.append(r)
            if r["status"] == "found":
                progress(f"match: {r['platform']} → {r['profile_url']}")
                if stop_at_first:
                    stopped_early = True
                    for other in futures:
                        other.cancel()                      # pending checks never start
                    break

    order = {p.name: i for i, p in enumerate(platforms)}
    results.sort(key=lambda r: (_ORDER.get(r["status"], 9), order.get(r["platform"], 99)))
    accounts["checked"] = len(results)
    for r in results:
        st = r["status"]
        if st == "found":
            accounts["found"].append({
                "platform": r["platform"], "category": r["category"], "profile_url": r["profile_url"],
                "evidence": r["detail"], "control_check": r["control_check"], "checked_at": r["checked_at"],
                "existence_confidence": r.get("existence_confidence") or "LOW",
                "username_in_page": r.get("username_in_page"),
                "identity_confidence": "UNVERIFIED",
            })
        elif st == "unreliable":
            accounts["unreliable"].append(r)
        elif st in ("inconclusive", "error"):
            accounts["inconclusive"].append(r)
        elif st in ("skipped_robots", "skipped_format", "offline"):
            accounts["skipped"].append(r)
        else:
            accounts["not_found_count"] += 1

    n_found = len(accounts["found"])
    report["sections"]["scan_summary"] = {
        "platforms_in_list": len(platforms), "checked": accounts["checked"], "matches": n_found,
        "not_found": accounts["not_found_count"], "unreliable_platforms": len(accounts["unreliable"]),
        "inconclusive_or_error": len(accounts["inconclusive"]), "skipped": len(accounts["skipped"]),
        "stopped_at_first_match": stopped_early,
    }
    for a in accounts["found"]:
        report["sources_used"].append(a["profile_url"])
        report["key_findings"].append({
            "finding": f"A public profile exists for '{username}' on {a['platform']} ({a['category']}): {a['profile_url']}",
            "source": a["profile_url"], "confidence": a["existence_confidence"] + " (existence only)"})
    if not n_found:
        report["key_findings"].append({
            "finding": f"No match for '{username}' on the {accounts['checked']} platform(s) ATLAS could check.",
            "source": "HTTP responses from the platforms listed", "confidence": "LOW"})
    report["key_findings"].append({"finding": IDENTITY_WARNING, "source": "ATLAS", "confidence": "n/a"})

    best = "HIGH" if any(a["existence_confidence"] == "HIGH" for a in accounts["found"]) else "MEDIUM" if any(
        a["existence_confidence"] == "MEDIUM" for a in accounts["found"]) else "LOW"
    report["confidence_summary"]["account_existence"] = {
        "level": best, "score": {"HIGH": 0.85, "MEDIUM": 0.6, "LOW": 0.3}[best],
        "note": "Based on HTTP responses, soft-404 detection and two control checks per match; sites change and may block automated requests."}
    report["confidence_summary"]["identity_linkage"] = {
        "level": "UNVERIFIED", "score": 0.0, "note": "Same username ≠ same person. ATLAS makes no identity claim."}

    if stopped_early:
        report["limitations"].append("Scan stopped at the first match (--stop-at-first); remaining platforms were not checked.")
    skipped_robots = [r["platform"] for r in accounts["skipped"] if r["status"] == "skipped_robots"]
    if skipped_robots:
        report["limitations"].append("Not queried because robots.txt disallows or could not be verified: " + ", ".join(skipped_robots) + ".")
    if accounts["unreliable"]:
        report["limitations"].append("Unreliable detection (control check failed): " +
                                     ", ".join(r["platform"] for r in accounts["unreliable"]) + ".")
    if accounts["inconclusive"]:
        report["limitations"].append("Inconclusive (rate-limited, blocked, error or redirected): " +
                                     ", ".join(r["platform"] for r in accounts["inconclusive"]) + ".")
    report["limitations"] += [
        "Only exact-username matches on a curated list of platforms are checked; accounts under other names are not found.",
        "Major social networks that block automated access are not queried (manual links are provided).",
        "Detection rules can go stale when a platform changes; a missing result is not proof an account does not exist.",
        IDENTITY_WARNING,
    ]
    report["completed_at"] = timestamp()
    return report


def run_selftest(progress_callback=None, platforms_path: Optional[str] = None, max_workers: int = 8) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Health-check every platform rule (negative control + known-good account). Makes ~1-2 requests per platform."""
    progress = make_progress(progress_callback, logger)
    out: Dict[str, Any] = {"checked_at": timestamp(), "results": [], "summary": {}, "recommendations": [], "warnings": []}
    if is_offline():
        out["status"] = "offline"
        return out
    if platforms_path:
        platforms, out["warnings"] = load_platforms(platforms_path)
    else:
        platforms = PLATFORMS
    progress(f"Health-checking {len(platforms)} platform rules (random-name control + known-good account)...")
    results: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=max_workers, thread_name_prefix="atlas-selftest") as pool:
        futs = {pool.submit(selftest_platform, p): p for p in platforms}
        for fut in as_completed(futs):
            p = futs[fut]
            try:
                results.append(fut.result())
            except Exception as e:
                results.append({"platform": p.name, "category": p.category, "verdict": "error", "note": str(e),
                                "negative": None, "positive": None})
    order = {p.name: i for i, p in enumerate(platforms)}
    results.sort(key=lambda r: order.get(r["platform"], 999))
    out["results"] = results
    for r in results:
        out["summary"][r["verdict"]] = out["summary"].get(r["verdict"], 0) + 1
    for r in results:
        if r["verdict"] in ("unreliable", "stale_rule"):
            out["recommendations"].append(f"{r['platform']}: {r['note']}")
    out["status"] = "done"
    return out
atlas/report.py331 lines
"""
Report generation and export for ATLAS.

One document model (12 consistent sections) is rendered to plain text and to a
script-free, escaped HTML page. JSON is the full structured record. Exports are
written with owner-only permissions; precise-location keys are scrubbed.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import json
import os
import re
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from . import __version__
from .utils import html_safe as hg
from .utils.source_reliability import rate_source

SECTION_TITLES = [
    "Investigation overview", "Target classification", "Main findings", "Network information",
    "Professional information", "Sources consulted", "Source reliability",
    "Confidence assessments", "Limitations", "Privacy and legal considerations",
]

_PRECISE_KEYS = {"latitude", "longitude", "lat", "lon", "lng", "postal", "postcode", "zip", "zip_code"}
HANDLING_NOTE = (
    "This report may contain personal data. It is stored locally with owner-only permissions. "
    "Delete it when no longer needed (`reports delete <name>`, `reports purge <days>`), keep it only for the "
    "stated purpose, and do not share it without a lawful basis."
)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Preparation / export
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _scrub_precise(node: Any) -> Any:
    if isinstance(node, dict):
        return {k: _scrub_precise(v) for k, v in node.items() if k.lower() not in _PRECISE_KEYS}
    if isinstance(node, list):
        return [_scrub_precise(v) for v in node]
    return node


def _strip_raw(report: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    for section in (report.get("sections") or {}).values():
        if isinstance(section, dict) and isinstance(section.get("data"), dict) and section.get("raw_summary"):
            section["data"] = {"_omitted": "raw payload omitted; re-run with --raw to include (contacts are always redacted)"}
    return report


def prepare_for_export(report: Dict[str, Any], include_raw: bool = False) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    out = _scrub_precise(copy.deepcopy(report))
    out.pop("export", None)
    return out if include_raw else _strip_raw(out)


def _safe_basename(target: str) -> str:
    cleaned = re.sub(r"[^\w.\-]+", "_", target.strip()).strip("._")[:80] or "unknown"
    ts = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
    return f"atlas_{cleaned}_{ts}"


def export_reports(report: Dict[str, Any], output_dir: str = "reports", include_raw: bool = False,
                   html: bool = False) -> Dict[str, str]:
    """Write JSON + TXT (and optionally HTML) sharing one base name. Returns {kind: path}."""
    os.makedirs(output_dir, mode=0o700, exist_ok=True)
    base = _safe_basename(str(report.get("target", "unknown")))
    data = prepare_for_export(report, include_raw)
    paths = {"json": os.path.join(output_dir, f"{base}.json"), "txt": os.path.join(output_dir, f"{base}.txt")}
    hg.write_private(paths["json"], json.dumps(data, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, default=str))
    hg.write_private(paths["txt"], render_text_report(data))
    if html:
        paths["html"] = os.path.join(output_dir, f"{base}.html")
        hg.write_private(paths["html"], render_html_report(data))
    return paths


def export_json(report: Dict[str, Any], output_dir: str = "reports") -> str:
    return export_reports(report, output_dir)["json"]


def export_txt(report: Dict[str, Any], output_dir: str = "reports") -> str:
    return export_reports(report, output_dir)["txt"]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Document model
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _source_reliability(report: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Tuple[str, str, str]]:
    rows, seen = [], set()
    srcs = list(report.get("sources_used") or [])
    for s in (report.get("professional") or {}).get("sources", []):
        srcs.append(s.get("url") or "")
    for s in srcs:
        s = str(s)
        if s and s not in seen:
            seen.add(s)
            rating, why = rate_source(s)
            rows.append((s, rating, why))
    return rows


def _conf_line(label: str, v: Any) -> str:
    if isinstance(v, dict):
        note = f" — {v['note']}" if v.get("note") else ""
        return f"{label}: {v.get('level')} (score {v.get('score')}){note}"
    return f"{label}: {v}"


def build_document(report: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Tuple[str, List[tuple]]]:
    report = _scrub_precise(report)          # defence in depth: never render coordinates/postcodes
    cls = report.get("classification") or {}
    sections: Dict[str, List[tuple]] = {t: [] for t in SECTION_TITLES}

    # 1 overview
    sections[SECTION_TITLES[0]].append(("kv", [
        ("Target", report.get("target")), ("Type", report.get("target_type")),
        ("Started", report.get("started_at")), ("Completed", report.get("completed_at")),
        ("ATLAS version", __version__),
        ("Mode", "OFFLINE (no network lookups)" if report.get("offline") else "online"),
        ("Scope", f"partial ({report['partial']} only)" if report.get("partial") else "full investigation"),
    ]))
    if report.get("error"):
        sections[SECTION_TITLES[0]].append(("warn", f"Error: {report['error']}"))
        if report.get("suggestion"):
            sections[SECTION_TITLES[0]].append(("p", report["suggestion"]))

    # 2 classification
    if cls:
        sections[SECTION_TITLES[1]].append(("kv", [
            ("Original input", cls.get("original_input")), ("Normalised", cls.get("normalised")),
            ("Type", cls.get("type")), ("Classifier confidence", cls.get("confidence")),
            ("Notes", cls.get("notes"))]))
    else:
        sections[SECTION_TITLES[1]].append(("p", "No classification recorded."))

    # 3 main findings
    kf = report.get("key_findings") or []
    sections[SECTION_TITLES[2]].append(("ul", [
        f"{f.get('finding')}  [confidence: {f.get('confidence', 'n/a')}; source: {f.get('source', 'n/a')}]" for f in kf
    ] or ["No findings were recorded."]))

    acc = report.get("accounts")
    if acc is not None:
        summ = (report.get("sections") or {}).get("scan_summary") or {}
        lines = []
        for a in acc.get("found", []):
            lines.append(f"MATCH – {a['platform']} ({a['category']}): {a['profile_url']}  "
                         f"[existence: {a['existence_confidence']}; identity: {a['identity_confidence']}]")
        if acc.get("status") == "offline":
            lines.append("Offline mode – no platforms were queried, so nothing can be said about this username.")
        elif not acc.get("found"):
            lines.append("No matches were found on the platforms ATLAS could check.")
        if summ:
            lines.append("Scan summary: " + ", ".join(f"{k.replace('_', ' ')}: {v}" for k, v in summ.items()))
        sections[SECTION_TITLES[2]].append(("p", "Accounts with the exact username (existence only; not proof of the same person):"))
        sections[SECTION_TITLES[2]].append(("ul", lines))

    # 4 network info
    net = {k: v for k, v in (report.get("sections") or {}).items() if k not in ("scope", "scan_summary")}
    sections[SECTION_TITLES[3]].append(("tree", net) if net else ("p", "Not applicable to this target type."))

    # 5 professional
    prof = report.get("professional")
    if prof:
        ident = prof.get("identity") or {}
        sections[SECTION_TITLES[4]].append(("kv", [("Identity assessment", str(ident.get("status", "")).replace("_", " ")),
                                                   ("Explanation", ident.get("explanation"))]))
        bio = prof.get("biography")
        if bio:
            sections[SECTION_TITLES[4]].append(("kv", [
                ("Biography (summary)", bio.get("text")), ("Description", bio.get("description")),
                ("Source", bio.get("source")), ("Reliability", bio.get("reliability")), ("Licence", bio.get("licence"))]))
        for title, key, label in (("Employment", "employment", None), ("Affiliations", "affiliations", "organisation"),
                                  ("Education", "education", "institution"), ("Achievements", "achievements", "achievement")):
            rows = prof.get(key) or []
            if rows:
                if key == "employment":
                    lines = [f"{', '.join(x for x in (r.get('job_title'), ', '.join(r.get('employer') or [])) if x)} "
                             f"(source: {r['source']}, reliability {r['source_reliability']})" for r in rows]
                else:
                    lines = [f"{r[label]} (source: {r['source']}, reliability {r['source_reliability']})" for r in rows]
                sections[SECTION_TITLES[4]].append(("p", title + ":"))
                sections[SECTION_TITLES[4]].append(("ul", lines))
        for s in prof.get("sources") or []:
            sections[SECTION_TITLES[4]].append(("kv", [("Source page", s.get("url")), ("Reliability", s.get("reliability")),
                                                       ("Name match", str(s.get("name_match")).replace("_", " ")),
                                                       ("Note", s.get("note") or "; ".join(s.get("errors") or []) or None)]))
        pi = prof.get("published_interviews") or {}
        sections[SECTION_TITLES[4]].append(("p", "Published interviews: " + str(pi.get("note", ""))))
    else:
        sections[SECTION_TITLES[4]].append(("p", "Not applicable to this target type."))

    # 6 sources
    srcs = list(dict.fromkeys(str(s) for s in (report.get("sources_used") or [])))
    sections[SECTION_TITLES[5]].append(("ul", srcs or ["No external sources returned data."]))
    hints = report.get("search_hints") or []
    if hints:
        sections[SECTION_TITLES[5]].append(("p", "Recommended public search starting points (manual use):"))
        sections[SECTION_TITLES[5]].append(("ul", [f"{h.get('description')}: {h.get('url')}" for h in hints]))

    # 7 reliability
    rel = report.get("source_reliability")
    rows = [(r["source"], r["rating"], r["rationale"]) for r in rel] if rel else _source_reliability(report)
    sections[SECTION_TITLES[6]].append(("ul", [f"{s} — {r}: {w}" for s, r, w in rows] or ["No sources to rate."]))

    # 8 confidence
    conf = report.get("confidence_summary") or {}
    sections[SECTION_TITLES[7]].append(("ul", [_conf_line(k, v) for k, v in conf.items()] or ["No confidence assessments recorded."]))

    # 9 limitations
    sections[SECTION_TITLES[8]].append(("ul", list(report.get("limitations") or []) or ["None recorded."]))

    # 10 privacy and legal
    legal = list(report.get("legal_notes") or [])
    sections[SECTION_TITLES[9]].append(("ul", legal or ["See SECURITY_AND_LEGAL.md."]))
    if report.get("strict_boundary"):
        sections[SECTION_TITLES[9]].append(("p", "Operational boundary: " + report["strict_boundary"]))
    la = report.get("legal_assessment") or {}
    if la.get("activity_classification"):
        sections[SECTION_TITLES[9]].append(("kv", [(k.replace("_", " "), f"{v['classification']} — {v['notes']}")
                                                    for k, v in la["activity_classification"].items()]))
    sections[SECTION_TITLES[9]].append(("p", HANDLING_NOTE))
    sections[SECTION_TITLES[9]].append(("p", la.get("disclaimer", "Not formal legal advice. Lawfulness depends on purpose, information, processing and circumstances.")))
    return [(t, sections[t]) for t in SECTION_TITLES]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Text renderer
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _render_tree(lines: list, data: Any, indent: int = 0) -> None:
    prefix = " " * indent
    if isinstance(data, dict):
        for k, v in data.items():
            if k in ("data", "raw_summary") and isinstance(v, dict) and len(str(v)) > 400:
                lines.append(f"{prefix}{k}: <structured data – see JSON export for full content>")
                for sk, sv in list(v.items())[:12]:
                    lines.append(f"{prefix}  {sk}: {sv}")
                if len(v) > 12:
                    lines.append(f"{prefix}  ... ({len(v) - 12} more keys)")
            elif isinstance(v, (dict, list)) and len(str(v)) > 300:
                lines.append(f"{prefix}{k}:")
                _render_tree(lines, v, indent + 2)
            else:
                lines.append(f"{prefix}{k}: {v}")
    elif isinstance(data, list):
        for i, item in enumerate(data[:25]):
            if isinstance(item, dict):
                lines.append(f"{prefix}- item {i + 1}:")
                _render_tree(lines, item, indent + 2)
            else:
                lines.append(f"{prefix}- {item}")
        if len(data) > 25:
            lines.append(f"{prefix}... ({len(data) - 25} more items)")
    else:
        lines.append(f"{prefix}{data}")


def render_text_report(report: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    lines = ["=" * 72, "ATLAS INTELLIGENCE REPORT", "=" * 72]
    for n, (title, blocks) in enumerate(build_document(report), 1):
        lines += ["", "-" * 72, f"{n}. {title.upper()}", "-" * 72]
        for kind, payload in blocks:
            if kind == "kv":
                for k, v in payload:
                    if v not in (None, "", []):
                        lines.append(f"  {k:<22}: {v}")
                lines.append("")
            elif kind == "ul":
                lines += [f"  • {x}" for x in payload]
            elif kind in ("p", "warn"):
                if payload:
                    lines.append(f"  {payload}")
            elif kind == "tree":
                _render_tree(lines, payload, 2)
    lines += ["", "=" * 72, "END OF REPORT – Generated by ATLAS (passive OSINT agent)", "=" * 72]
    return "\n".join(lines)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# HTML renderer
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def render_html_report(report: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    out = [f"<h1>ATLAS intelligence report</h1><p class='muted'>Generated {esc_now()} by ATLAS {hg.esc(__version__)}"
           " (passive OSINT). Contains no scripts; all values are escaped.</p>"]
    for n, (title, blocks) in enumerate(build_document(report), 1):
        out.append(f"<h2>{n}. {hg.esc(title)}</h2>")
        for kind, payload in blocks:
            if kind == "kv":
                rows = "".join(f"<tr><th>{hg.esc(k)}</th><td>{_linkify(v)}</td></tr>"
                               for k, v in payload if v not in (None, "", []))
                out.append(f"<table>{rows}</table>")
            elif kind == "ul":
                out.append("<ul>" + "".join(f"<li>{_linkify(x)}</li>" for x in payload) + "</ul>")
            elif kind == "p":
                out.append(f"<p>{_linkify(payload)}</p>")
            elif kind == "warn":
                out.append(f"<div class='notice'>{hg.esc(payload)}</div>")
            elif kind == "tree":
                lines: list = []
                _render_tree(lines, payload, 0)
                out.append(f"<pre>{hg.esc(chr(10).join(lines))}</pre>")
    return hg.page(f"ATLAS report – {report.get('target')}", "".join(out))


_URL_RE = re.compile(r"(https?://[^\s<>\"']+)")


def _linkify(text: Any) -> str:
    """Escape text and turn http(s) URLs into safe links."""
    text = "" if text is None else str(text)
    parts, last = [], 0
    for m in _URL_RE.finditer(text):
        parts.append(hg.esc(text[last:m.start()]))
        url = m.group(1).rstrip(".,;)]")
        parts.append(hg.safe_link(url))
        last = m.start() + len(url)
    parts.append(hg.esc(text[last:]))
    return "".join(parts)


def esc_now() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC")

atlas/sources/cert_transparency.py138 lines
"""
Certificate Transparency log search via crt.sh (public).
Useful for discovering subdomains and certificate history.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from typing import Any, Dict

from ..utils.helpers import safe_request, timestamp

logger = logging.getLogger("atlas.sources.ct")


_MULTI_TLDS = {"co.uk", "org.uk", "ac.uk", "gov.uk", "me.uk", "ltd.uk", "plc.uk", "com.au", "net.au", "org.au",
               "co.nz", "co.jp", "co.in", "com.br", "com.cn", "co.za", "com.mx", "com.tr", "com.sg", "com.hk"}
MULTI_TENANT_SAN_LIMIT = 20       # certificates listing more names than this are shared (CDN) certs: ignored


def registrable(host: str) -> str:
    """Approximate registrable domain (handles common multi-part public suffixes)."""
    parts = host.lower().strip(".").split(".")
    if len(parts) >= 3 and ".".join(parts[-2:]) in _MULTI_TLDS:
        return ".".join(parts[-3:])
    return ".".join(parts[-2:]) if len(parts) >= 2 else host


def _belongs_to(name: str, domain: str) -> bool:
    """True for the domain itself or a true subdomain (not 'evilexample.com')."""
    return name == domain or name.endswith("." + domain)


def search_crtsh(domain: str, max_results: int = 50, retries: int = 1) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Query crt.sh for certificates related to a domain."""
    result: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "queried_at": timestamp(), "domain": domain, "success": False,
        "source": "https://crt.sh", "certificates_found": 0,
        "unique_names": [], "sample_entries": [], "errors": [], "related_domains": [],
    }

    url = f"https://crt.sh/?q=%25.{domain}&output=json"
    data = None
    for attempt in range(retries + 1):
        resp, err = safe_request(url, timeout=25)
        if err or resp is None:
            result["errors"].append(f"attempt {attempt + 1}: {err}")
            continue
        if resp.status_code != 200:
            result["errors"].append(f"attempt {attempt + 1}: HTTP {resp.status_code}")
            continue
        try:
            data = resp.json()
            break
        except Exception as e:
            # crt.sh sometimes returns HTML on error / overload
            result["errors"].append(f"attempt {attempt + 1}: JSON parse failed ({e})")

    if data is None:
        return result
    if not isinstance(data, list):
        result["errors"].append("Unexpected response format")
        return result

    names = set()
    samples = []
    related: Dict[str, int] = {}
    own = registrable(domain)
    for entry in data[: max_results * 3]:
        name_value = entry.get("name_value") or entry.get("common_name") or ""
