        parts = [x.strip().lower().lstrip("*.") for x in name_value.replace("\n", " ").split()]
        for part in parts:
            if _belongs_to(part, domain):
                names.add(part)
        if len(set(parts)) <= MULTI_TENANT_SAN_LIMIT:               # skip shared CDN certificates
            for part in set(parts):
                reg = registrable(part)
                if "." in part and reg != own and not part.startswith("sni"):
                    related[reg] = related.get(reg, 0) + 1
        if len(samples) < 8:
            samples.append({
                "id": entry.get("id"),
                "logged_at": entry.get("entry_timestamp"),
                "not_before": entry.get("not_before"),
                "not_after": entry.get("not_after"),
                "issuer": entry.get("issuer_name"),
                "name_value": name_value[:200],
            })

    result["success"] = True
    result["errors"] = []  # a retry succeeded; earlier attempts are noise
    result["certificates_found"] = len(data)
    result["unique_names"] = sorted(names)[:max_results]
    result["related_domains"] = [{"domain": d, "certificates": n} for d, n in
                                 sorted(related.items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0]))[:25]]
    result["related_domains_note"] = ("Domains that appear on the same certificates as this one. Often the same owner, "
                                      "but also CDNs/hosting platforms and partners – verify before relying on them.")
    result["sample_entries"] = samples
    return result


def search_crtsh_org(org_name: str, max_results: int = 25) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Domains appearing on certificates issued to a subject organisation (OV/EV certificates only).
    crt.sh does not return the subject name in results, so matches cannot be verified here:
    treat every domain as a LOW-confidence lead.
    """
    from urllib.parse import quote
    result: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "queried_at": timestamp(), "organisation": org_name, "success": False, "source": "https://crt.sh (O= search)",
        "domains": [], "errors": [],
        "note": "Leads only: crt.sh does not confirm the subject organisation in its results; verify each domain.",
    }
    resp, err = safe_request(f"https://crt.sh/?O={quote(org_name, safe='')}&output=json", timeout=25, retries=1)
    if resp is None or resp.status_code != 200:
        result["errors"].append(err or f"HTTP {getattr(resp, 'status_code', '?')}")
        return result
    try:
        data = resp.json()
    except ValueError:
        result["errors"].append("JSON parse failed")
        return result
    if not isinstance(data, list):
        result["errors"].append("Unexpected response format")
        return result
    counts: Dict[str, int] = {}
    for entry in data[:3000]:
        parts = [x.strip().lower().lstrip("*.") for x in (entry.get("name_value") or "").replace("\n", " ").split()]
        if len(set(parts)) > MULTI_TENANT_SAN_LIMIT:
            continue
        for part in set(parts):
            if "." in part and not part.startswith("sni"):
                counts[registrable(part)] = counts.get(registrable(part), 0) + 1
    result["success"] = True
    result["domains"] = [{"domain": d, "certificates": n} for d, n in
                         sorted(counts.items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0]))[:max_results]]
    return result
atlas/sources/dns_lookup.py229 lines
"""
DNS record collection using only the standard library.

A small, strict DNS client (RFC 1035) is implemented here: UDP with TCP
fallback, name-compression handling with loop protection, and validation of
the transaction id and question section. It queries the system's configured
resolvers (/etc/resolv.conf). Where those cannot be determined (e.g. Windows),
only A/AAAA records are collected via the operating system's resolver.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import os
import random
import socket
import struct
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from ..utils.network_safety import OFFLINE_MESSAGE, is_offline
from ..utils.helpers import timestamp

logger = logging.getLogger("atlas.sources.dns")

QTYPES = {"A": 1, "NS": 2, "CNAME": 5, "SOA": 6, "MX": 15, "TXT": 16, "AAAA": 28, "CAA": 257}
_TYPE_NAMES = {v: k for k, v in QTYPES.items()}
RECORD_TYPES = ["A", "AAAA", "MX", "NS", "TXT", "CNAME", "SOA", "CAA"]


class DNSError(Exception):
    pass


def get_system_nameservers(path: str = "/etc/resolv.conf") -> List[str]:
    servers: List[str] = []
    try:
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
            for line in f:
                parts = line.split()
                if len(parts) >= 2 and parts[0] == "nameserver":
                    ns = parts[1].split("%")[0]
                    if ns not in servers:
                        servers.append(ns)
    except OSError:
        pass
    return servers[:3]


def build_query(name: str, qtype: int, txid: Optional[int] = None) -> Tuple[int, bytes]:
    txid = random.randint(0, 0xFFFF) if txid is None else txid
    header = struct.pack(">HHHHHH", txid, 0x0100, 1, 0, 0, 0)
    qname = b""
    for label in name.rstrip(".").split("."):
        try:
            raw = label.encode("idna")
        except UnicodeError:
            raise DNSError("invalid label")
        if not 1 <= len(raw) <= 63:
            raise DNSError("invalid label length")
        qname += bytes([len(raw)]) + raw
    return txid, header + qname + b"\x00" + struct.pack(">HH", qtype, 1)


def read_name(msg: bytes, offset: int) -> Tuple[str, int]:
    """Decode a (possibly compressed) name. Returns (name, offset_after_name)."""
    labels: List[str] = []
    end: Optional[int] = None
    jumps = 0
    while True:
        if offset >= len(msg):
            raise DNSError("name runs past end of message")
        length = msg[offset]
        if length == 0:
            offset += 1
            break
        if length & 0xC0 == 0xC0:
            if offset + 1 >= len(msg):
                raise DNSError("truncated compression pointer")
            if end is None:
                end = offset + 2
            offset = ((length & 0x3F) << 8) | msg[offset + 1]
            jumps += 1
            if jumps > 20:
                raise DNSError("compression loop")
            continue
        if length & 0xC0:
            raise DNSError("unsupported label type")
        offset += 1
        if offset + length > len(msg):
            raise DNSError("label runs past end of message")
        labels.append(msg[offset:offset + length].decode("ascii", errors="replace"))
        offset += length
    return ".".join(labels), (end if end is not None else offset)


def parse_response(data: bytes, txid: int) -> Tuple[int, bool, List[Tuple[str, str]]]:
    """Return (rcode, truncated, [(type_name, value), ...]). Raises DNSError on malformed data."""
    if len(data) < 12:
        raise DNSError("short response")
    rid, flags, qd, an, _ns, _ar = struct.unpack(">HHHHHH", data[:12])
    if rid != txid:
        raise DNSError("transaction id mismatch")
    if not flags & 0x8000:
        raise DNSError("not a response")
    rcode, truncated = flags & 0xF, bool(flags & 0x0200)
    off = 12
    for _ in range(qd):
        _, off = read_name(data, off)
        off += 4
    out: List[Tuple[str, str]] = []
    for _ in range(min(an, 64)):
        _, off = read_name(data, off)
        if off + 10 > len(data):
            raise DNSError("truncated record header")
        rtype, _cls, _ttl, rdlen = struct.unpack(">HHIH", data[off:off + 10])
        off += 10
        rdata_end = off + rdlen
        if rdata_end > len(data):
            raise DNSError("record data runs past end of message")
        tname = _TYPE_NAMES.get(rtype)
        if tname:
            out.append((tname, _decode_rdata(data, off, rdlen, tname)))
        off = rdata_end
    return rcode, truncated, out


def _decode_rdata(msg: bytes, off: int, rdlen: int, tname: str) -> str:
    raw = msg[off:off + rdlen]
    if tname == "A" and rdlen == 4:
        return socket.inet_ntop(socket.AF_INET, raw)
    if tname == "AAAA" and rdlen == 16:
        return socket.inet_ntop(socket.AF_INET6, raw)
    if tname in ("NS", "CNAME"):
        return read_name(msg, off)[0]
    if tname == "MX":
        pref = struct.unpack(">H", raw[:2])[0]
        return f"{pref} {read_name(msg, off + 2)[0]}"
    if tname == "TXT":
        parts, i = [], 0
        while i < len(raw):
            n = raw[i]
            parts.append(raw[i + 1:i + 1 + n].decode("utf-8", errors="replace"))
            i += 1 + n
        return "".join(parts)
    if tname == "SOA":
        mname, o = read_name(msg, off)
        rname, o = read_name(msg, o)
        serial, refresh, retry, expire, minimum = struct.unpack(">IIIII", msg[o:o + 20])
        return f"{mname} {rname} {serial} {refresh} {retry} {expire} {minimum}"
    if tname == "CAA" and rdlen >= 2:
        flags, tag_len = raw[0], raw[1]
        tag = raw[2:2 + tag_len].decode("ascii", errors="replace")
        return f'{flags} {tag} "{raw[2 + tag_len:].decode("utf-8", errors="replace")}"'
    return raw.hex()


def query(name: str, rtype: str, server: str, timeout: float = 4.0) -> Tuple[int, List[Tuple[str, str]]]:
    txid, packet = build_query(name, QTYPES[rtype])
    family = socket.AF_INET6 if ":" in server else socket.AF_INET
    with socket.socket(family, socket.SOCK_DGRAM) as s:
        s.settimeout(timeout)
        s.sendto(packet, (server, 53))
        data, _ = s.recvfrom(4096)
    rcode, truncated, answers = parse_response(data, txid)
    if truncated:
        with socket.socket(family, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as s:
            s.settimeout(timeout)
            s.connect((server, 53))
            s.sendall(struct.pack(">H", len(packet)) + packet)
            hdr = s.recv(2)
            if len(hdr) < 2:
                raise DNSError("short TCP response")
            need, buf = struct.unpack(">H", hdr)[0], b""
            while len(buf) < need and len(buf) < 65535:
                chunk = s.recv(need - len(buf))
                if not chunk:
                    break
                buf += chunk
        rcode, _, answers = parse_response(buf, txid)
    return rcode, answers


def get_dns_records(domain: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Collect common DNS records for a domain."""
    result: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "queried_at": timestamp(), "domain": domain, "success": False,
        "records": {}, "errors": [], "method": None,
    }
    if is_offline():
        result["errors"].append(OFFLINE_MESSAGE)
        return result

    servers = get_system_nameservers()
    if servers:
        result["method"] = f"stdlib DNS client via {servers[0]}"
        for rtype in RECORD_TYPES:
            for server in servers:
                try:
                    rcode, answers = query(domain, rtype, server)
                except (OSError, DNSError, UnicodeError) as e:
                    result["errors"].append(f"{rtype}@{server}: {e}")
                    continue
                values = [v for t, v in answers if t == rtype]
                if values:
                    result["records"][rtype] = values
                break                       # got a definite answer (even if empty)
        if result["records"]:
            result["success"] = True
            return result

    # Fallback: OS resolver, A/AAAA only
    try:
        a, aaaa = [], []
        for info in socket.getaddrinfo(domain, None, type=socket.SOCK_STREAM):
            ip = info[4][0]
            bucket = aaaa if ":" in ip else a
            if ip not in bucket:
                bucket.append(ip)
        if a:
            result["records"]["A"] = a
        if aaaa:
            result["records"]["AAAA"] = aaaa
        if result["records"]:
            result["success"] = True
            result["method"] = "socket.getaddrinfo (A/AAAA only)"
    except (OSError, UnicodeError) as e:
        result["errors"].append(f"stdlib resolve: {e}")
    return result
atlas/sources/geoip.py152 lines
"""
Coarse IP geolocation with cross-checking.

Two independent HTTPS providers (ipinfo.io, ipwho.is) are queried and compared; the result reports
whether they AGREE on country / region / city. ip-api.com (cleartext HTTP only) is used only if both
HTTPS providers fail. Always approximate network location, never a person's address.
Data minimisation: latitude/longitude and postcodes are never requested from or stored for ip-api
and are discarded from the others.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from ..utils.helpers import is_offline, is_public_ip, safe_request, timestamp

_ASN_RE = re.compile(r"\bAS(\d{1,10})\b", re.IGNORECASE)


def parse_asn(*texts):
    """Extract 'AS12345' from provider strings such as 'AS15169 Google LLC'."""
    for t in texts:
        if t:
            m = _ASN_RE.search(str(t))
            if m:
                return f"AS{m.group(1)}"
    return None


def _norm(v: Any) -> str:
    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]", "", str(v or "").lower())


def _ipinfo(ip: str):
    resp, err = safe_request(f"https://ipinfo.io/{ip}/json", timeout=8, retries=1)
    if resp is None or resp.status_code != 200:
        return None, f"ipinfo.io: {err or 'HTTP ' + str(getattr(resp, 'status_code', '?'))}"
    try:
        d = resp.json()
    except ValueError as e:
        return None, f"ipinfo.io parse: {e}"
    if not isinstance(d, dict) or d.get("bogon") or not d.get("country"):
        return None, "ipinfo.io: no usable data"
    return {"country_code": str(d.get("country")).upper(), "country": None, "region": d.get("region"),
            "city": d.get("city"), "timezone": d.get("timezone"), "organisation": d.get("org"),
            "hostname": d.get("hostname"), "as": d.get("org")}, None


def _ipwho(ip: str):
    resp, err = safe_request(f"https://ipwho.is/{ip}", timeout=8, retries=1)
    if resp is None or resp.status_code != 200:
        return None, f"ipwho.is: {err or 'HTTP ' + str(getattr(resp, 'status_code', '?'))}"
    try:
        d = resp.json()
    except ValueError as e:
        return None, f"ipwho.is parse: {e}"
    if not isinstance(d, dict) or d.get("success") is False or not d.get("country_code"):
        return None, f"ipwho.is: {d.get('message', 'no usable data') if isinstance(d, dict) else 'no usable data'}"
    conn = d.get("connection") or {}
    asn = conn.get("asn")
    org = conn.get("org") or conn.get("isp")
    tz = d.get("timezone")
    return {"country_code": str(d.get("country_code")).upper(), "country": d.get("country"),
            "region": d.get("region"), "city": d.get("city"),
            "timezone": tz.get("id") if isinstance(tz, dict) else tz,
            "organisation": f"AS{asn} {org}" if asn and org else org, "isp": conn.get("isp"),
            "as": f"AS{asn} {org}" if asn and org else None, "hostname": None}, None


def _ipapi(ip: str):
    url = (f"http://ip-api.com/json/{ip}?fields=status,message,country,countryCode,regionName,city,timezone,"
           "isp,org,as,asname,mobile,proxy,hosting")
    resp, err = safe_request(url, timeout=8, retries=1)
    if resp is None or resp.status_code != 200:
        return None, f"ip-api.com: {err or 'HTTP ' + str(getattr(resp, 'status_code', '?'))}"
    try:
        d = resp.json()
    except ValueError as e:
        return None, f"ip-api.com parse: {e}"
    if d.get("status") != "success":
        return None, f"ip-api.com: {d.get('message')}"
    return {"country_code": str(d.get("countryCode") or "").upper() or None, "country": d.get("country"),
            "region": d.get("regionName"), "city": d.get("city"), "timezone": d.get("timezone"),
            "isp": d.get("isp"), "organisation": d.get("org"), "as": d.get("as"), "as_name": d.get("asname"),
            "is_mobile": d.get("mobile"), "is_proxy": d.get("proxy"), "is_hosting": d.get("hosting")}, None


def _agree(a: Any, b: Any) -> Optional[bool]:
    if not a or not b:
        return None
    return _norm(a) == _norm(b)


def lookup_ip_geo(ip: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    result: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "queried_at": timestamp(), "ip": ip, "success": False, "source": None, "sources_used": [],
        "data": {}, "errors": [], "cross_check": {},
        "disclaimer": (
            "IP geolocation identifies an approximate NETWORK location (usually the provider's infrastructure), "
            "not necessarily where the person using the address is. It can be wrong at country level and is "
            "frequently wrong at city level. It does not identify a specific person or exact address. "
            "Coordinates and postcodes are intentionally not collected."),
    }
    if is_offline():
        result["errors"].append("offline mode: geolocation skipped.")
        return result
    if not is_public_ip(ip):
        result["errors"].append("Not a public IP address; geolocation skipped.")
        return result

    answers: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for name, fn in (("ipinfo.io", _ipinfo), ("ipwho.is", _ipwho)):
        data, err = fn(ip)
        if data:
            data["_source"] = name
            answers.append(data)
        else:
            result["errors"].append(err)
    if not answers:                                    # cleartext fallback only when both HTTPS providers failed
        data, err = _ipapi(ip)
        if data:
            data["_source"] = "ip-api.com (cleartext HTTP)"
            answers.append(data)
        else:
            result["errors"].append(err)
    if not answers:
        return result

    merged: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    for a in answers:                                  # first provider wins; later ones only fill gaps
        for k, v in a.items():
            if k != "_source" and v not in (None, "") and k not in merged:
                merged[k] = v
    if not merged.get("country"):
        merged["country"] = merged.get("country_code")
    result.update(success=True, data=merged, sources_used=[a["_source"] for a in answers],
                  source=" + ".join(a["_source"] for a in answers))
    if len(answers) >= 2:
        a, b = answers[0], answers[1]
        result["cross_check"] = {
            "providers": [a["_source"], b["_source"]],
            "country_agreement": _agree(a.get("country_code"), b.get("country_code")),
            "region_agreement": _agree(a.get("region"), b.get("region")),
            "city_agreement": _agree(a.get("city"), b.get("city")),
        }
    else:
        result["cross_check"] = {"providers": [answers[0]["_source"]], "country_agreement": None,
                                 "region_agreement": None, "city_agreement": None,
                                 "note": "Only one provider answered; the result could not be cross-checked."}
    return result
atlas/sources/page_metadata.py157 lines
"""
Metadata extraction from public HTML pages using html.parser (stdlib).
Never executes or renders page content; output is plain data.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import html
import json
import re
from html.parser import HTMLParser
from typing import Any, Dict, Iterator, List
from urllib.parse import urljoin

MAX_JSONLD_BLOCKS = 10
MAX_JSONLD_CHARS = 200_000


class _Parser(HTMLParser):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(convert_charrefs=True)
        self.title = ""
        self.meta: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
        self.links: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
        self.jsonld: List[str] = []
        self.h1: List[str] = []
        self._in_title = False
        self._in_jsonld = False
        self._in_h1 = False
        self._buf: List[str] = []

    def handle_starttag(self, tag, attrs):
        a = {k.lower(): (v or "") for k, v in attrs}
        if tag == "title" and not self.title:
            self._in_title, self._buf = True, []
        elif tag == "meta":
            key = (a.get("property") or a.get("name") or a.get("itemprop") or "").lower()
            if key and "content" in a:
                self.meta.setdefault(key, []).append(a["content"].strip())
        elif tag == "link" and a.get("href"):
            for rel in a.get("rel", "").lower().split():
                self.links.setdefault(rel, []).append(a["href"].strip())
        elif tag == "script" and "ld+json" in a.get("type", "").lower():
            if len(self.jsonld) < MAX_JSONLD_BLOCKS:
                self._in_jsonld, self._buf = True, []
        elif tag == "h1" and len(self.h1) < 3:
            self._in_h1, self._buf = True, []

    def handle_data(self, data):
        if self._in_title or self._in_jsonld or self._in_h1:
            self._buf.append(data)

    def handle_endtag(self, tag):
        if tag == "title" and self._in_title:
            self.title = " ".join("".join(self._buf).split())[:300]
            self._in_title = False
        elif tag == "script" and self._in_jsonld:
            self.jsonld.append("".join(self._buf)[:MAX_JSONLD_CHARS])
            self._in_jsonld = False
        elif tag == "h1" and self._in_h1:
            self.h1.append(" ".join("".join(self._buf).split())[:200])
            self._in_h1 = False


def extract(html_text: str, base_url: str = "") -> Dict[str, Any]:
    p = _Parser()
    try:
        p.feed(html_text)
        p.close()
    except Exception:       # malformed HTML must never crash a report
        pass
    nodes: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for block in p.jsonld:
        try:
            nodes.extend(iter_jsonld_nodes(json.loads(block)))
        except (ValueError, RecursionError):
            continue
    return {
        "title": p.title, "meta": p.meta, "links": p.links, "h1": p.h1,
        "jsonld": nodes, "base_url": base_url,
    }


def meta_first(meta: Dict[str, List[str]], *keys: str) -> str:
    for k in keys:
        vals = meta.get(k.lower())
        if vals and vals[0]:
            return vals[0]
    return ""


def iter_jsonld_nodes(data: Any, depth: int = 0) -> Iterator[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Flatten JSON-LD (@graph, lists, nested objects) into typed dict nodes."""
    if depth > 6:
        return
    if isinstance(data, list):
        for item in data[:50]:
            yield from iter_jsonld_nodes(item, depth + 1)
    elif isinstance(data, dict):
        if "@type" in data:
            yield data
        if "@graph" in data:
            yield from iter_jsonld_nodes(data["@graph"], depth + 1)


def node_types(node: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[str]:
    t = node.get("@type")
    if isinstance(t, str):
        return [t]
    if isinstance(t, list):
        return [x for x in t if isinstance(x, str)]
    return []


def text_of(value: Any, limit: int = 200) -> str:
    """Coerce a JSON-LD value (str / {name:..} / list) to a short clean string."""
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        value = value.get("name") or value.get("@id") or ""
    elif isinstance(value, list):
        value = value[0] if value else ""
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            value = value.get("name") or ""
    if not isinstance(value, str):
        return ""
    return " ".join(html.unescape(value).split())[:limit]


def image_urls_from_jsonld_value(value: Any, base_url: str) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
    """Return [{url, caption, license, credit}] from a JSON-LD image/logo property."""
    out: List[Dict[str, str]] = []
    items = value if isinstance(value, list) else [value]
    for item in items[:5]:
        if isinstance(item, str):
            out.append({"url": urljoin(base_url, item), "caption": "", "license": "", "credit": ""})
        elif isinstance(item, dict):
            u = item.get("contentUrl") or item.get("url") or item.get("@id")
            if isinstance(u, str):
                out.append({
                    "url": urljoin(base_url, u),
                    "caption": text_of(item.get("caption") or item.get("description") or item.get("name")),
                    "license": text_of(item.get("license")),
                    "credit": text_of(item.get("creditText") or item.get("creator")
                                      or item.get("copyrightHolder")),
                })
    return out


def tokens(name: str) -> List[str]:
    return [t for t in re.findall(r"[^\W_]+", name.lower()) if len(t) > 1]


def name_in_text(name: str, text: str) -> bool:
    """True if every significant token of *name* appears as a whole word in *text*."""
    toks = tokens(name)
    hay = set(re.findall(r"[^\W_]+", (text or "").lower()))
    return bool(toks) and all(t in hay for t in toks)
atlas/sources/provider_hints.py45 lines
"""Infer hosting / email / DNS providers from DNS records already collected (no extra requests)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Dict, List

_MX = {"google.com": "Google Workspace / Gmail", "googlemail.com": "Google Workspace / Gmail",
       "outlook.com": "Microsoft 365 / Exchange Online", "protection.outlook.com": "Microsoft 365 / Exchange Online",
       "mimecast.com": "Mimecast (email security)", "pphosted.com": "Proofpoint (email security)",
       "zoho.com": "Zoho Mail", "protonmail.ch": "Proton Mail", "messagingengine.com": "Fastmail",
       "mailgun.org": "Mailgun", "icloud.com": "Apple iCloud Mail", "yahoodns.net": "Yahoo Mail"}
_NS = {"cloudflare.com": "Cloudflare DNS", "awsdns": "Amazon Route 53", "azure-dns": "Microsoft Azure DNS",
       "googledomains.com": "Google Domains / Cloud DNS", "domaincontrol.com": "GoDaddy DNS",
       "registrar-servers.com": "Namecheap DNS", "nsone.net": "NS1", "dnsimple.com": "DNSimple",
       "ultradns": "UltraDNS", "akam.net": "Akamai DNS", "digitalocean.com": "DigitalOcean DNS"}
_SPF = {"_spf.google.com": "Google Workspace", "spf.protection.outlook.com": "Microsoft 365",
        "sendgrid.net": "SendGrid", "mailgun.org": "Mailgun", "amazonses.com": "Amazon SES",
        "servers.mcsv.net": "Mailchimp", "mandrillapp.com": "Mailchimp Transactional", "zoho.com": "Zoho",
        "salesforce.com": "Salesforce", "hubspot": "HubSpot", "zendesk": "Zendesk", "freshdesk": "Freshdesk"}


def _match(value: str, table: Dict[str, str]) -> List[str]:
    v = value.lower().rstrip(".")
    return [label for key, label in table.items() if key in v]


def infer_providers(records: Dict[str, List[str]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    out: Dict[str, Any] = {"email": [], "dns": [], "sending_services": [], "caveat":
                           "Inferred from public DNS records; indicates services configured, not who operates the domain."}
    for mx in records.get("MX", []):
        host = mx.split()[-1]
        for label in _match(host, _MX):
            if label not in out["email"]:
                out["email"].append(label)
    for ns in records.get("NS", []):
        for label in _match(ns, _NS):
            if label not in out["dns"]:
                out["dns"].append(label)
    for txt in records.get("TXT", []):
        if txt.lower().startswith("v=spf1"):
            for label in _match(txt, _SPF):
                if label not in out["sending_services"]:
                    out["sending_services"].append(label)
    return out
atlas/sources/rdap.py163 lines
"""
RDAP (Registration Data Access Protocol) lookups.
Uses the public rdap.org bootstrap redirector first, then well-known
registry endpoints as fallbacks. No authentication required.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import logging
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from ..utils.helpers import safe_request, timestamp

logger = logging.getLogger("atlas.sources.rdap")

IP_RDAP_SERVERS = [
    "https://rdap.org/ip/{ip}",                      # bootstrap: redirects to the right RIR
    "https://rdap.arin.net/registry/ip/{ip}",
    "https://rdap.db.ripe.net/ip/{ip}",
    "https://rdap.apnic.net/ip/{ip}",
    "https://rdap.lacnic.net/rdap/ip/{ip}",
    "https://rdap.afrinic.net/rdap/ip/{ip}",
]

# TLD-specific registry endpoints, used only when the TLD actually matches.
TLD_RDAP_SERVERS = {
    "com": "https://rdap.verisign.com/com/v1/domain/{domain}",
    "net": "https://rdap.verisign.com/net/v1/domain/{domain}",
    "uk": "https://rdap.nic.uk/domain/{domain}",
}

_PERSONAL_VCARD_FIELDS = {"tel", "email", "adr", "geo", "impp"}


def redact_personal_contacts(data: Any) -> Any:
    """
    Return a deep copy of RDAP data with vCard phone / email / postal-address
    fields replaced by a marker. ATLAS's stated policy is not to collect
    personal contact details, so raw exports drop them by default.
    """
    data = copy.deepcopy(data)

    def walk(node: Any) -> None:
        if isinstance(node, dict):
            vcard = node.get("vcardArray")
            if isinstance(vcard, list) and len(vcard) == 2 and isinstance(vcard[1], list):
                for item in vcard[1]:
                    if isinstance(item, list) and item and item[0] in _PERSONAL_VCARD_FIELDS:
                        item[1:] = [{}, "text", "[redacted by ATLAS]"]
            for v in node.values():
                walk(v)
        elif isinstance(node, list):
            for v in node:
                walk(v)

    walk(data)
    return data


def _extract_entity_name(entity: Dict) -> Optional[str]:
    """Try to pull a human-readable name from an RDAP entity."""
    vcard = entity.get("vcardArray")
    if isinstance(vcard, list) and len(vcard) == 2:
        try:
            for item in vcard[1]:
                if item[0] == "fn":
                    return item[3]
        except (IndexError, TypeError, KeyError):
            pass
    return entity.get("handle") or entity.get("name")


def _fetch_first(urls: List[str], result: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Try each URL in turn; record errors; return parsed JSON of the first success."""
    for url in urls:
        resp, err = safe_request(url, timeout=12)
        if err or resp is None:
            result["errors"].append(f"{url}: {err}")
            continue
        if resp.status_code != 200:
            result["errors"].append(f"{url}: HTTP {resp.status_code}")
            continue
        try:
            data = resp.json()
        except Exception as e:
            result["errors"].append(f"{url}: JSON parse error – {e}")
            continue
        result["source"] = resp.url or url
        return data
    return None


def query_rdap_ip(ip: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Query public RDAP services for an IP address."""
    result: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "queried_at": timestamp(), "ip": ip, "success": False,
        "source": None, "data": {}, "raw_summary": {}, "errors": [],
    }
    data = _fetch_first([t.format(ip=ip) for t in IP_RDAP_SERVERS], result)
    if data is None:
        return result

    result["success"] = True
    result["data"] = redact_personal_contacts(data)
    result["raw_summary"] = {
        "handle": data.get("handle"),
        "name": data.get("name"),
        "type": data.get("type"),
        "country": data.get("country"),
        "start_address": data.get("startAddress"),
        "end_address": data.get("endAddress"),
        "ip_version": data.get("ipVersion"),
        "status": data.get("status"),
        "entities": [
            {"roles": e.get("roles"), "name": _extract_entity_name(e), "handle": e.get("handle")}
            for e in data.get("entities", []) or []
        ],
        "remarks": [
            {"title": r.get("title"), "description": r.get("description")}
            for r in data.get("remarks", []) or []
        ],
        "links": [
            {"rel": l.get("rel"), "href": l.get("href")} for l in data.get("links", []) or []
        ],
    }
    return result


def query_rdap_domain(domain: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Query public RDAP for a domain name."""
    result: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "queried_at": timestamp(), "domain": domain, "success": False,
        "source": None, "data": {}, "raw_summary": {}, "errors": [],
    }
    urls = [f"https://rdap.org/domain/{domain}"]
    tld = domain.rsplit(".", 1)[-1].lower()
    if tld in TLD_RDAP_SERVERS:
        urls.append(TLD_RDAP_SERVERS[tld].format(domain=domain))

    data = _fetch_first(urls, result)
    if data is None:
        return result

    result["success"] = True
    result["data"] = redact_personal_contacts(data)
    result["raw_summary"] = {
        "ldhName": data.get("ldhName"),
        "unicodeName": data.get("unicodeName"),
        "status": data.get("status"),
        "port43": data.get("port43"),
        "events": data.get("events", []),
        "nameservers": [
            ns.get("ldhName") or ns.get("unicodeName") for ns in data.get("nameservers", []) or []
        ],
        "entities": [
            {"roles": e.get("roles"), "handle": e.get("handle"), "name": _extract_entity_name(e)}
            for e in data.get("entities", []) or []
        ],
        "secureDNS": data.get("secureDNS"),
    }
    return result
atlas/sources/reputation.py105 lines
"""
Public reputation / blocklist checks (passive DNS-based blocklist queries).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import ipaddress
import logging
import socket
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from ..utils.helpers import is_offline, is_public_ip, timestamp

logger = logging.getLogger("atlas.sources.reputation")

# (zone, display name). Edit this list to add or remove sources.
RBLS: List[Tuple[str, str]] = [
    ("zen.spamhaus.org", "Spamhaus ZEN"),
    ("bl.spamcop.net", "SpamCop"),
]


def _rbl_query_name(ip: str, zone: str) -> str:
    """Reverse-notation query name for IPv4 *and* IPv6 addresses."""
    pointer = ipaddress.ip_address(ip).reverse_pointer
    for suffix in (".in-addr.arpa", ".ip6.arpa"):
        if pointer.endswith(suffix):
            pointer = pointer[: -len(suffix)]
    return f"{pointer}.{zone}"


def interpret_rbl_answer(answer: Optional[str]) -> Tuple[Optional[bool], str]:
    """
    Turn a DNSBL answer into (listed, detail).

    * NXDOMAIN (None)       -> not listed
    * 127.255.255.x         -> Spamhaus-style "query refused" (public resolver,
                               rate limit, etc.) -> UNKNOWN, *not* a listing
    * any other 127.x.x.x   -> listed
    * anything else         -> unknown (possible DNS hijack / wildcard)
    """
    if answer is None:
        return False, "Not listed (NXDOMAIN)"
    if answer.startswith("127.255.255."):
        return None, (
            f"Query refused by blocklist ({answer}) – typically a public/open resolver "
            "or rate limit; result is UNKNOWN, not a listing"
        )
    if answer.startswith("127."):
        return True, f"Listed (return code {answer})"
    return None, f"Unexpected answer {answer}; ignoring"


def check_ip_reputation(ip: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Check a few public, free DNS blocklists.
    Indicators only – not definitive proof of malicious activity.
    """
    result: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "queried_at": timestamp(), "ip": ip, "success": False,
        "sources_checked": [], "findings": [], "errors": [],
        "disclaimer": (
            "Reputation data is incomplete and can contain false positives. "
            "Presence on a list does not automatically mean the IP is malicious. "
            "Always corroborate with multiple sources and context."
        ),
    }

    if is_offline():
        result["errors"].append("offline mode: blocklist checks skipped.")
        return result
    if not is_public_ip(ip):
        result["errors"].append("Not a public IP address; blocklist checks skipped.")
        return result

    for zone, name in RBLS:
        query = _rbl_query_name(ip, zone)
        try:
            answer: Optional[str] = socket.gethostbyname(query)
        except socket.gaierror:
            answer = None
        except Exception as e:
            result["errors"].append(f"{name}: {e}")
            continue
        listed, detail = interpret_rbl_answer(answer)
        result["findings"].append({
            "source": name,
            "listed": listed,
            "detail": detail,
            "confidence": 0.0 if listed is None else (0.7 if listed else 0.6),
        })
        result["sources_checked"].append(name)

    result["findings"].append({
        "source": "Manual recommendation",
        "listed": None,
        "detail": (
            "For deeper reputation data consider https://www.abuseipdb.com/ , "
            "https://virustotal.com/ or https://www.greynoise.io/ (respect their terms)."
        ),
        "confidence": 0.0,
    })
    result["success"] = bool(result["sources_checked"])
    return result
atlas/sources/username_sources.py329 lines
"""
Username presence checks on public platforms (passive, existence only).

For each platform ATLAS requests ONE public profile/API URL for the exact username and
interprets the HTTP response. It never stores profile content (bio, photos, followers, location),
never logs in, never tries variants, and never links accounts to a person.

Accuracy protections (important with a large platform list):
* robots.txt is honoured (platforms that disallow automated access are reported as skipped)
* usernames are validated against each platform's own format before any request is made
* soft-404 detection: a 200 page whose title/heading says "not found" is treated as absent
* TWO CONTROL CHECKS: when a name looks "found", two random names that cannot exist are tested on
  the same platform; if either also looks "found" the platform is marked unreliable
* transient failures (429/502/503/504, connection errors) are retried once; long Retry-After is respected
* graded confidence: HIGH (controls passed AND the username appears in the page title/URL),
  MEDIUM (controls passed), LOW (controls inconclusive)
* `selftest` health-checks every rule with a negative control and, where available, a known-good account

Page text is read transiently (first 100 KB) only to compute two booleans (soft-404, username-in-title);
nothing from it is stored.

The platform list lives in atlas/data/platforms.json and can be replaced with your own file (--platforms).
Policy: mainstream developer / community / creative / gaming platforms only. No dating, adult, messaging,
health or finance services. Platforms that block bots are never queried; manual links are printed instead.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import pathlib
import re
import secrets
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from urllib.parse import quote, urlparse

from ..utils.helpers import safe_request, timestamp
from . import page_metadata as pm

logger = logging.getLogger("atlas.sources.username")

USERNAME_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,38}$")
BODY_CAP = 100_000
MAX_PLATFORMS = 300
DATA_FILE = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "data" / "platforms.json"
EXCLUDED_CATEGORIES = ("dating", "adult", "messaging", "health", "finance")
KINDS = ("status", "message", "json")

SOFT_404 = re.compile(
    r"\b(404|not found|page not found|doesn'?t exist|does not exist|no such (user|page|profile|account)|"
    r"(profile|user|account|page) (is )?(unavailable|not available)|couldn'?t find|could not find|"
    r"can'?t be found|isn'?t available|no longer available)\b", re.IGNORECASE)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Platform:
    name: str
    category: str
    url: str                                   # URL requested ("{}" = username)
    kind: str = "status"                       # status | message | json
    profile_url: Optional[str] = None          # human-facing URL if different from `url`
    pattern: Optional[str] = None              # username format accepted by this platform
    absent_text: Optional[str] = None          # kind=message: text shown when the user does not exist
    exists_path: Tuple[Any, ...] = ()          # kind=json: path that exists when the user exists
    absent_path: Tuple[Any, ...] = ()          # kind=json: path that exists when the user is absent
    absent_if_empty: bool = False              # kind=json: an empty list/object means "no such user"
    absent_codes: Tuple[int, ...] = (404, 410)  # HTTP codes that mean "no such user"
    known_good: Optional[str] = None           # an account expected to exist (used by selftest only)


# Platforms that restrict automated access: never queried, links are printed for manual use only.
MANUAL_LINKS: List[Tuple[str, str]] = [
    ("Instagram", "https://www.instagram.com/{}/"),
    ("X (Twitter)", "https://x.com/{}"),
    ("TikTok", "https://www.tiktok.com/@{}"),
    ("Facebook", "https://www.facebook.com/{}"),
    ("LinkedIn", "https://www.linkedin.com/in/{}"),
    ("Reddit", "https://www.reddit.com/user/{}"),
    ("YouTube", "https://www.youtube.com/@{}"),
    ("Threads", "https://www.threads.net/@{}"),
    ("Pinterest", "https://www.pinterest.com/{}/"),
]


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- loading
def _as_path(v: Any) -> Tuple[Any, ...]:
    return tuple(v) if isinstance(v, (list, tuple)) else ()


def parse_platform(d: Dict[str, Any]) -> Platform:
    """Validate one platform entry. Raises ValueError with a clear message."""
    if not isinstance(d, dict):
        raise ValueError("entry is not an object")
    name, cat, url = d.get("name"), d.get("category"), d.get("url")
    if not all(isinstance(x, str) and x.strip() for x in (name, cat, url)):
        raise ValueError("name, category and url are required text fields")
    if str(cat).lower() in EXCLUDED_CATEGORIES:
        raise ValueError(f"category {cat!r} is not supported by ATLAS (dating/adult/messaging/health/finance are excluded)")
    if not url.startswith("https://") or url.count("{}") != 1:
        raise ValueError("url must start with https:// and contain exactly one {} placeholder")
    host = urlparse(url.replace("{}", "x")).hostname or ""
    if not host or re.fullmatch(r"[\d.:]+", host):
        raise ValueError("url must use a host name, not an IP address")
    kind = d.get("kind", "status")
    if kind not in KINDS:
        raise ValueError(f"kind must be one of {KINDS}")
    if kind == "message" and not d.get("absent_text"):
        raise ValueError("kind 'message' needs absent_text")
    if kind == "json" and not (d.get("exists_path") or d.get("absent_path") or d.get("absent_if_empty")):
        raise ValueError("kind 'json' needs exists_path, absent_path or absent_if_empty")
    pattern = d.get("pattern")
    if pattern:
        re.compile(pattern)
    pu = d.get("profile_url")
    if pu and (not pu.startswith("https://") or pu.count("{}") != 1):
        raise ValueError("profile_url must start with https:// and contain one {}")
    codes = d.get("absent_codes") or [404, 410]
    if not all(isinstance(c, int) and 300 <= c < 600 for c in codes):
        raise ValueError("absent_codes must be HTTP status codes")
    return Platform(name=name.strip(), category=str(cat).lower(), url=url, kind=kind, profile_url=pu or None,
                    pattern=pattern or None, absent_text=d.get("absent_text"),
                    exists_path=_as_path(d.get("exists_path")), absent_path=_as_path(d.get("absent_path")),
                    absent_if_empty=bool(d.get("absent_if_empty")), absent_codes=tuple(codes),
                    known_good=d.get("known_good") or None)


def load_platforms(path: Optional[str] = None) -> Tuple[List[Platform], List[str]]:
    """Load the built-in list or a user file. Returns (platforms, warnings); invalid entries are skipped."""
    warnings: List[str] = []
    src = pathlib.Path(path) if path else DATA_FILE
    try:
        if not src.is_file():
            raise ValueError("file not found")
        if src.stat().st_size > 1_000_000:
            raise ValueError("file is larger than 1 MB")
        data = json.loads(src.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, ValueError) as e:
        return [], [f"could not read platform list {src}: {e}"]
    rows = data.get("platforms") if isinstance(data, dict) else data
    if not isinstance(rows, list):
        return [], [f"{src}: expected a list of platforms"]
    out: List[Platform] = []
    seen = set()
    for i, row in enumerate(rows[:MAX_PLATFORMS]):
        try:
            p = parse_platform(row)
        except (ValueError, re.error) as e:
            warnings.append(f"entry {i + 1} ({row.get('name') if isinstance(row, dict) else '?'}) skipped: {e}")
            continue
        if p.name in seen:
            warnings.append(f"duplicate platform {p.name!r} skipped")
            continue
        seen.add(p.name)
        out.append(p)
    if len(rows) > MAX_PLATFORMS:
        warnings.append(f"only the first {MAX_PLATFORMS} platforms were used")
    return out, warnings


PLATFORMS, _BUILTIN_WARNINGS = load_platforms()


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- helpers
def validate_username(name: str) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    if not isinstance(name, str) or not name:
        return False, "empty username"
    if not USERNAME_RE.match(name):
        return False, ("usernames may contain letters, digits, '.', '_' and '-' (1-39 characters, "
                       "starting with a letter or digit)")
    return True, ""


def _fill(template: str, username: str) -> str:
    return template.replace("{}", quote(username, safe=""))


def _dig(data: Any, path: Tuple[Any, ...]) -> bool:
    cur = data
    for key in path:
        try:
            cur = cur[key]
        except (KeyError, IndexError, TypeError):
            return False
    return True


def _same_page(requested: str, final: str) -> bool:
    a, b = urlparse(requested), urlparse(final)
    norm = lambda p: (p.path.rstrip("/").lower(), p.query.lower())
    return a.hostname == b.hostname and norm(a) == norm(b)


def control_name(p: Optional[Platform] = None) -> str:
    """A random name that cannot reasonably exist, sized to fit the platform's length limit."""
    maxlen = 18
    if p is not None and p.pattern:
        limits = [int(b) for _a, b in re.findall(r"\{(\d+),(\d+)\}", p.pattern)]
        if limits:
            maxlen = max(8, min(maxlen, limits[-1]))
    name = ("zx" + secrets.token_hex(9))[:maxlen]
    if p is not None and p.pattern and not re.match(p.pattern, name):
        logger.debug("control name does not fit %s's pattern; using it anyway", p.name)
    return name


@dataclass
class Probe:
    state: str                    # exists | absent | inconclusive | skipped_robots | offline | error
    detail: str = ""
    http_status: Optional[int] = None
    username_in_page: Optional[bool] = None


def _signals(resp, username: str) -> Tuple[bool, bool]:
    """(soft_404, username_in_page) computed from the page; text is not kept."""
    ctype = resp.header("Content-Type").lower()
    uname = username.lower()
    if "html" in ctype:
        doc = pm.extract(resp.text, resp.url or "")
        canon = (doc["links"].get("canonical") or [""])[0]
        og_title = pm.meta_first(doc["meta"], "og:title")
        og_url = pm.meta_first(doc["meta"], "og:url")
        h1 = " ".join(doc["h1"])
        soft = bool(SOFT_404.search(f"{doc['title']} {og_title} {h1}"))
        hay = " ".join([doc["title"], og_title, canon, og_url, h1]).lower()
        return soft, uname in hay
    return False, uname in resp.text.lower()


def probe(p: Platform, username: str, retries: int = 1) -> Probe:
    """One check of one username on one platform."""
    url = _fill(p.url, username)
    resp, err = safe_request(url, timeout=10, respect_robots=True, read_body=True, max_bytes=BODY_CAP,
                             retries=retries, headers={"Accept": "text/html,application/json;q=0.9,*/*;q=0.5"})
    if resp is None:
        if err and err.startswith("offline"):
            return Probe("offline", err)
        if err and err.startswith("not fetched"):
            return Probe("skipped_robots", err)
        return Probe("error", err or "request failed")
    code = resp.status_code
    if code in p.absent_codes:
        return Probe("absent", f"HTTP {code}", code)
    if code != 200:
        return Probe("inconclusive", f"HTTP {code} (rate-limited, blocked or error)", code)
    if not _same_page(url, resp.url or url):
        return Probe("inconclusive", "redirected to a different page", code)

    soft404, in_page = _signals(resp, username)
    if soft404:
        return Probe("absent", "page says the profile was not found (soft 404)", code, in_page)
    if p.kind == "status":
        return Probe("exists", "HTTP 200", code, in_page)
    if p.kind == "message":
        if p.absent_text and p.absent_text.lower() in resp.text.lower():
            return Probe("absent", "profile-not-found message shown", code, in_page)
        return Probe("exists", "HTTP 200, no not-found message", code, in_page)
    try:                                                    # kind == "json"
        data = resp.json()
    except ValueError:
        return Probe("inconclusive", "unexpected (non-JSON) response", code)
    if p.absent_if_empty and not data:
        return Probe("absent", "API returned no user", code, False)
    if p.absent_path and _dig(data, p.absent_path):
        return Probe("absent", "API reports the user is missing", code, False)
    if p.exists_path and _dig(data, p.exists_path):
        return Probe("exists", "API returned a user record", code, in_page)
    return Probe("inconclusive", "API response not understood", code)


def check_platform(p: Platform, username: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    res: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "platform": p.name, "category": p.category, "status": "error", "detail": "",
        "profile_url": _fill(p.profile_url or p.url, username), "http_status": None,
        "checked_at": timestamp(), "control_check": None, "existence_confidence": None,
        "username_in_page": None,
    }
    if p.pattern and not re.match(p.pattern, username):
        res.update(status="skipped_format", detail="username format not valid on this platform (no request made)")
        return res

    first = probe(p, username)
    res["http_status"], res["detail"], res["username_in_page"] = first.http_status, first.detail, first.username_in_page
    if first.state == "absent":
        res["status"] = "not_found"
    elif first.state in ("skipped_robots", "offline", "error", "inconclusive"):
        res["status"] = first.state
    elif first.state == "exists":
        controls = [probe(p, control_name(p)).state for _ in range(2)]      # two names that cannot exist
        res["controls"] = controls
        if "exists" in controls:
            res.update(status="unreliable", control_check="failed",
                       detail="a random non-existent name also looked 'found' – this platform cannot be checked reliably")
        else:
            res["status"] = "found"
            if controls == ["absent", "absent"]:
                res["control_check"] = "passed"
                res["existence_confidence"] = "HIGH" if first.username_in_page else "MEDIUM"
            else:
                res["control_check"] = "inconclusive"
                res["existence_confidence"] = "LOW"
            if first.username_in_page:
                res["detail"] += "; username appears in the page title/URL"
    return res


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- selftest
def selftest_platform(p: Platform) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Negative control (random name) + positive control (known-good account) for one rule."""
    neg = probe(p, control_name(p))
    pos = probe(p, p.known_good) if p.known_good and (not p.pattern or re.match(p.pattern, p.known_good)) else None
    if neg.state == "exists":
        verdict, note = "unreliable", "answers 'found' for a name that cannot exist – remove or fix this rule"
    elif neg.state == "skipped_robots":
        verdict, note = "blocked_by_robots", "robots.txt disallows (or could not confirm) automated access"
    elif neg.state in ("inconclusive", "error", "offline"):
        verdict, note = "unreachable_or_blocked", neg.detail
    elif pos is None:
        verdict, note = "ok_negative_only", "negative control passes; no known-good account to confirm positives"
    elif pos.state == "exists":
        verdict, note = "healthy", "negative and positive controls both behave as expected"
    elif pos.state == "absent":
        verdict, note = "stale_rule", f"known-good account '{p.known_good}' was reported absent: rule may be outdated"
    else:
        verdict, note = "positive_inconclusive", f"known-good account check was {pos.state}: {pos.detail}"
    return {"platform": p.name, "category": p.category, "verdict": verdict, "note": note,
            "negative": neg.state, "positive": pos.state if pos else None}
atlas/sources/wayback.py66 lines
"""
Internet Archive (Wayback Machine) capture history via its public CDX API (keyless).
Gives a domain's age/continuity signal: first capture, latest capture, months with captures.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from ..utils.helpers import safe_request, timestamp

CDX = "https://web.archive.org/cdx/search/cdx"
_TS = re.compile(r"^\d{14}$")


def _rows(url: str, result: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[List[List[str]]]:
    resp, err = safe_request(url, timeout=20, retries=1)
    if resp is None:
        result["errors"].append(err or "no response")
        return None
    if resp.status_code != 200:
        result["errors"].append(f"HTTP {resp.status_code}")
        return None
    text = resp.text.strip()
    if not text:
        return []
    try:
        data = resp.json()
    except ValueError:
        result["errors"].append("invalid JSON")
        return None
    return [r for r in data[1:] if isinstance(r, list) and r] if isinstance(data, list) else None


def _fmt(ts: str) -> str:
    return f"{ts[:4]}-{ts[4:6]}-{ts[6:8]}"


def _snap(ts: str, domain: str) -> str:
    return f"https://web.archive.org/web/{ts}/https://{domain}/"


def query_wayback(domain: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    result: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "queried_at": timestamp(), "domain": domain, "success": False, "source": "web.archive.org (CDX API)",
        "captures_found": False, "first_capture": None, "last_capture": None, "months_with_captures": 0,
        "first_snapshot_url": None, "last_snapshot_url": None, "errors": [],
        "note": "Archive captures show that a site existed at a time; they do not show who operated it.",
    }
    base = f"{CDX}?url={domain}&output=json&fl=timestamp"
    first = _rows(base + "&limit=1", result)
    if first is None:
        return result
    result["success"] = True
    if not first or not _TS.match(str(first[0][0])):
        return result                                          # reachable, but nothing archived
    last = _rows(base + "&limit=-1", result)
    monthly = _rows(base + "&collapse=timestamp:6&limit=3000", result)
    f_ts = str(first[0][0])
    l_ts = str(last[0][0]) if last and _TS.match(str(last[0][0])) else f_ts
    result.update(captures_found=True, first_capture=_fmt(f_ts), last_capture=_fmt(l_ts),
                  months_with_captures=len(monthly) if monthly else 0,
                  first_snapshot_url=_snap(f_ts, domain), last_snapshot_url=_snap(l_ts, domain))
    return result
atlas/sources/web_pages.py49 lines
"""
Fetch + parse ONE public web page (robots.txt honoured, SSRF-safe, size-capped).
Returns metadata only (title, headings, meta tags, JSON-LD); results are cached per investigation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Dict

from ..utils.helpers import safe_request, timestamp
from . import page_metadata as pm

_cache: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}


def clear_cache() -> None:
    _cache.clear()


def fetch_page(url: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    if url not in _cache:
        _cache[url] = _fetch(url)
    return _cache[url]


def _fetch(url: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    out: Dict[str, Any] = {"page_url": url, "accessed_at": timestamp(), "ok": False, "page_title": None,
                           "site_name": None, "jsonld": [], "doc": {}, "errors": []}
    resp, err = safe_request(url, timeout=12, respect_robots=True)
    if resp is None:
        out["errors"].append(err or "no response")
        return out
    out["final_url"] = resp.url or url
    if resp.status_code != 200:
        out["errors"].append(f"HTTP {resp.status_code}")
        return out
    ctype = resp.header("Content-Type").split(";")[0].strip().lower()
    if "html" not in ctype:
        out["errors"].append(f"content type {ctype or 'unknown'!r} is not HTML")
        return out
    doc = pm.extract(resp.text, out["final_url"])
    out["ok"] = True
    out["page_title"] = pm.meta_first(doc["meta"], "og:title", "twitter:title") or doc["title"] or None
    out["site_name"] = pm.meta_first(doc["meta"], "og:site_name") or None
    out["jsonld"] = doc["jsonld"]
    out["doc"] = {"title": doc["title"], "h1": doc["h1"],
                  "meta": {k: v for k, v in doc["meta"].items() if k.startswith(("og:", "twitter:", "description"))}}
    return out
atlas/sources/web_presence.py93 lines
"""
Lightweight public web presence checks.
Only retrieves publicly available information (headers, title, basic tech
hints). Single request, size-capped, no crawling, public hosts only.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import html
import logging
import re
from typing import Any, Dict

from . import page_metadata as pm

from ..utils.helpers import safe_request, timestamp

logger = logging.getLogger("atlas.sources.web")

_TITLE_RE = re.compile(r"<title[^>]*>(.*?)</title>", re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)


def basic_web_info(host_or_url: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Fetch basic public information about a website: final URL, status code,
    Server header, page title and simple technology hints from headers.
    """
    result: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "queried_at": timestamp(), "target": host_or_url, "success": False,
        "url_tried": None, "final_url": None, "status_code": None,
        "headers": {}, "title": None, "server": None,
        "technology_hints": [], "security_headers": {}, "errors": [],
        "site_name": None, "h1": None,
    }

    if host_or_url.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
        candidates = [host_or_url]
    else:
        candidates = [f"https://{host_or_url}", f"http://{host_or_url}"]

    for url in candidates:
        result["url_tried"] = url
        resp, err = safe_request(url, timeout=12, respect_robots=True)
        if err or resp is None:
            result["errors"].append(f"{url}: {err}")
            continue

        result["status_code"] = resp.status_code
        result["final_url"] = resp.url or url

        h = {k.lower(): v for k, v in (resp.headers or {}).items()}
        result["headers"] = dict(list(h.items())[:20])
        result["server"] = h.get("server")

        hints = []
        if "x-powered-by" in h:
            hints.append(f"X-Powered-By: {h['x-powered-by']}")
        if "server" in h:
            hints.append(f"Server: {h['server']}")
        if "cf-ray" in h or "cf-cache-status" in h:
            hints.append("Cloudflare detected")
        if any(k.startswith("x-shopify") for k in h) or "shopify" in h.get("server", "").lower():
            hints.append("Possible Shopify")
        if "x-generator" in h:
            hints.append(f"Generator: {h['x-generator']}")
        result["technology_hints"] = hints

        # Presence of common hardening headers (observation only).
        result["security_headers"] = {
            name: (name in h)
            for name in (
                "strict-transport-security", "content-security-policy",
                "x-content-type-options", "x-frame-options", "referrer-policy",
            )
        }

        content_type = h.get("content-type", "")
        if "html" in content_type.lower() or not content_type:
            m = _TITLE_RE.search(resp.text)
            if m:
                title = " ".join(html.unescape(m.group(1)).split())
                result["title"] = title[:200] or None
            doc = pm.extract(resp.text, result["final_url"])        # names used to verify what the site is
            result["site_name"] = (pm.meta_first(doc["meta"], "og:site_name", "application-name") or None)
            result["h1"] = (doc["h1"][0] if doc["h1"] else None)
            if result["site_name"]:
                result["site_name"] = result["site_name"][:120]

        result["success"] = True
        break

    return result
atlas/sources/wikidata.py115 lines
"""
Wikidata lookups (public, keyless MediaWiki API) for named public figures and organisations.

Purpose: (1) tell people/organisations with the same name apart (disambiguation) and
(2) obtain a community-recorded official website, occupation and employer.
Only professional/public facts are read: label, description, official website, occupation,
employer and the Wikipedia link. Birth dates, family, residence and other personal
properties are deliberately never requested or returned.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
from urllib.parse import quote

from ..utils.helpers import safe_request, timestamp
from ..utils.network_safety import validate_url

API = "https://www.wikidata.org/w/api.php"
HUMAN = "Q5"
MAX_CANDIDATES = 5


def _norm(s: str) -> str:
    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]", "", (s or "").lower())


def _get(url: str, result: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    resp, err = safe_request(url, timeout=12, retries=1)
    if resp is None:
        result["errors"].append(err or "no response")
        return None
    if resp.status_code != 200:
        result["errors"].append(f"HTTP {resp.status_code}")
        return None
    try:
        data = resp.json()
    except ValueError:
        result["errors"].append("invalid JSON")
        return None
    return data if isinstance(data, dict) else None


def _claim_values(entity: Dict[str, Any], prop: str) -> List[Any]:
    out = []
    for c in (entity.get("claims") or {}).get(prop, [])[:6]:
        try:
            out.append(c["mainsnak"]["datavalue"]["value"])
        except (KeyError, TypeError):
            continue
    return out


def _qids(values: List[Any]) -> List[str]:
    return [v["id"] for v in values if isinstance(v, dict) and isinstance(v.get("id"), str)]


def lookup(name: str, kind: str = "person") -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    kind: 'person' or 'org'. Returns {found, ambiguous, selected, candidates, errors, ...}.
    `selected` is set ONLY when exactly one exact-label candidate of the right kind exists.
    """
    result: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "queried_at": timestamp(), "source": "Wikidata (public API)", "kind": kind,
        "found": False, "ambiguous": False, "selected": None, "candidates": [], "errors": [],
    }
    q = quote(name.strip(), safe="")
    data = _get(f"{API}?action=wbsearchentities&search={q}&language=en&uselang=en&type=item&limit=10&format=json", result)
    if not data:
        return result
    hits = [h for h in data.get("search", []) if isinstance(h, dict) and h.get("id")]
    exact = [h for h in hits if _norm(h.get("label", "")) == _norm(name) or
             _norm((h.get("match") or {}).get("text", "")) == _norm(name)]
    chosen = (exact or hits)[:MAX_CANDIDATES]
    if not chosen:
        return result

    ids = "|".join(h["id"] for h in chosen)
    ent = _get(f"{API}?action=wbgetentities&ids={ids}&props=labels|descriptions|claims|sitelinks"
               f"&languages=en&sitefilter=enwiki&format=json", result)
    entities = (ent or {}).get("entities", {})
    # second call: human-readable labels for occupation / employer items
    label_ids = set()
    for h in chosen:
        e = entities.get(h["id"], {})
        label_ids.update(_qids(_claim_values(e, "P106")) + _qids(_claim_values(e, "P108")))
    labels: Dict[str, str] = {}
    if label_ids:
        lab = _get(f"{API}?action=wbgetentities&ids={'|'.join(sorted(label_ids)[:40])}&props=labels&languages=en&format=json", result)
        for qid, e in ((lab or {}).get("entities") or {}).items():
            labels[qid] = ((e.get("labels") or {}).get("en") or {}).get("value", "")

    for h in chosen:
        e = entities.get(h["id"], {})
        is_human = HUMAN in _qids(_claim_values(e, "P31"))
        site = next((v for v in _claim_values(e, "P856") if isinstance(v, str) and validate_url(v)[0]), None)
        title = ((e.get("sitelinks") or {}).get("enwiki") or {}).get("title")
        result["candidates"].append({
            "qid": h["id"], "label": (h.get("label") or "")[:120], "description": (h.get("description") or "")[:200],
            "exact_label": h in exact, "is_human": is_human, "official_website": site,
            "occupations": [labels[q] for q in _qids(_claim_values(e, "P106")) if labels.get(q)][:5],
            "employers": [labels[q] for q in _qids(_claim_values(e, "P108")) if labels.get(q)][:5],
            "wikipedia_url": f"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/{quote(title.replace(' ', '_'))}" if title else None,
            "url": f"https://www.wikidata.org/wiki/{h['id']}",
        })

    pool = [c for c in result["candidates"] if c["exact_label"] and (c["is_human"] if kind == "person" else not c["is_human"])]
    result["found"] = bool(pool)
    if len(pool) == 1:
        result["selected"] = pool[0]
    elif len(pool) > 1:
        result["ambiguous"] = True
    return result
atlas/sources/wikimedia.py76 lines
"""
Wikipedia / Wikimedia Commons lookups (public, keyless, community-edited).

Used only for named public figures. Returns a short summary and the page URL.
This is a public MediaWiki endpoint, not a paid or key-based API.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import html
import re
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional
from urllib.parse import quote, unquote, urlparse

from ..utils.helpers import safe_request, timestamp
from .page_metadata import tokens

SUMMARY_URL = "https://en.wikipedia.org/api/rest_v1/page/summary/{title}"
_TAG_RE = re.compile(r"<[^>]+>")


def _clean(value: Any, limit: int = 300) -> str:
    if not isinstance(value, str):
        return ""
    return " ".join(html.unescape(_TAG_RE.sub(" ", value)).split())[:limit]


def _title_matches(requested: str, returned: str) -> bool:
    returned = re.sub(r"\s*\(.*?\)\s*", " ", returned or "")
    return tokens(requested) == tokens(returned)


def lookup_person(name: str, with_licence: bool = False) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Look up a Wikipedia summary for *name*.
    Result keys: found, ambiguous, name_match, title, description, extract, page_url,
    image {url, thumbnail, licence...}, errors, queried_at.
    """
    result: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "queried_at": timestamp(), "source": "Wikipedia (en) public REST API",
        "found": False, "ambiguous": False, "name_match": False, "errors": [],
    }
    title = quote(name.strip().replace(" ", "_"), safe="")
    resp, err = safe_request(SUMMARY_URL.format(title=title), timeout=10)
    if resp is None:
        result["errors"].append(err or "no response")
        return result
    if resp.status_code == 404:
        result["errors"].append("No Wikipedia article with this exact title.")
        return result
    if resp.status_code != 200:
        result["errors"].append(f"HTTP {resp.status_code}")
        return result
    try:
        data = resp.json()
    except ValueError as e:
        result["errors"].append(f"JSON error: {e}")
        return result

    if data.get("type") == "disambiguation":
        result["ambiguous"] = True
        result["page_url"] = ((data.get("content_urls") or {}).get("desktop") or {}).get("page")
        return result

    result["found"] = True
    result["title"] = _clean(data.get("title"), 150)
    result["name_match"] = _title_matches(name, data.get("title", ""))
    result["description"] = _clean(data.get("description"), 200)
    result["extract"] = _clean(data.get("extract"), 900)
    result["page_url"] = ((data.get("content_urls") or {}).get("desktop") or {}).get("page")

    if not result["name_match"]:
        return result      # redirected to a differently-named article: do not use its image

    return result
atlas/ui.py229 lines
"""
Terminal user interface for ATLAS.

Standard library only. Colour uses plain ANSI codes that ATLAS adds itself, only on a colour-capable
terminal (honours NO_COLOR). ALL printed text is stripped of control characters BEFORE any styling
is applied, which prevents terminal escape-sequence injection from remote page content.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import re
import shlex
import sys
import time
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple


_SGR = {"bold": "1", "dim": "2", "red": "31", "green": "32", "cyan": "36", "bold cyan": "1;36", "bold red": "1;31"}


def _color_enabled() -> bool:
    if os.environ.get("NO_COLOR") or os.environ.get("ATLAS_PLAIN") or not sys.stdout.isatty():
        return False
    if os.name == "nt":                # only terminals known to understand ANSI
        return bool(os.environ.get("WT_SESSION") or os.environ.get("ANSICON") or os.environ.get("TERM"))
    return os.environ.get("TERM", "") not in ("", "dumb")


_CTRL = re.compile(r"[\x00-\x08\x0b-\x1f\x7f-\x9f]")

BANNER = r"""
    ___   ________  ___    ___   _____
   /   | /_  __/ / / / |  / / | / ___/
  / /| |  / / / / / /| | / /| | \__ \
 / ___ | / / / /_/ / | |/ / | |___/ /
/_/  |_|/_/  \____/  |___/  |_/____/

  ATLAS – Passive OSINT Intelligence Agent
  Ethical • Transparent • Source-aware
"""

HELP = """
Commands
--------
  investigate <target> [--type T] [--source URL ...]
                         Full investigation (IP, domain, URL, organisation, person, username)
  username <name> [--stop-at-first] [--platforms FILE]
                         Which public platforms have an account with this exact username
                         (also: @name, or a bare handle such as john_smith99)
  selftest [--platforms FILE]
                         Health-check the username platform rules (finds stale/unreliable ones)
  report                 Show the last report again
  export [html]          Export last report (JSON + TXT, optionally HTML)
  reports list | delete <name|all> | purge <days>
                         Manage locally stored reports
  offline on|off         Disable/enable all network access
  help, exit

Notes
-----
* Only public, passive sources; robots.txt is honoured; private/internal addresses are never contacted.
* --source <URL>: an official/public page you supply (e.g. a biography page or a company's real website).
* Organisations and public figures are looked up in Wikidata/Wikipedia, certificate-transparency logs and the
  Internet Archive; domains also get web history and related-domain discovery.
* IP geolocation is an approximate NETWORK location, never a person's address.
* Username results show that a public profile EXISTS, not who owns it: the same username is often used by different people.
"""


def sanitize(text: Any) -> str:
    """Remove control characters (incl. ESC) from untrusted text."""
    return _CTRL.sub("", "" if text is None else str(text))


def emit(text: str = "", style: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
    text = sanitize(text)                 # ALWAYS strip control characters (terminal escape injection)
    code = _SGR.get(style or "")
    print(f"\x1b[{code}m{text}\x1b[0m" if code and _color_enabled() else text)


def link(url: Optional[str]) -> str:
    """
    Clickable URL for the terminal. Deliberately a plain, sanitised URL: virtually every terminal
    makes plain http(s) URLs clickable, and emitting escape-sequence hyperlinks would require
    letting control characters through the sanitiser.
    """
    return sanitize(url)


def print_banner() -> None:
    emit(BANNER, "bold cyan")
    emit("Passive research only. No unauthorised access. No private data collection.\n", "dim")


def print_help() -> None:
    emit(HELP)


def progress_printer(message: str) -> None:
    emit(f"  → {message}", "cyan")


def print_error(msg: str) -> None:
    emit(f"Error: {msg}", "bold red")


def print_info(msg: str) -> None:
    emit(msg, "bold")


def print_report_text(text: str) -> None:
    emit(text)


def show_classification(c: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    emit("\n── Input classification ──", "bold")
    for k in ("original_input", "normalised", "type", "confidence", "notes"):
        emit(f"  {k.replace('_', ' '):<15}: {c.get(k)}")


def show_summary(report: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    emit("\n=== Investigation Complete ===", "bold cyan")
    for f in report.get("key_findings") or []:
        emit(f"  • {sanitize(f.get('finding'))}  [{f.get('confidence')}]")
    conf = report.get("confidence_summary") or {}
    for k, v in conf.items():
        if isinstance(v, dict):
            emit(f"  {k}: {v.get('level')} – {sanitize(v.get('note'))}")
    show_accounts(report)
    for lim in (report.get("limitations") or [])[:4]:
        emit(f"  limitation: {sanitize(lim)}", "dim")
    export = report.get("export") or {}
    for kind, path in export.items():
        emit(f"{kind.upper():<8}: {path}", "green")
    if export:
        emit("Type 'report' to see the full report.", "dim")


def show_accounts(report: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    acc = report.get("accounts")
    if acc is None:
        return
    summ = (report.get("sections") or {}).get("scan_summary") or {}
    emit("\n── Username presence ──", "bold")
    found = acc.get("found", [])
    if found:
        for a in found:
            emit(f"  ✔ {sanitize(a['platform']):<28} {link(a['profile_url'])}", "green")
            emit(f"      {a['category']}; existence {a['existence_confidence']} ({sanitize(a['evidence'])}; control check {a['control_check']})", "dim")
    elif acc.get("status") == "offline":
        emit("  Offline mode – no platforms were queried, so nothing can be said about this username.")
    else:
        emit("  No matches found on the platforms ATLAS could check.")
    if summ:
        emit(f"  checked {summ.get('checked')} of {summ.get('platforms_in_list')} platforms · not found {summ.get('not_found')} · "
             f"skipped {summ.get('skipped')} · inconclusive {summ.get('inconclusive_or_error')} · unreliable {summ.get('unreliable_platforms')}", "dim")
    emit("  Same username ≠ same person: results are leads to verify, never identification.", "bold")
    hints = report.get("search_hints") or []
    if hints:
        emit("  Platforms that block automated access (open manually if you have a legitimate need):", "dim")
        for h in hints:
            emit(f"    {sanitize(h['description']).split(' (')[0]:<14} {link(h['url'])}", "dim")


_VERDICT = {"healthy": "OK", "ok_negative_only": "ok*", "stale_rule": "STALE?", "unreliable": "UNRELIABLE",
            "blocked_by_robots": "robots", "unreachable_or_blocked": "blocked", "positive_inconclusive": "?", "error": "error"}


def show_selftest(res: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    emit("\n── Platform rule health check ──", "bold")
    if res.get("status") == "offline":
        emit("  Offline mode – nothing was checked."); return
    for w in res.get("warnings", []):
        emit(f"  warning: {w}", "red")
    for r in res["results"]:
        emit(f"  {_VERDICT.get(r['verdict'], r['verdict']):<11} {sanitize(r['platform']):<30} {sanitize(r['note'])}",
             "green" if r["verdict"] == "healthy" else "dim" if r["verdict"].startswith("ok") else "red")
    emit("  Summary: " + ", ".join(f"{k} {v}" for k, v in sorted(res["summary"].items())))
    for rec in res["recommendations"]:
        emit(f"  ! {sanitize(rec)}", "red")
    emit("  ok* = negative control passed but no known-good account exists to confirm positive matches.", "dim")


def show_reports(rows: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
    if not rows:
        emit("  No stored reports.")
    for r in rows:
        when = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M", time.localtime(r["modified"]))
        emit(f"  {r['name']}   {when}   {r['bytes']} bytes   ({', '.join(r['files'])})")


def ask_confirm(prompt: str) -> bool:
    """Explicit user permission. Refuses automatically when not interactive."""
    if not sys.stdin.isatty():
        emit("  Cannot ask for permission (non-interactive session); action refused.", "red")
        return False
    try:
        return input(f"  {prompt} [y/N] ").strip().lower() in ("y", "yes")
    except (EOFError, KeyboardInterrupt):
        return False


def split_command(line: str) -> List[str]:
    try:
        return shlex.split(line)
    except ValueError:
        return line.split()


def parse_target_args(tokens: List[str]) -> Tuple[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Parse '<target words> [--type T] [--source URL]... [--platforms FILE]' from interactive input."""
    words, opts = [], {"type": None, "sources": [], "stop_at_first": False, "platforms": None}
    it = iter(tokens)
    for tok in it:
        if tok == "--type":
            opts["type"] = next(it, None)
        elif tok == "--source":
            val = next(it, None)
            if val:
                opts["sources"].append(val)
        elif tok == "--platforms":
            opts["platforms"] = next(it, None)
        elif tok == "--stop-at-first":
            opts["stop_at_first"] = True
        else:
            words.append(tok)
    return " ".join(words), opts
