atlas/utils/helpers.py103 lines
"""
Utility helpers for ATLAS (validation, timestamps, progress).

Network functionality lives in :mod:`atlas.utils.network_safety`; the names are
re-exported here for backwards compatibility.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import ipaddress
import logging
import re
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Callable, Optional
from urllib.parse import urlparse

logger = logging.getLogger("atlas.utils")



# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Small generic helpers
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

def timestamp() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S UTC")


def confidence_label(score: float) -> str:
    """Map 0.0–1.0 score to human label."""
    if score >= 0.85:
        return "HIGH"
    if score >= 0.60:
        return "MEDIUM"
    if score >= 0.35:
        return "LOW"
    return "VERY LOW / UNVERIFIED"


def make_progress(callback: Optional[Callable[[str], None]], log: logging.Logger) -> Callable[[str], None]:
    """Build the progress function every module uses (callback + debug log)."""
    def progress(msg: str) -> None:
        if callback:
            callback(msg)
        log.debug(msg)
    return progress


def clean_target(value: str) -> str:
    """Trim whitespace and one layer of matching surrounding quotes."""
    value = (value or "").strip()
    if len(value) >= 2 and value[0] == value[-1] and value[0] in "\"'“”‘’":
        value = value[1:-1].strip()
    return value


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Validation
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

def is_valid_ipv4(value: str) -> bool:
    try:
        return isinstance(ipaddress.ip_address(value.strip()), ipaddress.IPv4Address)
    except ValueError:
        return False


def is_valid_ipv6(value: str) -> bool:
    try:
        return isinstance(ipaddress.ip_address(value.strip().strip("[]")), ipaddress.IPv6Address)
    except ValueError:
        return False


_DOMAIN_RE = re.compile(
    r"^(?:[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,61}[a-z0-9])?\.)+[a-z]{2,63}$", re.IGNORECASE
)


def is_valid_domain(value: str) -> bool:
    """Basic domain validation (not exhaustive)."""
    value = value.strip().lower().rstrip(".")
    if value.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
        return False
    if not 1 <= len(value) <= 253:
        return False
    return bool(_DOMAIN_RE.match(value))


def is_valid_url(value: str) -> bool:
    try:
        result = urlparse(value.strip())
        return result.scheme in ("http", "https") and bool(result.netloc)
    except ValueError:
        return False

    SimpleResponse, USER_AGENT, host_is_public, is_public_ip, is_offline,
    rate_limit, resolve_hostname, safe_request, set_offline,
)

"""
Safe HTML rendering helpers (standard library only).

Generated pages contain NO JavaScript, set a restrictive Content-Security-Policy,
escape every dynamic value, only emit http(s) URLs, and add rel/referrer protections.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import html
import os
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional

from .network_safety import is_safe_public_url

CSP = ("default-src 'none'; img-src https: http:; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; "
       "base-uri 'none'; form-action 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'")

BASE_CSS = """
:root{color-scheme:light dark;--bg:#fff;--fg:#1b1f24;--mut:#59636e;--card:#f6f8fa;--bd:#d0d7de;--acc:#0969da;--ok:#1a7f37;--warn:#9a6700;--bad:#cf222e}
@media (prefers-color-scheme:dark){:root{--bg:#0d1117;--fg:#e6edf3;--mut:#8d96a0;--card:#161b22;--bd:#30363d;--acc:#58a6ff;--ok:#3fb950;--warn:#d29922;--bad:#f85149}}
body{font:15px/1.55 system-ui,-apple-system,Segoe UI,sans-serif;background:var(--bg);color:var(--fg);max-width:980px;margin:2rem auto;padding:0 1rem}
h1{font-size:1.6rem}h2{font-size:1.15rem;border-bottom:1px solid var(--bd);padding-bottom:.3rem;margin-top:2rem}
a{color:var(--acc);word-break:break-all}table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%}td,th{border:1px solid var(--bd);padding:.35rem .6rem;vertical-align:top;text-align:left}
th{background:var(--card);width:28%}pre{background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--bd);padding:.7rem;overflow:auto;white-space:pre-wrap}
.muted{color:var(--mut)}.badge{display:inline-block;border:1px solid var(--bd);border-radius:1rem;padding:0 .6rem;font-size:.8rem}
.verified{color:var(--ok)}.probable{color:var(--warn)}.unverified{color:var(--bad)}
.notice{border-left:4px solid var(--warn);background:var(--card);padding:.5rem .8rem;margin:1rem 0}
"""


def esc(value: Any) -> str:
    return html.escape("" if value is None else str(value), quote=True)


def safe_link(url: Optional[str], text: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
    """An <a> tag for http(s) URLs; plain escaped text for anything else."""
    if url and is_safe_public_url(str(url)):
        return (f'<a href="{esc(url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow">'
                f"{esc(text or url)}</a>")
    return esc(text or url or "")


def page(title: str, body: str) -> str:
    return (f'<!doctype html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8">'
            f'<meta http-equiv="Content-Security-Policy" content="{CSP}">'
            f'<meta name="referrer" content="no-referrer">'
            f'<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">'
            f"<title>{esc(title)}</title><style>{BASE_CSS}</style></head><body>{body}</body></html>")


def write_private(path: str, text: str) -> None:
    """Write a file readable only by the current user (best effort on Windows)."""
    fd = os.open(path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC, 0o600)
    with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(text)
atlas/utils/network_safety.py419 lines
"""
Network safety layer for ATLAS (standard library only).

Every outbound request in ATLAS goes through :func:`safe_request`, which:

* only allows http/https URLs without embedded credentials, on a small set of ports
* resolves the host itself, refuses non-public addresses (SSRF protection) and then
  connects to the *validated IP* (defeats DNS-rebinding between check and use)
* re-validates every redirect hop (max 5)
* optionally honours robots.txt (conservatively: if it cannot be read, it is treated
  as "no automated access")
* applies per-host rate limiting, timeouts, a hard body-size cap and a total deadline
* can be switched off entirely with offline mode
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import http.client
import ipaddress
import logging
import socket
import ssl
import threading
import time
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from urllib import robotparser
from urllib.parse import urljoin, urlparse

logger = logging.getLogger("atlas.net")

_sleep = time.sleep                 # indirection so tests can run without real delays
RETRY_STATUSES = (429, 502, 503, 504)
MAX_RETRY_AFTER = 5.0               # never wait (or retry) past this: a longer Retry-After is respected, not evaded

USER_AGENT = "ATLAS-OSINT/2.0 (Passive Research Agent)"
ROBOTS_TOKEN = "ATLAS-OSINT"
DEFAULT_DELAY = 1.2            # seconds between requests to the same host
MAX_BODY_BYTES = 1_000_000     # default cap on any response body
MAX_REDIRECTS = 5
TOTAL_DEADLINE = 30.0          # seconds, whole request incl. body read
ALLOWED_PORTS = {80, 443, 8080, 8443}
MAX_URL_LENGTH = 2048

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Offline mode
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
_offline = False


def set_offline(value: bool) -> None:
    global _offline
    _offline = bool(value)


def is_offline() -> bool:
    return _offline


OFFLINE_MESSAGE = "offline mode: network access disabled"


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# IP classification
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
_CGNAT = ipaddress.ip_network("100.64.0.0/10")
_DOC_NETS = [ipaddress.ip_network(n) for n in
             ("192.0.2.0/24", "198.51.100.0/24", "203.0.113.0/24", "2001:db8::/32")]


def parse_ip(value: str):
    """Parse an IP (unwrapping IPv4-mapped IPv6 and zone ids). Raises ValueError."""
    addr = ipaddress.ip_address(value.strip().strip("[]").split("%")[0])
    if isinstance(addr, ipaddress.IPv6Address) and addr.ipv4_mapped:
        addr = addr.ipv4_mapped
    return addr


def ip_category(value: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Classify an IP address.
    Returns {"category", "is_global", "explanation"} – category is one of:
    public, private, loopback, link-local, multicast, unspecified, documentation,
    shared-address-space, reserved.
    """
    addr = parse_ip(value)
    if addr.is_unspecified:
        cat, why = "unspecified", "Unspecified address (0.0.0.0 / ::)."
    elif addr.is_loopback:
        cat, why = "loopback", "Loopback address – refers to the local machine only."
    elif addr.is_link_local:
        cat, why = "link-local", "Link-local address – only valid on the local network segment."
    elif addr.is_multicast:
        cat, why = "multicast", "Multicast address – not assigned to a single host."
    elif any(addr in n for n in _DOC_NETS if n.version == addr.version):
        cat, why = "documentation", "Reserved for documentation/examples; never routed on the internet."
    elif addr.version == 4 and addr in _CGNAT:
        cat, why = "shared-address-space", "Carrier-grade NAT range (100.64.0.0/10); not globally routable."
    elif addr.is_reserved:
        cat, why = "reserved", "Reserved / special-use address; not globally routable."
    elif addr.is_private:
        cat, why = "private", "Private-use address (RFC 1918 / ULA)."
    elif addr.is_global:
        cat, why = "public", "Publicly routable address."
    else:
        cat, why = "reserved", "Reserved / special-use address; not globally routable."
    return {"category": cat, "is_global": cat == "public", "explanation": why,
            "ip_version": addr.version, "normalised": str(addr)}


def is_public_ip(ip: str) -> bool:
    try:
        return ip_category(ip)["is_global"]
    except ValueError:
        return False


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Resolution
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

def resolve_hostname(hostname: str) -> List[str]:
    """Resolve hostname to IP addresses (empty list in offline mode / on failure)."""
    if _offline:
        return []
    results: List[str] = []
    try:
        for info in socket.getaddrinfo(hostname, None, type=socket.SOCK_STREAM):
            ip = info[4][0]
            if ip not in results:
                results.append(ip)
    except (socket.gaierror, UnicodeError, OSError):
        pass
    return results


def resolve_public(host: str) -> Tuple[List[str], str]:
    """
    Resolve *host* and require that EVERY address is globally routable.
    Returns (ips, error); error is "" on success.
    """
    host = host.strip("[]")
    try:
        ips = [str(parse_ip(host))]
    except ValueError:
        ips = resolve_hostname(host)
    if not ips:
        return [], "host did not resolve"
    for ip in ips:
        if not is_public_ip(ip):
            return [], f"resolves to non-public address {ip}"
    return ips, ""


def host_is_public(host: str) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    ips, err = resolve_public(host)
    return (not err), err


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# URL validation
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

def validate_url(url: str) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    """Static checks only (no DNS): scheme, credentials, port, length, control chars."""
    if not isinstance(url, str) or not url:
        return False, "empty URL"
    if len(url) > MAX_URL_LENGTH:
        return False, "URL too long"
    if any(ord(c) < 33 or ord(c) == 127 for c in url):
        return False, "URL contains whitespace/control characters"
    try:
        p = urlparse(url)
        port = p.port
    except ValueError:
        return False, "malformed URL"
    if p.scheme not in ("http", "https"):
        return False, f"unsupported scheme {p.scheme!r} (only http/https)"
    if not p.hostname:
        return False, "URL has no host"
    if p.username or p.password:
        return False, "URLs with embedded credentials are refused"
    if port is not None and port not in ALLOWED_PORTS:
        return False, f"port {port} not allowed"
    return True, ""


def is_safe_public_url(url: str) -> bool:
    """Static validation only – used for links/images rendered in reports."""
    ok, _ = validate_url(url)
    return ok


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Rate limiting
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
_rate_lock = threading.Lock()
_last_request: Dict[str, float] = {}


def rate_limit(host: str, delay: float = DEFAULT_DELAY) -> None:
    """Thread-safe per-host rate limiting."""
    with _rate_lock:
        now = time.monotonic()
        wait = delay - (now - _last_request.get(host, -1e9))
        _last_request[host] = now + max(wait, 0.0)
    if wait > 0:
        time.sleep(wait)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# HTTP
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

class SimpleResponse:
    def __init__(self, status_code: int, headers: Dict[str, str], url: str, content: bytes):
        self.status_code = status_code
        self.headers = headers
        self.url = url
        self.content = content
        self.truncated = False

    @property
    def text(self) -> str:
        return self.content.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")

    def json(self) -> Any:
        import json
        return json.loads(self.text)

    def header(self, name: str, default: str = "") -> str:
        for k, v in self.headers.items():
            if k.lower() == name.lower():
                return v
        return default


class _PinnedHTTPConnection(http.client.HTTPConnection):
    def __init__(self, host, port, pin_ip, timeout):
        super().__init__(host, port, timeout=timeout)
        self._pin_ip = pin_ip

    def connect(self):
        self.sock = socket.create_connection((self._pin_ip, self.port), self.timeout)


class _PinnedHTTPSConnection(http.client.HTTPSConnection):
    def __init__(self, host, port, pin_ip, timeout):
        super().__init__(host, port, timeout=timeout, context=ssl.create_default_context())
        self._pin_ip = pin_ip

    def connect(self):
        sock = socket.create_connection((self._pin_ip, self.port), self.timeout)
        self.sock = self._context.wrap_socket(sock, server_hostname=self.host)


def _single_request(url: str, method: str, headers: Dict[str, str], timeout: float,
                    ips: List[str], max_bytes: int, read_body: bool) -> SimpleResponse:
    """One HTTP(S) request to a pinned, pre-validated IP. No redirect following."""
    p = urlparse(url)
    https = p.scheme == "https"
    port = p.port or (443 if https else 80)
    path = (p.path or "/") + (f"?{p.query}" if p.query else "")
    cls = _PinnedHTTPSConnection if https else _PinnedHTTPConnection
    last_err: Optional[Exception] = None
    for ip in ips:
        conn = cls(p.hostname, port, ip, timeout)
        try:
            conn.request(method, path, headers={**headers, "Connection": "close"})
            r = conn.getresponse()
            hdrs = {k.lower(): v for k, v in r.getheaders()}
            body = b""
            truncated = False
            if read_body and method != "HEAD":
                deadline = time.monotonic() + TOTAL_DEADLINE
                while True:
                    chunk = r.read(16384)
                    if not chunk:
                        break
                    body += chunk
                    if len(body) > max_bytes:
                        body, truncated = body[:max_bytes], True
                        break
                    if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                        truncated = True
                        break
            resp = SimpleResponse(r.status, hdrs, url, body)
            resp.truncated = truncated
            return resp
        except Exception as e:            # try the next validated IP
            last_err = e
        finally:
            try:
                conn.close()
            except Exception:
                pass
    raise last_err or OSError("no addresses to connect to")


# robots.txt cache: host-key -> RobotFileParser | None(disallow all)
_robots_cache: Dict[str, Optional[robotparser.RobotFileParser]] = {}
_robots_lock = threading.Lock()


def robots_allowed(url: str) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    """
    Check robots.txt for *url*. Conservative: 4xx => allowed (RFC 9309),
    5xx / unreachable / offline => NOT allowed (cannot verify permission).
    """
    p = urlparse(url)
    key = f"{p.scheme}://{p.netloc}"
    with _robots_lock:
        cached = key in _robots_cache
        rp = _robots_cache.get(key)
    if not cached:
        resp, err = safe_request(f"{key}/robots.txt", timeout=8, max_bytes=200_000,
                                 respect_robots=False)
        if resp is None:
            rp = None
        elif 200 <= resp.status_code < 300:
            rp = robotparser.RobotFileParser()
            rp.parse(resp.text.splitlines())
        elif 400 <= resp.status_code < 500:
            rp = robotparser.RobotFileParser()
            rp.parse([])                       # empty robots.txt => everything allowed
        else:
            rp = None
        with _robots_lock:
            _robots_cache[key] = rp
    if rp is None:
        return False, "robots.txt could not be verified; treated as no automated access"
    if rp.can_fetch(ROBOTS_TOKEN, url):
        return True, ""
    return False, "disallowed by robots.txt"


def _retry_delay(resp: "SimpleResponse") -> Optional[float]:
    """Seconds to wait before a retry, or None if the server asked for a longer pause than we allow."""
    raw = resp.header("Retry-After").strip()
    if raw.isdigit():
        secs = float(raw)
        return None if secs > MAX_RETRY_AFTER else secs
    return 1.5


def clear_robots_cache() -> None:
    with _robots_lock:
        _robots_cache.clear()


def safe_request(
    url: str,
    timeout: float = 12.0,
    headers: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None,
    method: str = "GET",
    allow_redirects: bool = True,
    max_bytes: int = MAX_BODY_BYTES,
    read_body: bool = True,
    respect_robots: bool = False,
    retries: int = 0,
) -> Tuple[Optional[SimpleResponse], Optional[str]]:
    """
    Safe, polite HTTP request. Returns (response_or_None, error_or_None).

    retries: how many times to repeat a request after a transient failure (connection error, or HTTP
    429/502/503/504). A Retry-After longer than MAX_RETRY_AFTER is honoured by NOT retrying.
    """
    if _offline:
        return None, OFFLINE_MESSAGE
    hdrs = {
        "User-Agent": USER_AGENT,
        "Accept": "application/rdap+json, application/json, text/html, */*",
        "Accept-Language": "en-GB,en;q=0.9",
    }
    if headers:
        hdrs.update(headers)

    current = url
    try:
        for _ in range(MAX_REDIRECTS + 1):
            ok, why = validate_url(current)
            if not ok:
                return None, f"refused URL: {why}"
            host = urlparse(current).hostname
            ips, err = resolve_public(host)
            if err:
                return None, f"refused to contact {host}: {err}"
            if respect_robots and method == "GET":
                allowed, why = robots_allowed(current)
                if not allowed:
                    return None, f"not fetched: {why}"
            attempt = 0
            while True:
                rate_limit(urlparse(current).netloc)
                try:
                    resp = _single_request(current, method, hdrs, timeout, ips, max_bytes, read_body)
                except Exception:
                    if attempt < retries:
                        attempt += 1
                        _sleep(1.0)
                        continue
                    raise
                if resp.status_code in RETRY_STATUSES and attempt < retries:
                    wait = _retry_delay(resp)
                    if wait is not None:
                        attempt += 1
                        _sleep(wait)
                        continue
                break
            location = resp.header("Location")
            if allow_redirects and resp.status_code in (301, 302, 303, 307, 308) and location:
                current = urljoin(current, location)
                continue
            resp.url = current
            return resp, None
        return None, f"too many redirects (> {MAX_REDIRECTS})"
    except Exception as e:
        logger.debug("Request failed for %s: %s", url, e)
        return None, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"
atlas/utils/retention.py70 lines
"""Local report retention controls: list, delete, purge. Path-traversal safe."""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import re
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Optional

_BASE_RE = re.compile(r"^atlas_[\w.\-]+_\d{8}T\d{6}Z$")
_EXTS = (".json", ".txt", ".html")


def _dir(output_dir: str) -> Path:
    return Path(output_dir).resolve()


def _files_for(base: str, d: Path) -> List[Path]:
    out = []
    for ext in _EXTS:
        p = (d / f"{base}{ext}").resolve()
        if p.parent == d and p.is_file():          # must live directly inside the reports dir
            out.append(p)
    return out


def list_reports(output_dir: str) -> List[Dict]:
    d = _dir(output_dir)
    if not d.is_dir():
        return []
    bases = set()
    for p in d.iterdir():
        name = p.name
        for ext in _EXTS:
            if name.endswith(ext) and _BASE_RE.match(name[: -len(ext)]):
                bases.add(name[: -len(ext)])
    rows = []
    for b in bases:
        files = _files_for(b, d)
        if files:
            rows.append({"name": b, "files": [f.name for f in files],
                         "modified": max(f.stat().st_mtime for f in files),
                         "bytes": sum(f.stat().st_size for f in files)})
    return sorted(rows, key=lambda r: r["modified"], reverse=True)


def delete_report(output_dir: str, name: str) -> int:
    """Delete one report set by base name (e.g. atlas_example.com_20260101T000000Z). Returns files removed."""
    name = name.strip()
    for ext in _EXTS:                              # tolerate a pasted filename
        if name.endswith(ext):
            name = name[: -len(ext)]
    if not _BASE_RE.match(name):
        raise ValueError("not a valid ATLAS report name")
    d = _dir(output_dir)
    files = _files_for(name, d)
    for f in files:
        f.unlink()
    return len(files)


def delete_all(output_dir: str) -> int:
    return sum(delete_report(output_dir, r["name"]) for r in list_reports(output_dir))


def purge_older_than(output_dir: str, days: float, now: Optional[float] = None) -> int:
    cutoff = (now or time.time()) - days * 86400
    return sum(delete_report(output_dir, r["name"]) for r in list_reports(output_dir) if r["modified"] < cutoff)
atlas/utils/source_reliability.py52 lines
"""Heuristic source-reliability ratings (transparent, deliberately conservative)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Dict, Tuple
from urllib.parse import urlparse

_OFFICIAL_SUFFIXES = (".gov.uk", ".gov", ".ac.uk", ".edu", ".nhs.uk", ".mod.uk", ".police.uk",
                      ".parliament.uk", ".sch.uk", ".europa.eu", ".int")
_COMMUNITY = ("wikipedia.org", "wikimedia.org", "wikidata.org")

# Non-URL sources used by ATLAS.
_NAMED: Dict[str, Tuple[str, str]] = {
    "rdap": ("HIGH", "Regional/registry-operated RDAP data (authoritative for registration, not for identity)."),
    "dns": ("HIGH", "Live DNS answers (authoritative for what the DNS currently says)."),
    "crt.sh": ("MEDIUM", "Public Certificate Transparency logs via crt.sh; complete for logged certs, not for all hosts."),
    "ipinfo.io": ("LOW", "Commercial IP-geolocation estimate; network location only, often inaccurate below country level."),
    "ip-api.com": ("LOW", "Free IP-geolocation estimate; network location only, often inaccurate below country level."),
    "spamhaus": ("MEDIUM", "Widely used DNS blocklist; an indicator, not proof."),
    "spamcop": ("MEDIUM", "Widely used DNS blocklist; an indicator, not proof."),
    "heuristic": ("LOW", "Automated guess; requires human verification."),
}


def rate_domain(host: str) -> Tuple[str, str]:
    host = (host or "").lower().rstrip(".")
    if host.endswith(_OFFICIAL_SUFFIXES):
        return "HIGH", "Government / academic / institutional domain; likely an official source."
    if any(host == d or host.endswith("." + d) for d in _COMMUNITY):
        return "MEDIUM", "Community-edited reference; generally reliable for notable subjects but not authoritative."
    return "LOW", "Domain ownership/authority not independently verified by ATLAS."


def rate_source(source: str) -> Tuple[str, str]:
    """Rate a URL or a named data source."""
    s = (source or "").strip()
    if s.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
        host = urlparse(s).hostname or ""
        for key in ("rdap", "crt.sh"):
            if key in host:
                return _NAMED[key if key != "rdap" else "rdap"]
        if host.endswith("iana.org") or host.startswith("rdap."):
            return _NAMED["rdap"]
        return rate_domain(host)
    low = s.lower()
    for key, val in _NAMED.items():
        if key in low:
            return val
    if low.startswith("dns"):
        return _NAMED["dns"]
    return "LOW", "Source not classified by ATLAS."
tests/__init__.py1 lines
tests/helpers.py101 lines
"""Shared test fixtures: a fake web served through ATLAS's real safe_request pipeline."""
import os, sys, socket
from contextlib import ExitStack
from unittest import mock

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))

from atlas.utils import network_safety as ns

PUBLIC_IP = "93.184.216.34"


class FakeWeb:
    """
    Maps URL -> (status, headers, body). Anything unmapped is 404; robots.txt defaults to 404 (allow).
    Use as a context manager. `requests` records every URL fetched.
    """
    def __init__(self, pages=None, resolver=None, default=None):
        self.pages = dict(pages or {})
        self.default = default or (404, {"content-type": "text/plain"}, b"")
        self.requests = []
        self.sequences = {}      # url -> list of responses (or Exceptions) consumed in order
        self.routes = []         # (substring, response) checked in order when the exact URL is unknown
        self.resolver = resolver or (lambda host: [PUBLIC_IP])
        self._stack = ExitStack()

    def add(self, url, body="", status=200, ctype="text/html; charset=utf-8", headers=None):
        h = {"content-type": ctype}
        h.update(headers or {})
        self.pages[url] = (status, h, body.encode() if isinstance(body, str) else body)

    def sequence(self, url, items):
        self.sequences[url] = list(items)

    def route(self, substring, body="", status=200, ctype="application/json"):
        self.routes.append((substring, (status, {"content-type": ctype}, body.encode() if isinstance(body, str) else body)))

    def _single(self, url, method, headers, timeout, ips, max_bytes, read_body):
        self.requests.append((method, url))
        seq = self.sequences.get(url)
        if seq:
            item = seq.pop(0) if len(seq) > 1 else seq[0]
            if isinstance(item, Exception):
                raise item
            status, hdrs, body = item
            return ns.SimpleResponse(status, {k.lower(): v for k, v in hdrs.items()}, url, body if read_body else b"")
        if url in self.pages:
            status, hdrs, body = self.pages[url]
        else:
            status, hdrs, body = next((r for sub, r in self.routes if sub in url), self.default)
        r = ns.SimpleResponse(status, dict(hdrs), url, body if read_body else b"")
        return r

    def __enter__(self):
        ns.clear_robots_cache()
        from atlas.sources import web_pages
        web_pages.clear_cache()
        self._stack.enter_context(mock.patch.object(ns, "_single_request", side_effect=self._single))
        self._stack.enter_context(mock.patch.object(ns, "resolve_hostname", side_effect=self.resolver))
        self._stack.enter_context(mock.patch.object(ns, "rate_limit"))
        self.sleeps = []
        self._stack.enter_context(mock.patch.object(ns, "_sleep", side_effect=self.sleeps.append))
        ns.set_offline(False)
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *a):
        self._stack.close()
        ns.set_offline(False)
        ns.clear_robots_cache()


def patch_agent_network():
    """Patch module-level references so full investigations never touch the real network."""
    stack = ExitStack()
    gai = socket.gaierror("blocked in tests")
    stack.enter_context(mock.patch("atlas.sources.reputation.socket.gethostbyname", side_effect=gai))
    stack.enter_context(mock.patch("atlas.modules.domain_module.resolve_hostname", return_value=[PUBLIC_IP]))
    stack.enter_context(mock.patch("atlas.modules.domain_module.get_dns_records",
                                   return_value={"success": True, "method": "test", "records": {"A": [PUBLIC_IP]}}))
    return stack

<link rel="license" href="https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/">
<meta name="copyright" content="(c) Example Org">
<script type="application/ld+json">{"@context":"https://schema.org","@type":"Organization","name":"Example Org",
"logo":"/logo.png","address":{"@type":"PostalAddress","streetAddress":"1 Secret Street","postalCode":"AB1 2CD",
"addressLocality":"Cambridge","addressCountry":"GB"}}</script></head><body><h1>Example Org</h1></body></html>"""


def read(path):
    with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return f.read()


def write(path, text="x"):
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(text)

tests/test_accuracy.py439 lines
import json, unittest
from unittest import mock
from tests.helpers import FakeWeb, OG_PAGE, PUBLIC_IP, ns, patch_agent_network
from atlas.agent import AtlasAgent
from atlas.modules import org_module
from atlas.modules.ip_module import investigate_ip
from atlas.modules.org_module import candidate_domains, investigate_organisation, name_tokens, verify_site_name
from atlas.modules.person_module import investigate_person
from atlas.sources import cert_transparency as ct, provider_hints, wayback, wikidata
from atlas.sources.geoip import lookup_ip_geo


def A(status, headers=None, body=""):
    return (status, headers or {}, body.encode() if isinstance(body, str) else body)


class Retries(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_transient_503_is_retried_once(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.sequence("https://a.example/x", [A(503), A(200, {"content-type": "text/plain"}, "ok")])
            resp, err = ns.safe_request("https://a.example/x", retries=1)
            self.assertEqual((resp.status_code, resp.text), (200, "ok"))
            self.assertEqual(len([r for r in web.requests if r[1].endswith("/x")]), 2)
            self.assertEqual(web.sleeps, [1.5])

    def test_default_is_no_retry(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.sequence("https://a.example/x", [A(503), A(200)])
            self.assertEqual(ns.safe_request("https://a.example/x")[0].status_code, 503)

    def test_long_retry_after_is_respected_not_evaded(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.sequence("https://a.example/x", [A(429, {"retry-after": "60"}), A(200)])
            self.assertEqual(ns.safe_request("https://a.example/x", retries=3)[0].status_code, 429)
            self.assertEqual(len([r for r in web.requests if r[1].endswith("/x")]), 1)
            self.assertEqual(web.sleeps, [])

    def test_short_retry_after_is_honoured(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.sequence("https://a.example/x", [A(429, {"retry-after": "2"}), A(200)])
            self.assertEqual(ns.safe_request("https://a.example/x", retries=1)[0].status_code, 200)
            self.assertEqual(web.sleeps, [2.0])

    def test_connection_error_retried_then_reported(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.sequence("https://a.example/x", [OSError("reset"), A(200)])
            self.assertEqual(ns.safe_request("https://a.example/x", retries=1)[0].status_code, 200)
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.sequence("https://a.example/x", [OSError("reset")])
            resp, err = ns.safe_request("https://a.example/x", retries=2)
            self.assertIsNone(resp); self.assertIn("OSError", err)
            self.assertEqual(len(web.requests), 3)                           # 1 + 2 retries, then gives up

    def test_non_transient_codes_not_retried(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.sequence("https://a.example/x", [A(404), A(200)])
            self.assertEqual(ns.safe_request("https://a.example/x", retries=2)[0].status_code, 404)


IPINFO = json.dumps({"country": "US", "region": "California", "city": "Mountain View", "org": "AS15169 Google LLC",
                     "loc": "37.4,-122.0", "postal": "94043"})
def ipwho(cc="US", region="California", city="Mountain View"):
    return json.dumps({"success": True, "country": "United States", "country_code": cc, "region": region, "city": city,
                       "latitude": 37.4, "longitude": -122.0, "postal": "94043",
                       "connection": {"asn": 15169, "org": "Google LLC", "isp": "Google LLC"}, "timezone": {"id": "America/Los_Angeles"}})


class GeoConsensus(unittest.TestCase):
    def geo(self, ipinfo=IPINFO, who=None, apiapi=None):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            if ipinfo: web.add("https://ipinfo.io/8.8.8.8/json", ipinfo, ctype="application/json")
            if who: web.add("https://ipwho.is/8.8.8.8", who, ctype="application/json")
            if apiapi: web.add("http://ip-api.com/json/8.8.8.8?fields=status,message,country,countryCode,regionName,city,timezone,"
                               "isp,org,as,asname,mobile,proxy,hosting", apiapi, ctype="application/json")
            return lookup_ip_geo("8.8.8.8"), web

    def test_agreement(self):
        g, _ = self.geo(who=ipwho())
        self.assertEqual(g["sources_used"], ["ipinfo.io", "ipwho.is"])
        cc = g["cross_check"]
        self.assertEqual((cc["country_agreement"], cc["region_agreement"], cc["city_agreement"]), (True, True, True))
        self.assertEqual(g["data"]["country"], "United States")
        blob = json.dumps(g)
        for banned in ("37.4", "-122.0", "94043", "latitude", "postal"):
            self.assertNotIn(banned, blob)

    def test_country_disagreement_is_surfaced(self):
        g, _ = self.geo(who=ipwho(cc="CA", region="Ontario", city="Toronto"))
        self.assertIs(g["cross_check"]["country_agreement"], False)

    def test_city_only_disagreement(self):
        g, _ = self.geo(who=ipwho(city="Sunnyvale"))
        cc = g["cross_check"]
        self.assertEqual((cc["country_agreement"], cc["city_agreement"]), (True, False))

    def test_single_provider_is_flagged_as_uncrosschecked(self):
        g, _ = self.geo()
        self.assertTrue(g["success"]); self.assertIn("could not be cross-checked", g["cross_check"]["note"])
        self.assertIsNone(g["cross_check"]["country_agreement"])

    def test_cleartext_fallback_only_when_https_providers_fail(self):
        g, web = self.geo(ipinfo=None, apiapi=json.dumps({"status": "success", "countryCode": "US", "country": "United States"}))
        self.assertEqual(g["source"], "ip-api.com (cleartext HTTP)")
        g, web = self.geo(who=ipwho(), apiapi=json.dumps({"status": "success", "countryCode": "US"}))
        self.assertFalse([r for r in web.requests if "ip-api.com" in r[1]])

    def test_all_providers_fail_and_special_addresses(self):
        g, _ = self.geo(ipinfo=None)
        self.assertFalse(g["success"]); self.assertTrue(g["errors"])
        self.assertIn("Not a public", lookup_ip_geo("10.0.0.1")["errors"][0])
        ns.set_offline(True)
        try: self.assertIn("offline", lookup_ip_geo("8.8.8.8")["errors"][0])
        finally: ns.set_offline(False)

    def test_malformed_provider_data_never_crashes(self):
        for bad in ("not json", "[]", "{}", json.dumps({"success": False, "message": "quota"})):
            g, _ = self.geo(ipinfo=bad, who=bad)
            self.assertFalse(g["success"])


class IPAccuracy(unittest.TestCase):
    def run_ip(self, who, rdap_country="US"):
        rdap = json.dumps({"name": "GOOGLE", "country": rdap_country, "startAddress": "8.8.8.0", "endAddress": "8.8.8.255"})
        with FakeWeb() as web, mock.patch("atlas.sources.reputation.socket.gethostbyname", side_effect=__import__("socket").gaierror):
            web.add("https://rdap.org/ip/8.8.8.8", rdap, ctype="application/rdap+json")
            web.add("https://ipinfo.io/8.8.8.8/json", IPINFO, ctype="application/json")
            if who: web.add("https://ipwho.is/8.8.8.8", who, ctype="application/json")
            return investigate_ip("8.8.8.8")

    def test_agreeing_providers_raise_confidence(self):
        r = self.run_ip(ipwho())
        self.assertEqual(r["confidence_summary"]["geolocation"]["level"], "MEDIUM")
        self.assertEqual(r["sections"]["accuracy_checks"]["registry_vs_geolocation"], "match")

    def test_disagreement_lowers_confidence_and_warns(self):
        r = self.run_ip(ipwho(cc="CA"))
        self.assertIn("VERY LOW", r["confidence_summary"]["geolocation"]["level"])
        self.assertLess(r["confidence_summary"]["geolocation"]["score"], 0.35)
        self.assertTrue(any("disagree" in l for l in r["limitations"]))

    def test_single_provider_is_low_confidence(self):
        r = self.run_ip(None)
        self.assertEqual(r["confidence_summary"]["geolocation"]["level"], "LOW")

    def test_registry_country_difference_is_explained_not_called_an_error(self):
        r = self.run_ip(ipwho(), rdap_country="NL")
        ac = r["sections"]["accuracy_checks"]
        self.assertEqual(ac["registry_vs_geolocation"], "differs"); self.assertIn("not an error", ac["explanation"])


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- Wikidata
def wd_entity(human=True, site=None, occ=None, emp=None, wp=None):
    claims = {"P31": [{"mainsnak": {"datavalue": {"value": {"id": "Q5" if human else "Q4830453"}}}}]}
    if site: claims["P856"] = [{"mainsnak": {"datavalue": {"value": site}}}]
    if occ: claims["P106"] = [{"mainsnak": {"datavalue": {"value": {"id": q}}}} for q in occ]
    if emp: claims["P108"] = [{"mainsnak": {"datavalue": {"value": {"id": q}}}} for q in emp]
    e = {"claims": claims}
    if wp: e["sitelinks"] = {"enwiki": {"title": wp}}
    return e


def wd_web(web, hits, entities, labels=None):
    web.route("action=wbsearchentities", json.dumps({"search": hits}))
    web.route("props=labels|descriptions|claims", json.dumps({"entities": entities}))
    web.route("props=labels&languages", json.dumps({"entities": {q: {"labels": {"en": {"value": v}}} for q, v in (labels or {}).items()}}))


def hit(qid, label, desc=""):
    return {"id": qid, "label": label, "description": desc, "match": {"type": "label", "text": label}}


class Wikidata(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_single_human_selected_with_professional_facts_only(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            wd_web(web, [hit("Q1", "Jane Roe", "scientist")],
                   {"Q1": wd_entity(site="https://jane.example/", occ=["Q901"], emp=["Q902"], wp="Jane Roe")},
                   {"Q901": "physicist", "Q902": "Example University"})
            r = wikidata.lookup("Jane Roe", "person")
        sel = r["selected"]
        self.assertEqual((r["found"], r["ambiguous"], sel["qid"]), (True, False, "Q1"))
        self.assertEqual((sel["occupations"], sel["employers"], sel["official_website"]),
                         (["physicist"], ["Example University"], "https://jane.example/"))
        self.assertEqual(sel["wikipedia_url"], "https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jane_Roe")
        for banned in ("birth", "residence", "spouse", "P569", "P551", "P26"):
            self.assertNotIn(banned, json.dumps(r))

    def test_two_people_same_name_is_ambiguous_and_lists_candidates(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            wd_web(web, [hit("Q1", "Jane Roe", "scientist"), hit("Q2", "Jane Roe", "footballer")],
                   {"Q1": wd_entity(), "Q2": wd_entity()})
            r = wikidata.lookup("Jane Roe", "person")
        self.assertTrue(r["ambiguous"]); self.assertIsNone(r["selected"])
        self.assertEqual({c["description"] for c in r["candidates"]}, {"scientist", "footballer"})

    def test_kind_filters_people_from_organisations(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            wd_web(web, [hit("Q1", "Acme", "company"), hit("Q2", "Acme", "person")],
                   {"Q1": wd_entity(human=False, site="https://acme.example/"), "Q2": wd_entity()})
            self.assertEqual(wikidata.lookup("Acme", "org")["selected"]["qid"], "Q1")
            self.assertEqual(wikidata.lookup("Acme", "person")["selected"]["qid"], "Q2")

    def test_unsafe_official_website_is_ignored(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            wd_web(web, [hit("Q1", "Acme")], {"Q1": wd_entity(human=False, site="javascript:alert(1)")})
            self.assertIsNone(wikidata.lookup("Acme", "org")["selected"]["official_website"])
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            wd_web(web, [hit("Q1", "Acme")], {"Q1": wd_entity(human=False, site="https://u:p@acme.example/")})
            self.assertIsNone(wikidata.lookup("Acme", "org")["selected"]["official_website"])

    def test_no_match_and_failures(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            wd_web(web, [], {})
            self.assertFalse(wikidata.lookup("Nobody Here", "person")["found"])
        with FakeWeb():                                                     # 404 everywhere
            r = wikidata.lookup("Jane Roe", "person"); self.assertFalse(r["found"]); self.assertTrue(r["errors"])
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.route("action=wbsearchentities", "not json")
            self.assertFalse(wikidata.lookup("Jane Roe", "person")["found"])

    def test_fuzzy_non_exact_labels_are_never_selected(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            wd_web(web, [hit("Q1", "Jane Roeder")], {"Q1": wd_entity()})
            r = wikidata.lookup("Jane Roe", "person")
        self.assertIsNone(r["selected"]); self.assertFalse(r["found"])


class PersonWithWikidata(unittest.TestCase):
    def run_p(self, setup, sources=None):
        with FakeWeb() as web, mock.patch("atlas.modules.person_module.wikimedia.lookup_person", return_value={"found": False}):
            setup(web)
            return investigate_person("Jane Roe", sources=sources or []), web

    def test_unique_match_adds_roles_and_fetches_the_recorded_official_site(self):
        page = ('<title>Jane Roe</title><script type="application/ld+json">{"@type":"Person","name":"Jane Roe",'
                '"jobTitle":"Professor","address":{"addressLocality":"HomeTown"}}</script>')
        def setup(web):
            wd_web(web, [hit("Q1", "Jane Roe", "scientist")],
                   {"Q1": wd_entity(site="https://jane.example/", occ=["Q901"], emp=["Q902"])},
                   {"Q901": "physicist", "Q902": "Example University"})
            web.add("https://jane.example/", page)
        r, web = self.run_p(setup)
        p = r["professional"]
        self.assertEqual(p["identity"]["status"], "name_match_only")
        self.assertEqual(p["wikidata_profile"]["occupations"], ["physicist"])
        jobs = {(e["job_title"], e["source"].split("/")[2]) for e in p["employment"]}
        self.assertIn(("physicist", "www.wikidata.org"), jobs); self.assertIn(("Professor", "jane.example"), jobs)
        self.assertIn(("GET", "https://jane.example/"), web.requests)
        self.assertNotIn("HomeTown", json.dumps(r))
        self.assertNotEqual(r["confidence_summary"]["overall"]["level"], "HIGH")

    def test_ambiguous_name_lists_candidates_and_merges_nothing(self):
        def setup(web):
            wd_web(web, [hit("Q1", "Jane Roe", "scientist"), hit("Q2", "Jane Roe", "footballer")],
                   {"Q1": wd_entity(site="https://jane.example/"), "Q2": wd_entity()})
            web.add("https://jane.example/", "<title>Jane Roe</title>")
        r, web = self.run_p(setup)
        p = r["professional"]
        self.assertEqual(p["identity"]["status"], "ambiguous")
        self.assertIn("footballer", p["identity"]["explanation"]); self.assertIn("scientist", p["identity"]["explanation"])
        self.assertNotIn("wikidata_profile", p); self.assertEqual(p["employment"], [])
        self.assertNotIn(("GET", "https://jane.example/"), web.requests)       # no site fetched for an ambiguous match
        self.assertLessEqual(r["confidence_summary"]["overall"]["score"], 0.2)

    def test_explicit_user_sources_still_work_alongside(self):
        def setup(web):
            wd_web(web, [], {})
            web.add("https://dept.uni.ac.uk/jane", '<title>Jane Roe</title><script type="application/ld+json">'
                    '{"@type":"Person","name":"Jane Roe","jobTitle":"Chair"}</script>')
        r, _ = self.run_p(setup, sources=["https://dept.uni.ac.uk/jane"])
        self.assertEqual(r["professional"]["employment"][0]["job_title"], "Chair")


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- org
class OrgVerification(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_candidates_strip_legal_suffixes_and_are_capped(self):
        self.assertEqual(candidate_domains("Example Corp Ltd")[0], "example.com")
        self.assertIn("example.co.uk", candidate_domains("Example Ltd"))
        self.assertLessEqual(len(candidate_domains("Bank of America")), 8)
        self.assertEqual(candidate_domains("Ltd"), [])
        self.assertEqual(name_tokens("The Bank of America Corp")[1], ["the"][:0] + ["bank", "america"])

    def test_name_verification(self):
        ok = lambda n, **w: verify_site_name(n, w)[0]
        self.assertTrue(ok("Example Corp", title="Example Corp – Home"))
        self.assertTrue(ok("Example Corp", title="Welcome to Example Corp"))
        self.assertTrue(ok("Bank of America", title="Bank of America | Official Site"))
        self.assertTrue(ok("Example Corp", site_name="ExampleCorp"))
        self.assertTrue(ok("Bank of America", h1="Bank of America"))
        self.assertFalse(ok("Example Corp", title="Buy cheap domains"))
        self.assertFalse(ok("Example Corp", title="Example Widgets"))              # partial words are not enough
        self.assertFalse(ok("Example Corp", title="Examples of good design"))
        self.assertFalse(ok("Ltd", title="Ltd"))

    def run_org(self, setup, name="Example Corp"):
        with FakeWeb() as web, patch_agent_network():
            setup(web)
            return investigate_organisation(name), web

    def test_unrelated_responding_domain_is_excluded_and_called_unrelated(self):
        def setup(web):
            web.add("https://example.com", "<title>Parked domain for sale</title>")
        r, _ = self.run_org(setup)
        site = r["sections"]["possible_official_sites"]["responsive_sites"][0]
        self.assertFalse(site["name_match"])
        self.assertEqual(r["sections"]["possible_official_sites"]["name_verified_sites"], [])
        self.assertTrue(any("probably unrelated" in f["finding"] for f in r["key_findings"]))
        self.assertLess(r["confidence_summary"]["web_presence"]["score"], 0.35)

    def test_guessed_site_with_matching_name_is_only_a_low_lead(self):
        def setup(web):
            web.add("https://example.com", "<title>Example Corp – Welcome</title>")
        r, _ = self.run_org(setup)
        self.assertEqual(r["sections"]["possible_official_sites"]["name_verified_sites"], ["example.com"])
        f = next(f for f in r["key_findings"] if "Likely official" in f["finding"])
        self.assertEqual(f["confidence"], "LOW"); self.assertIn("NOT confirmed", f["finding"])

    def test_wikidata_official_site_beats_guessing_and_is_name_verified(self):
        def setup(web):
            wd_web(web, [hit("Q9", "Example Corp", "company")], {"Q9": wd_entity(human=False, site="https://www.excorp.test/")})
            web.add("https://www.excorp.test/", "<title>Example Corp | Official</title>")
            web.add("https://example.com", "<title>Example Corp</title>")      # a guess that must NOT be tested
            web.route("https://crt.sh/?O=", "[]")
        r, web = self.run_org(setup)
        site = r["sections"]["possible_official_sites"]["responsive_sites"][0]
        self.assertEqual((site["domain"], site["name_match"]), ("excorp.test", True))
        self.assertIn("Wikidata", site["origin"])
        self.assertEqual(r["confidence_summary"]["web_presence"]["score"], 0.7)
        self.assertFalse([x for x in web.requests if x[1].rstrip("/") == "https://example.com"])
        self.assertTrue(any("per Wikidata" in f["finding"] for f in r["key_findings"]))

    def test_ambiguous_organisation_is_not_resolved_by_guessing_wikidata(self):
        def setup(web):
            wd_web(web, [hit("Q9", "Example Corp", "company"), hit("Q8", "Example Corp", "charity")],
                   {"Q9": wd_entity(human=False, site="https://a.test/"), "Q8": wd_entity(human=False, site="https://b.test/")})
            web.route("https://crt.sh/?O=", "[]")
        r, web = self.run_org(setup)
        self.assertTrue(r["sections"]["wikidata"]["ambiguous"])
        self.assertTrue(any("share this name" in l for l in r["limitations"]))
        self.assertNotIn(("GET", "https://a.test/"), web.requests)

    def test_certificate_organisation_domains_are_low_confidence_leads(self):
        def setup(web):
            web.route("https://crt.sh/?O=", json.dumps([{"name_value": "www.example-corp.com\nexample-corp.co.uk"},
                                                        {"name_value": "api.example-corp.com"}]))
        r, _ = self.run_org(setup)
        doms = {d["domain"]: d["certificates"] for d in r["sections"]["certificate_organisation_domains"]["domains"]}
        self.assertEqual(doms, {"example-corp.com": 2, "example-corp.co.uk": 1})
        f = next(f for f in r["key_findings"] if "unverified leads" in f["finding"]); self.assertEqual(f["confidence"], "LOW")


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- domain extras
class Wayback(unittest.TestCase):
    def web(self, web, first, last, months):
        base = "https://web.archive.org/cdx/search/cdx?url=example.org&output=json&fl=timestamp"
        web.add(base + "&limit=1", json.dumps([["timestamp"]] + first), ctype="application/json")
        web.add(base + "&limit=-1", json.dumps([["timestamp"]] + last), ctype="application/json")
        web.add(base + "&collapse=timestamp:6&limit=3000", json.dumps([["timestamp"]] + months), ctype="application/json")

    def test_history(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            self.web(web, [["20050301120000"]], [["20260101000000"]], [[f"2005{m:02d}01000000"] for m in range(3, 13)])
            r = wayback.query_wayback("example.org")
        self.assertEqual((r["first_capture"], r["last_capture"], r["months_with_captures"]), ("2005-03-01", "2026-01-01", 10))
        self.assertTrue(r["first_snapshot_url"].startswith("https://web.archive.org/web/20050301120000/"))
        self.assertIn("not show who operated", r["note"])

    def test_no_captures_is_reported_as_such(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            self.web(web, [], [], [])
            r = wayback.query_wayback("example.org")
        self.assertEqual((r["success"], r["captures_found"]), (True, False))

    def test_failure_and_garbage(self):
        with FakeWeb():
            self.assertFalse(wayback.query_wayback("example.org")["success"])
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.route("web.archive.org/cdx", "garbage")
            self.assertFalse(wayback.query_wayback("example.org")["success"])
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.route("web.archive.org/cdx", json.dumps([["timestamp"], ["not-a-timestamp"]]))
            self.assertFalse(wayback.query_wayback("example.org")["captures_found"])


class RelatedDomains(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_related_domains_exclude_own_and_shared_cdn_certificates(self):
        shared = "\n".join(f"customer{i}.net" for i in range(30)) + "\nexample.org"
        entries = [{"name_value": "www.example.org\nexample-shop.com\nsni123.cloudflaressl.com"},
                   {"name_value": "api.example.org\nexample-shop.com\npartner.co.uk"},
                   {"name_value": shared}]
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.add("https://crt.sh/?q=%25.example.org&output=json", json.dumps(entries), ctype="application/json")
            r = ct.search_crtsh("example.org")
        rel = {d["domain"]: d["certificates"] for d in r["related_domains"]}
        self.assertEqual(rel, {"example-shop.com": 2, "partner.co.uk": 1})
        self.assertNotIn("example.org", rel); self.assertFalse(any("customer" in d for d in rel))
        self.assertIn("verify", r["related_domains_note"])

    def test_registrable(self):
        self.assertEqual(ct.registrable("a.b.example.co.uk"), "example.co.uk")
        self.assertEqual(ct.registrable("www.example.com"), "example.com")


class ProviderHints(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_inference(self):
        r = provider_hints.infer_providers({"MX": ["10 aspmx.l.google.com."], "NS": ["ns-1.awsdns-01.org."],
                                            "TXT": ["v=spf1 include:_spf.google.com include:sendgrid.net ~all", "other"]})
        self.assertEqual((r["email"], r["dns"], r["sending_services"]),
                         (["Google Workspace / Gmail"], ["Amazon Route 53"], ["Google Workspace", "SendGrid"]))
        self.assertEqual(provider_hints.infer_providers({}), {"email": [], "dns": [], "sending_services": [], "caveat": r["caveat"]})


class DomainIntegration(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_new_sections_and_findings(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web, patch_agent_network(), \
             mock.patch("atlas.modules.domain_module.get_dns_records", return_value={
                 "success": True, "method": "t", "records": {"MX": ["10 aspmx.l.google.com."], "NS": ["a.awsdns-1.org."]}}):
            base = "https://web.archive.org/cdx/search/cdx?url=example.org&output=json&fl=timestamp"
            web.add(base + "&limit=1", json.dumps([["timestamp"], ["20100101000000"]]), ctype="application/json")
            web.add(base + "&limit=-1", json.dumps([["timestamp"], ["20260101000000"]]), ctype="application/json")
            web.add(base + "&collapse=timestamp:6&limit=3000", json.dumps([["timestamp"], ["20100101000000"]]), ctype="application/json")
            web.add("https://crt.sh/?q=%25.example.org&output=json",
                    json.dumps([{"name_value": "example.org\nexample-shop.com"}]), ctype="application/json")
            web.add("https://example.org/", OG_PAGE)
            from atlas.modules.domain_module import investigate_domain
            r = investigate_domain("example.org")
        s = r["sections"]
        self.assertEqual(s["web_history"]["first_capture"], "2010-01-01")
        self.assertEqual(s["related_domains"]["domains"][0]["domain"], "example-shop.com")
        self.assertEqual(s["provider_inference"]["email"], ["Google Workspace / Gmail"])
        text = " ".join(f["finding"] for f in r["key_findings"])
        for needle in ("Internet Archive has captures from 2010-01-01", "Possibly related domains", "Provider inference"):
            self.assertIn(needle, text)
        self.assertTrue(all(f["source"] for f in r["key_findings"]))
        self.assertEqual(r["confidence_summary"]["web_history"]["level"], "MEDIUM")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

tests/test_classifier.py46 lines
import unittest
from tests.helpers import ns
from atlas.classifier import TargetType as T, classify, parse_forced_type


class Classifier(unittest.TestCase):
    def check(self, s, typ, norm=None):
        r = classify(s)
        self.assertEqual(r.target_type, typ, s)
        if norm is not None:
            self.assertEqual(r.normalised, norm, s)
        return r

    def test_names_and_orgs(self):
        self.check("Abraham Lincoln", T.PERSON)
        self.check("Tim Cook", T.PERSON, "Tim Cook")
        self.check("Apple Inc", T.ORGANISATION)
        self.check("Bank of America", T.ORGANISATION)
        self.check("Microsoft", T.ORGANISATION)
        self.check("Mary-Jane O'Neil", T.PERSON)

    def test_urls(self):
        r = self.check("https://user:pw@example.com:8443/x", T.URL, "example.com")
        self.assertNotIn("pw", r.original)
        self.check("http://8.8.8.8/path", T.IP, "8.8.8.8")
        self.check("http://[2001:db8::1]/", T.IP, "2001:db8::1")

    def test_ips_and_domains(self):
        self.check("8.8.8.8", T.IP)
        self.check("[2001:db8::1]", T.IP, "2001:db8::1")
        self.check("www.Example.com/a", T.DOMAIN, "example.com")

    def test_quotes_and_empty(self):
        self.check('"Tim Cook"', T.PERSON, "Tim Cook")
        self.check("", T.UNKNOWN)

    def test_force(self):
        self.assertEqual(classify("Tim Cook", parse_forced_type("org")).target_type, T.ORGANISATION)
        self.assertEqual(classify("nonsense", parse_forced_type("ip")).target_type, T.UNKNOWN)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            parse_forced_type("nonsense")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

tests/test_cli_ui.py148 lines
import contextlib, io, os, tempfile, unittest
from unittest import mock
from tests.helpers import FakeWeb, ns
import main as atlas_main
from atlas import ui


def run(argv, stdin=None):
    out, err = io.StringIO(), io.StringIO()
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out), contextlib.redirect_stderr(err):
        try:
            code = atlas_main.main(argv)
        except SystemExit as e:
            code = e.code
    return code, out.getvalue(), err.getvalue()


class OriginalCLIStillWorks(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(); self.addCleanup(self.tmp.cleanup)
        self.addCleanup(lambda: ns.set_offline(False))

    def test_dash_t_form(self):
        code, out, _ = run(["-t", "10.0.0.1", "--no-report", "-o", self.tmp.name])
        self.assertEqual(code, 0); self.assertIn("Classified as IP", out)
        self.assertEqual(len(os.listdir(self.tmp.name)), 2)

    def test_investigate_form_unquoted_multiword(self):
        code, out, _ = run(["investigate", "Tim", "Cook", "--offline", "--no-report", "-o", self.tmp.name])
        self.assertEqual(code, 0); self.assertIn("Classified as PERSON", out)

    def test_bare_target_and_type_override(self):
        code, out, _ = run(["Microsoft", "--type", "person", "--offline", "--no-report", "--no-save"])
        self.assertIn("Classified as PERSON", out)

    def test_bad_type_is_a_clean_error(self):
        code, _, err = run(["--type", "bogus", "x"])
        self.assertEqual(code, 2); self.assertIn("Unknown target type", err)

    def test_unknown_target_returns_error_code(self):
        code, out, _ = run(["investigate", "--type", "ip", "notanip", "--no-save"])
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)

    def test_full_report_is_printed_by_default_with_all_sections(self):
        code, out, _ = run(["10.0.0.1", "--no-save"])
        for n in range(1, 11):
            self.assertIn(f"{n}. ", out)
        self.assertIn("PRIVACY AND LEGAL CONSIDERATIONS", out)

    def test_html_flag_and_no_save(self):
        code, out, _ = run(["10.0.0.1", "--html", "--no-report", "-o", self.tmp.name])
        self.assertTrue(any(f.endswith(".html") for f in os.listdir(self.tmp.name)))
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
            run(["10.0.0.1", "--no-save", "-o", d])
            self.assertEqual(os.listdir(d), [])



class NewCommands(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.addCleanup(lambda: ns.set_offline(False))

    def test_reports_management(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
            run(["10.0.0.1", "--no-report", "-o", d])
            code, out, _ = run(["reports", "list", "-o", d]); self.assertIn("atlas_10.0.0.1_", out)
            code, out, _ = run(["--list-reports", "-o", d]); self.assertEqual(code, 0)
            name = [r for r in os.listdir(d) if r.endswith(".json")][0][:-5]
            code, out, _ = run(["reports", "delete", name, "-o", d]); self.assertIn("Deleted 2", out)
            code, out, _ = run(["reports", "delete", "../../etc/passwd", "-o", d]); self.assertEqual(code, 1)
            run(["10.0.0.1", "--no-report", "-o", d])
            with mock.patch("sys.stdin.isatty", return_value=False):          # no permission => nothing deleted
                code, out, _ = run(["--delete-reports", "-o", d])
            self.assertEqual(code, 1); self.assertEqual(len(os.listdir(d)), 2)
            with mock.patch("sys.stdin.isatty", return_value=True), mock.patch("builtins.input", return_value="y"):
                run(["--delete-reports", "-o", d])
            self.assertEqual(os.listdir(d), [])
            code, out, _ = run(["--purge-older-than", "1", "-o", d]); self.assertEqual(code, 0)


class Interactive(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_interactive_session_all_commands(self):
        lines = iter(["help", "report", "export", "investigate 10.0.0.1", "10.0.0.2", "report",
                      "export html", "selftest", "reports list", "reports purge x", "offline on", "offline off",
                      "bogus --type", "investigate", "investigate --type nonsense a", "", "exit"])
        tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(); self.addCleanup(tmp.cleanup); self.addCleanup(lambda: ns.set_offline(False))
        from atlas.agent import AtlasAgent
        agent = AtlasAgent(output_dir=tmp.name, offline=True)
        out = io.StringIO()
        with mock.patch("builtins.input", side_effect=lambda *a: next(lines)), contextlib.redirect_stdout(out):
            atlas_main.run_interactive(agent)
        text = out.getvalue()
        for needle in ("Commands", "No report to export", "Classified as IP", "Exported:",
                       "Platform rule health check", "Offline mode ON", "Offline mode OFF", "Goodbye"):
            self.assertIn(needle, text, needle)
        self.assertNotIn("Traceback", text)


class UI(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_sanitize_strips_terminal_escapes(self):
        s = ui.sanitize("a\x1b[2Jb\x1b]0;pwned\x07c\x9bd\x00e\nf\tg")
        self.assertNotIn("\x1b", s); self.assertNotIn("\x07", s); self.assertNotIn("\x9b", s)
        self.assertIn("\nf\tg", s)

    def test_colour_added_only_by_atlas_after_sanitising(self):
        out = io.StringIO()
        with mock.patch("atlas.ui._color_enabled", return_value=True), contextlib.redirect_stdout(out):
            ui.emit("a\x1b[31mINJECTED\x1b[0m", "red")
        self.assertEqual(out.getvalue(), "\x1b[31ma[31mINJECTED[0m\x1b[0m\n")
        # i.e. the injected escapes lost their ESC byte; the only ESC bytes are ATLAS's own wrapper
        self.assertEqual(out.getvalue().count("\x1b"), 2)

    def test_no_colour_when_not_a_tty_or_no_color(self):
        with mock.patch("sys.stdout.isatty", return_value=False):
            self.assertFalse(ui._color_enabled())
        with mock.patch("sys.stdout.isatty", return_value=True), mock.patch.dict(os.environ, {"NO_COLOR": "1", "TERM": "xterm"}):
            self.assertFalse(ui._color_enabled())

    def test_emit_sanitises_remote_text(self):
        out = io.StringIO()
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out):
            ui.emit("title \x1b[31mred\x1b[0m \x1b]8;;http://evil\x1b\\click")
        self.assertNotIn("\x1b", out.getvalue())

    def test_links_are_plain_sanitised_urls_never_escape_sequences(self):
        for tty in (False, True):
            with mock.patch("sys.stdout.isatty", return_value=tty), mock.patch.dict(os.environ, {"TERM": "xterm"}):
                self.assertEqual(ui.link("https://a.example/x"), "https://a.example/x")
                self.assertNotIn("\x1b", ui.link("https://a.example/\x1b]8;;http://evil\x1b\\"))

    def test_options_may_precede_the_target(self):
        code, out, _ = run(["investigate", "--type", "person", "--offline", "--no-save", "--no-report", "Tim", "Cook"])
        self.assertEqual(code, 0); self.assertIn("Classified as PERSON", out)

    def test_parse_target_args(self):
        t, o = ui.parse_target_args(["Jane", "Roe", "--source", "https://a.example/", "--type", "person",
                                     "--platforms", "my.json", "--stop-at-first"])
        self.assertEqual((t, o["sources"], o["type"], o["platforms"], o["stop_at_first"]),
                         ("Jane Roe", ["https://a.example/"], "person", "my.json", True))

    def test_split_command_tolerates_bad_quotes(self):
        self.assertEqual(ui.split_command('investigate "Tim Cook'), ["investigate", '"Tim', "Cook"])
        self.assertEqual(ui.split_command('investigate "Tim Cook"'), ["investigate", "Tim Cook"])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

tests/test_dns.py66 lines
import struct, unittest
from tests.helpers import ns
from atlas.sources import dns_lookup as d


def _resp(txid=0x1234, answers=b"", an=0, flags=0x8180):
    _, pkt = d.build_query("example.com", 1, txid=txid)
    return struct.pack(">HHHHHH", txid, flags, 1, an, 0, 0) + pkt[12:] + answers


class DNSClient(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_parse_a_and_mx_with_compression(self):
        a = b"\xc0\x0c" + struct.pack(">HHIH", 1, 1, 60, 4) + bytes([93, 184, 216, 34])
        mx_rdata = struct.pack(">H", 10) + b"\x04mail\xc0\x0c"
        a += b"\xc0\x0c" + struct.pack(">HHIH", 15, 1, 60, len(mx_rdata)) + mx_rdata
        rcode, trunc, ans = d.parse_response(_resp(answers=a, an=2), 0x1234)
        self.assertEqual((rcode, trunc), (0, False))
        self.assertEqual(ans, [("A", "93.184.216.34"), ("MX", "10 mail.example.com")])

    def test_txt_and_caa(self):
        txt = b"\x05hello\x06 world"
        caa = bytes([0, 5]) + b"issue" + b"letsencrypt.org"
        a = (b"\xc0\x0c" + struct.pack(">HHIH", 16, 1, 1, len(txt)) + txt +
             b"\xc0\x0c" + struct.pack(">HHIH", 257, 1, 1, len(caa)) + caa)
        _, _, ans = d.parse_response(_resp(answers=a, an=2), 0x1234)
        self.assertEqual(ans[0], ("TXT", "hello world"))
        self.assertEqual(ans[1], ("CAA", '0 issue "letsencrypt.org"'))

    def test_malformed_inputs_raise_dnserror_not_crash(self):
        good = _resp(answers=b"\xc0\x0c" + struct.pack(">HHIH", 1, 1, 60, 4) + b"\x01\x02\x03\x04", an=1)
        cases = [b"", b"\x00" * 5, good[:20], good[:-2],                         # truncated
                 _resp(txid=0x9999),                                               # id mismatch
                 _resp(flags=0x0100),                                              # not a response
                 struct.pack(">HHHHHH", 0x1234, 0x8180, 1, 0, 0, 0) + b"\xc0\x0c" + b"\0\1\0\1",  # pointer loop
                 _resp(answers=b"\xc0\x0c" + struct.pack(">HHIH", 1, 1, 60, 500), an=1)]  # rdlen overruns
        for c in cases:
            with self.assertRaises(d.DNSError, msg=c[:12]):
                d.parse_response(c, 0x1234)

    def test_label_validation(self):
        with self.assertRaises(d.DNSError):
            d.build_query("a" * 70 + ".com", 1)

    def test_nameserver_parsing(self):
        import tempfile, os
        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile("w", delete=False, suffix=".conf") as f:
            f.write("# c\nnameserver 192.0.2.1\nnameserver fe80::1%eth0\nsearch x\n")
        try:
            self.assertEqual(d.get_system_nameservers(f.name), ["192.0.2.1", "fe80::1"])
        finally:
            os.unlink(f.name)
        self.assertEqual(d.get_system_nameservers("/nonexistent/resolv.conf"), [])

    def test_offline(self):
        ns.set_offline(True)
        try:
            r = d.get_dns_records("example.com")
            self.assertFalse(r["success"])
            self.assertIn("offline", r["errors"][0])
        finally:
            ns.set_offline(False)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

import contextlib, io, json, os, socket, tempfile, unittest
from unittest import mock
from tests.helpers import FakeWeb, OG_PAGE, PUBLIC_IP, ns, patch_agent_network, read
from atlas.agent import AtlasAgent
from atlas.modules.ip_module import investigate_ip

IPINFO = json.dumps({"ip": "93.184.216.34", "country": "US", "region": "Massachusetts", "city": "Norwood",
                     "loc": "42.19,-71.2", "postal": "02062", "org": "AS15133 Edgecast Hosting Inc", "hostname": "x.cloud.example"})
RDAP = json.dumps({"handle": "NET-93", "name": "EDGECAST-NETBLK", "country": "US", "startAddress": "93.184.216.0",
                   "endAddress": "93.184.223.255", "ipVersion": "v4", "entities": [{"roles": ["abuse"], "vcardArray": [
                       "vcard", [["fn", {}, "text", "Abuse Team"], ["email", {}, "text", "abuse@example.net"],
                                 ["tel", {}, "text", "+1 555 0100"]]]}]})


def ip_web(web, ip="93.184.216.34"):
    web.add(f"https://rdap.org/ip/{ip}", RDAP, ctype="application/rdap+json")
    web.add(f"https://ipinfo.io/{ip}/json", IPINFO, ctype="application/json")


class IPModule(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_special_addresses_make_zero_network_calls(self):
        cases = {"10.0.0.1": "private", "127.0.0.1": "loopback", "169.254.9.9": "link-local", "224.0.0.5": "multicast",
                 "240.1.1.1": "reserved", "0.0.0.0": "unspecified", "::1": "loopback", "fe80::1": "link-local",
                 "ff02::1": "multicast", "100.64.0.1": "shared-address-space", "::ffff:10.0.0.1": "private"}
        with mock.patch.object(ns, "_single_request", side_effect=AssertionError("network used")), \
             mock.patch("socket.getaddrinfo", side_effect=AssertionError("dns used")):
            for ip, cat in cases.items():
                r = investigate_ip(ip)
                self.assertEqual(r["sections"]["basic"]["category"], cat, ip)
                self.assertFalse({"rdap", "geolocation", "reputation"} & set(r["sections"]), ip)
                self.assertTrue(any("skipped" in l for l in r["limitations"]), ip)

    def test_invalid_ip(self):
        self.assertIn("error", investigate_ip("999.1.1.1")["sections"]["basic"])

    def test_public_ipv4_full_findings_and_uncertainty(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web, mock.patch("atlas.sources.reputation.socket.gethostbyname", side_effect=socket.gaierror):
            ip_web(web)
            r = investigate_ip("93.184.216.34")
        self.assertTrue(r["sections"]["rdap"]["success"])
        self.assertTrue(r["sections"]["hosting_indicators"]["likely_hosting_or_datacentre"])
        self.assertIn("not proof", r["sections"]["uncertainty"]["warning"])
        kf = " ".join(f["finding"] for f in r["key_findings"])
        self.assertIn("EDGECAST-NETBLK", kf); self.assertIn("AS15133", kf)
        self.assertTrue(all(f["source"] for f in r["key_findings"]))
        blob = json.dumps(r)
        for banned in ("42.19", "-71.2", "02062", "abuse@example.net", "+1 555 0100"):
            self.assertNotIn(banned, blob)

    def test_ipv6_and_mapped_addresses(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web, mock.patch("atlas.sources.reputation.socket.gethostbyname", side_effect=socket.gaierror):
            ip_web(web, "2606:4700::1111")
            r = investigate_ip("2606:4700::1111")
            self.assertEqual(r["sections"]["basic"]["ip_version"], "IPv6")
            self.assertTrue(r["sections"]["rdap"]["success"])
            ip_web(web, "8.8.8.8")
            self.assertEqual(investigate_ip("::ffff:8.8.8.8")["target"], "8.8.8.8")

    def test_blocklist_refusal_is_not_a_listing(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web, mock.patch("atlas.sources.reputation.socket.gethostbyname", return_value="127.255.255.254"):
            ip_web(web)
            r = investigate_ip("93.184.216.34")
        self.assertTrue(all(f["listed"] is not True for f in r["sections"]["reputation"]["findings"]))
        self.assertIn("no usable answer", r["confidence_summary"]["reputation"]["note"])

    def test_sources_unavailable_degrade_gracefully(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web, mock.patch("atlas.sources.reputation.socket.gethostbyname", side_effect=socket.gaierror):
            r = investigate_ip("93.184.216.34")                      # everything 404
        self.assertFalse(r["sections"]["rdap"]["success"]); self.assertTrue(r["limitations"])

    def test_offline_public_ip(self):
        ns.set_offline(True)
        try:
            r = investigate_ip("8.8.8.8")
        finally:
            ns.set_offline(False)
        self.assertTrue(any("Offline" in l or "offline" in l for l in r["limitations"]))
        self.assertFalse(r["sections"]["rdap"]["success"])


class AgentFlows(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(); self.addCleanup(self.tmp.cleanup)
        self.agent = AtlasAgent(output_dir=self.tmp.name)

    def test_full_ip_report_exports_and_has_no_removed_sections(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web, patch_agent_network():
            ip_web(web)
            r = self.agent.investigate("93.184.216.34", html=True)
        self.assertEqual(set(r["export"]), {"json", "txt", "html"})
        self.assertTrue(all(os.path.exists(p) for p in r["export"].values()))
        for gone in ("images", "location"):
            self.assertNotIn(gone, r)
        self.assertIn("legal_assessment", r); self.assertTrue(r["source_reliability"])

    def test_domain_report(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web, patch_agent_network():
            web.add("https://example.org/", OG_PAGE)
            r = self.agent.investigate("example.org")
        self.assertEqual(r["target_type"], "DOMAIN")
        self.assertIn("web_history", r["sections"])
        self.assertTrue(any("responded" in f["finding"] for f in r["key_findings"]))

    def test_person_report_is_conservative_without_sources(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web, mock.patch("atlas.modules.person_module.wikimedia.lookup_person",
                                          return_value={"found": False, "errors": []}):
            r = self.agent.investigate("Jane Roe")
        self.assertEqual(r["target_type"], "PERSON")
        self.assertEqual(r["professional"]["identity"]["status"], "no_information_located")
        self.assertIn("person_focused_research", r["legal_assessment"]["activity_classification"])
        self.assertNotIn("images", r)

    def test_unsafe_sources_are_rejected_and_reported(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web, mock.patch("atlas.modules.person_module.wikimedia.lookup_person", return_value={"found": False}):
            r = self.agent.investigate("Jane Roe", sources=["file:///etc/passwd", "http://127.0.0.1/x", "https://u:p@a.example/"])
        self.assertEqual(sum("Ignored unsafe" in l for l in r["limitations"]), 3)
        self.assertEqual([x for x in web.requests if "passwd" in x[1] or "127.0.0.1" in x[1]], [])

    def test_unknown_input(self):
        r = self.agent.investigate("")
        self.assertEqual(r["target_type"], "UNKNOWN"); self.assertIn("error", r)
        self.assertEqual(os.listdir(self.tmp.name), [])

    def test_org_flow(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web, patch_agent_network():
            web.add("https://examplecorp.com/", "<html><title>Example Corp – Home</title></html>")
            r = self.agent.investigate("Example Corp")
        self.assertEqual(r["target_type"], "ORGANISATION"); self.assertTrue(r["key_findings"])

    def test_every_type_works_fully_offline_without_network_attempts(self):
        agent = AtlasAgent(output_dir=self.tmp.name, offline=True)
        with mock.patch.object(ns, "_single_request", side_effect=AssertionError("network used")), \
             mock.patch("socket.getaddrinfo", side_effect=AssertionError("dns used")), \
             mock.patch("socket.gethostbyname", side_effect=AssertionError("dns used")), \
             mock.patch("socket.socket", side_effect=AssertionError("socket used")):
            for target in ("8.8.8.8", "example.org", "https://example.org/x", "Example Corp", "Jane Roe", "10.0.0.1", "@jdoe_42"):
                r = agent.investigate(target)
                self.assertTrue(r["offline"], target); self.assertIn("completed_at", r)
        self.addCleanup(lambda: ns.set_offline(False))

    def test_save_disabled_writes_nothing(self):
        AtlasAgent(output_dir=self.tmp.name, save=False).investigate("10.0.0.1")
        self.assertEqual(os.listdir(self.tmp.name), [])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
