import http.server, threading, unittest
import unittest.mock
from tests.helpers import FakeWeb, ns, PUBLIC_IP


class IPCategories(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_categories(self):
        table = {"8.8.8.8": "public", "10.1.1.1": "private", "192.168.0.1": "private", "127.0.0.1": "loopback",
                 "169.254.1.1": "link-local", "224.0.0.1": "multicast", "0.0.0.0": "unspecified",
                 "100.64.1.1": "shared-address-space", "192.0.2.5": "documentation", "240.0.0.1": "reserved",
                 "::1": "loopback", "fe80::1": "link-local", "ff02::1": "multicast", "fc00::1": "private",
                 "2001:db8::1": "documentation", "2606:4700::1111": "public", "::ffff:8.8.8.8": "public",
                 "::ffff:10.0.0.1": "private"}
        for ip, cat in table.items():
            self.assertEqual(ns.ip_category(ip)["category"], cat, ip)

    def test_invalid(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            ns.ip_category("999.1.1.1")


class URLValidation(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_rejected(self):
        for u in ("", "ftp://a.com", "file:///etc/passwd", "javascript:alert(1)", "data:text/html,x", "http://a b.com",
                  "https://u:p@a.com/", "http://a.com:22/", "http://", "http://a.com/\x00", "https://a.com/" + "x" * 3000):
            self.assertFalse(ns.validate_url(u)[0], u)

    def test_accepted(self):
        for u in ("https://a.com/x?y=1", "http://a.com:8080/", "https://[2606:4700::1111]/"):
            self.assertTrue(ns.validate_url(u)[0], u)


class SafeRequest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_refuses_private_literals(self):
        for u in ("http://127.0.0.1/", "http://10.0.0.5/x", "http://[::1]/", "http://169.254.169.254/latest/meta-data/",
                  "http://192.168.1.1/", "http://0.0.0.0/"):
            resp, err = ns.safe_request(u)
            self.assertIsNone(resp, u)
            self.assertIn("refused", err)

    def test_hostname_resolving_to_private_refused(self):
        with FakeWeb(resolver=lambda h: ["10.0.0.9"]) as web:
            resp, err = ns.safe_request("http://internal.example/")
            self.assertIsNone(resp)
            self.assertIn("non-public", err)
            self.assertEqual(web.requests, [])

    def test_mixed_resolution_refused(self):
        with FakeWeb(resolver=lambda h: [PUBLIC_IP, "127.0.0.1"]):
            self.assertIsNone(ns.safe_request("http://rebind.example/")[0])

    def test_redirect_to_private_blocked(self):
        def resolver(h):
            return ["127.0.0.1"] if h == "evil.internal" else [PUBLIC_IP]
        with FakeWeb(resolver=resolver) as web:
            web.add("http://a.example/", "", 302, headers={"location": "http://evil.internal/admin"})
            resp, err = ns.safe_request("http://a.example/")
            self.assertIsNone(resp)
            self.assertIn("refused", err)

    def test_redirect_to_file_scheme_blocked(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.add("http://a.example/", "", 302, headers={"location": "file:///etc/passwd"})
            resp, err = ns.safe_request("http://a.example/")
            self.assertIsNone(resp)
            self.assertIn("unsupported scheme", err)

    def test_redirect_loop_capped(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.add("http://a.example/", "", 302, headers={"location": "http://a.example/"})
            self.assertIn("too many redirects", ns.safe_request("http://a.example/")[1])

    def test_relative_redirect_followed(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.add("http://a.example/", "", 301, headers={"location": "/final"})
            web.add("http://a.example/final", "ok")
            resp, err = ns.safe_request("http://a.example/")
            self.assertEqual(resp.text, "ok")

    def test_offline_mode(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            ns.set_offline(True)
            resp, err = ns.safe_request("https://a.example/")
            self.assertIsNone(resp)
            self.assertIn("offline", err)
            self.assertEqual(web.requests, [])

    def test_offline_resolution_returns_nothing(self):
        ns.set_offline(True)
        try:
            self.assertEqual(ns.resolve_hostname("localhost"), [])
        finally:
            ns.set_offline(False)


class Robots(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_disallowed(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.add("https://a.example/robots.txt", "User-agent: *\nDisallow: /private\n", ctype="text/plain")
            web.add("https://a.example/private/x", "secret")
            web.add("https://a.example/public", "fine")
            self.assertIsNone(ns.safe_request("https://a.example/private/x", respect_robots=True)[0])
            self.assertEqual(ns.safe_request("https://a.example/public", respect_robots=True)[0].text, "fine")

    def test_specific_agent_blocked(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.add("https://a.example/robots.txt", "User-agent: ATLAS-OSINT\nDisallow: /\n", ctype="text/plain")
            web.add("https://a.example/", "x")
            resp, err = ns.safe_request("https://a.example/", respect_robots=True)
            self.assertIsNone(resp)
            self.assertIn("robots", err)

    def test_missing_robots_allows(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.add("https://a.example/", "x")
            self.assertIsNotNone(ns.safe_request("https://a.example/", respect_robots=True)[0])

    def test_robots_server_error_is_conservative(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.add("https://a.example/robots.txt", "", 503)
            web.add("https://a.example/", "x")
            self.assertIsNone(ns.safe_request("https://a.example/", respect_robots=True)[0])

    def test_robots_unreachable_is_conservative(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            with unittest.mock.patch.object(ns, "_single_request", side_effect=OSError("boom")):
                self.assertIsNone(ns.safe_request("https://a.example/", respect_robots=True)[0])


class _Handler(http.server.BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def log_message(self, *a): pass
    def do_GET(self):
        if self.path == "/big":
            self.send_response(200); self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/plain"); self.end_headers()
            for _ in range(50):
                self.wfile.write(b"x" * 100_000)
        else:
            self.send_response(200); self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/plain")
            self.send_header("X-Host", self.headers.get("Host", "")); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(b"hello")


class RealSocketBehaviour(unittest.TestCase):
    """Exercise the real pinned-connection code against a loopback server."""
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        class Quiet(http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer):
            def handle_error(self, *a):          # clients closing early (body-cap test) is expected
                pass
        cls.srv = Quiet(("127.0.0.1", 0), _Handler)
        cls.port = cls.srv.server_address[1]
        threading.Thread(target=cls.srv.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()

    @classmethod
    def tearDownClass(cls):
        cls.srv.shutdown(); cls.srv.server_close()

    def test_connection_is_pinned_to_validated_ip(self):
        # The hostname does not resolve anywhere; the pinned IP is what gets connected to.
        r = ns._single_request(f"http://pinned.invalid:{self.port}/", "GET", {}, 5, ["127.0.0.1"], 10_000, True)
        self.assertEqual(r.text, "hello")
        self.assertTrue(r.header("X-Host").startswith("pinned.invalid"))

    def test_body_cap(self):
        r = ns._single_request(f"http://x.invalid:{self.port}/big", "GET", {}, 5, ["127.0.0.1"], 250_000, True)
        self.assertTrue(r.truncated)
        self.assertEqual(len(r.content), 250_000)

    def test_headers_only_probe_reads_no_body(self):
        r = ns._single_request(f"http://x.invalid:{self.port}/", "GET", {}, 5, ["127.0.0.1"], 10, False)
        self.assertEqual((r.status_code, r.content), (200, b""))

    def test_connection_failure_raises(self):
        with self.assertRaises(OSError):
            ns._single_request("http://x.invalid:9/", "GET", {}, 2, ["127.0.0.1"], 10, True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

tests/test_person.py101 lines
import json, unittest
from unittest import mock
from tests.helpers import FakeWeb, ns
from atlas.modules.person_module import investigate_person

def page(name, job=None, employer=None, awards=None, extra=""):
    d = {"@type": "Person", "name": name}
    if job: d["jobTitle"] = job
    if employer: d["worksFor"] = {"@type": "Organization", "name": employer}
    if awards: d["award"] = awards
    d.update({"address": {"addressLocality": "PrivateVille", "streetAddress": "9 Home Rd"},
              "sameAs": ["https://social.example/private"], "telephone": "+44 1234 567890", "email": "x@y.z"})
    return f'<title>{name}</title><script type="application/ld+json">{json.dumps(d)}</script>{extra}'


class Person(unittest.TestCase):
    def run_p(self, name="Jane Roe", pages=None, wiki=None):
        pages = pages or {}
        with FakeWeb() as web, mock.patch("atlas.modules.person_module.wikimedia.lookup_person",
                                          return_value=wiki or {"found": False, "errors": ["none"]}):
            for u, b in pages.items():
                web.add(u, b)
            return investigate_person(name, sources=list(pages))

    def test_no_information_does_not_invent_a_profile(self):
        r = self.run_p()
        self.assertEqual(r["professional"]["identity"]["status"], "no_information_located")
        self.assertIsNone(r["professional"]["biography"])
        self.assertEqual(r["professional"]["employment"], [])
        self.assertIn(r["confidence_summary"]["overall"]["level"], ("VERY LOW / UNVERIFIED",))
        self.assertTrue(r["search_hints"])                          # guidance still provided
        self.assertIn("does not build dossiers", r["professional"]["identity"]["explanation"])

    def test_ambiguous_name_is_not_merged(self):
        r = self.run_p(pages={"https://x.example/p": page("Jane Roe", "Chef", "Cafe")}, wiki={"ambiguous": True, "found": False})
        self.assertEqual(r["professional"]["identity"]["status"], "ambiguous")
        self.assertIn("cannot", r["professional"]["identity"]["explanation"])
        self.assertIsNone(r["professional"]["biography"])
        self.assertLessEqual(r["confidence_summary"]["overall"]["score"], 0.2)

    def test_conflicting_employers_are_not_merged(self):
        r = self.run_p(pages={"https://a.example/p": page("Jane Roe", "Chef", "Cafe"),
                              "https://b.example/p": page("Jane Roe", "Judge", "Court")})
        self.assertEqual(r["professional"]["identity"]["status"], "possible_multiple_people")
        self.assertEqual(len(r["professional"]["employment"]), 2)           # listed separately, per source

    def test_official_source_extracts_professional_facts_with_provenance(self):
        r = self.run_p(pages={"https://dept.uni.ac.uk/jane": page("Jane Roe", "Professor", "Uni", ["Prize 2020"])},
                       wiki={"found": True, "name_match": True, "title": "Jane Roe", "description": "Scientist",
                             "extract": "Jane Roe is a scientist.", "page_url": "https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jane_Roe",
                             "queried_at": "t"})
        p = r["professional"]
        self.assertEqual(p["identity"]["status"], "name_match_only")
        self.assertEqual(p["employment"][0]["job_title"], "Professor")
        self.assertEqual(p["employment"][0]["source_reliability"], "HIGH")
        self.assertEqual(p["achievements"][0]["achievement"], "Prize 2020")
        self.assertEqual(p["biography"]["reliability"], "MEDIUM")
        self.assertIn("CC BY-SA", p["biography"]["licence"])
        self.assertTrue(all(f["source"] for f in r["key_findings"]))

    def test_identity_confidence_is_never_high(self):
        r = self.run_p(pages={"https://dept.uni.ac.uk/jane": page("Jane Roe", "Professor", "Uni"),
                              "https://gov.uk/jane": page("Jane Roe", "Professor", "Uni")},
                       wiki={"found": True, "name_match": True, "title": "Jane Roe", "extract": "x",
                             "page_url": "https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jane_Roe", "queried_at": "t"})
        self.assertNotEqual(r["confidence_summary"]["overall"]["level"], "HIGH")
        self.assertLessEqual(r["confidence_summary"]["overall"]["score"], 0.80)

    def test_page_about_a_different_person_is_ignored(self):
        r = self.run_p(pages={"https://a.example/p": page("Someone Else", "Chef", "Cafe")})
        self.assertEqual(r["professional"]["sources"][0]["name_match"], "different_person")
        self.assertEqual(r["professional"]["employment"], [])

    def test_private_fields_are_never_collected(self):
        r = self.run_p(pages={"https://dept.uni.ac.uk/jane": page("Jane Roe", "Professor", "Uni")})
        blob = json.dumps(r)
        for banned in ("PrivateVille", "9 Home Rd", "social.example", "+44 1234", "x@y.z"):
            self.assertNotIn(banned, blob)

    def test_wikipedia_redirect_to_other_name_not_used(self):
        r = self.run_p(wiki={"found": True, "name_match": False, "title": "Other Name", "extract": "nope"})
        self.assertIsNone(r["professional"]["biography"])
        self.assertTrue(any("not used" in l for l in r["limitations"]))

    def test_interviews_are_not_fabricated(self):
        r = self.run_p()
        self.assertFalse(r["professional"]["published_interviews"]["automated"])

    def test_offline(self):
        ns.set_offline(True)
        try:
            r = investigate_person("Jane Roe", sources=["https://a.example/"])
        finally:
            ns.set_offline(False)
        self.assertEqual(r["professional"]["identity"]["status"], "no_information_located")
        self.assertTrue(any("Offline" in l or "offline" in l for l in r["limitations"]))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

tests/test_report.py137 lines
import json, os, stat, tempfile, time, unittest
from tests.helpers import FakeWeb, ns, read, write
from atlas import report as R
from atlas.utils import html_safe as hg, retention

EVIL = '<script>alert(1)</script>"><img src=x onerror=alert(2)>'
REPORT = {
    "target": "example.org", "target_type": "DOMAIN", "started_at": "t0", "completed_at": "t1", "offline": False,
    "classification": {"original_input": EVIL, "normalised": "example.org", "type": "DOMAIN", "confidence": 0.9, "notes": EVIL},
    "key_findings": [{"finding": EVIL, "source": "https://example.org/", "confidence": "HIGH"},
                     {"finding": "see [x]", "source": "https://example.org/]", "confidence": "LOW"}],
    "sections": {"dns": {"records": {"A": ["1.1.1.1"]}}, "geo": {"latitude": 1.0, "longitude": 2.0, "postal": "AB1", "country": "GB"}},
    "sources_used": ["https://rdap.org/domain/example.org", "DNS (test)", "javascript:alert(3)"],
    "confidence_summary": {"dns": {"level": "HIGH", "score": 0.9, "note": "n"}},
    "limitations": [EVIL], "legal_notes": ["UK GDPR ..."],
    "search_hints": [{"description": "manual", "url": "https://example.net/search?q=a"}],
}


class Rendering(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_ten_numbered_sections_in_text(self):
        self.assertEqual(len(R.SECTION_TITLES), 10)
        txt = R.render_text_report(REPORT)
        for n, title in enumerate(R.SECTION_TITLES, 1):
            self.assertIn(f"{n}. {title.upper()}", txt)

    def test_removed_sections_are_gone(self):
        txt = R.render_text_report(REPORT).lower()
        for gone in ("publicly available images", "approximate location information", "gallery"):
            self.assertNotIn(gone, txt)

    def test_coordinates_and_postcodes_scrubbed_at_render_time(self):
        txt = R.render_text_report(REPORT)
        for k in ("latitude", "longitude", "postal"):
            self.assertNotIn(k, txt)
        self.assertIn("country", txt)

    def test_html_is_safe(self):
        out = R.render_html_report(REPORT)
        low = out.lower()
        self.assertNotIn("<script", low); self.assertNotIn("<img", low)
        self.assertIn("&lt;script&gt;", out)
        self.assertIn("content-security-policy", low); self.assertIn("default-src 'none'", out)
        self.assertIn('name="referrer" content="no-referrer"', out)
        self.assertNotIn('href="javascript:', low)
        self.assertIn('rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow"', out)
        self.assertNotIn('example.org/]', out)                  # trailing bracket not swallowed into links
        for n, t in enumerate(R.SECTION_TITLES, 1):
            self.assertIn(f"{n}. {t}", out)

    def test_manual_search_hints_listed(self):
        self.assertIn("https://example.net/search?q=a", R.render_text_report(REPORT))

    def test_empty_minimal_report_renders(self):
        R.render_text_report({"target": "x"}); R.render_html_report({"target": "x"})

    def test_safe_link_helper(self):
        self.assertNotIn("<a ", hg.safe_link("javascript:alert(1)"))
        self.assertIn("<a ", hg.safe_link("https://a.example/x"))
        self.assertNotIn("<b>", hg.safe_link("https://a.example/x?q=<b>"))


class Export(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_exports_and_privacy(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
            paths = R.export_reports(REPORT, os.path.join(d, "out"), html=True)
            self.assertEqual(set(paths), {"json", "txt", "html"})
            self.assertEqual(len({os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(p))[0] for p in paths.values()}), 1)
            data = json.loads(read(paths["json"]))
            for k in ("latitude", "longitude", "postal"):
                self.assertNotIn(f'"{k}"', json.dumps(data))
            self.assertEqual(data["sections"]["geo"]["country"], "GB")
            if os.name == "posix":
                for p in paths.values():
                    self.assertEqual(stat.S_IMODE(os.stat(p).st_mode), 0o600)
                self.assertEqual(stat.S_IMODE(os.stat(os.path.join(d, "out")).st_mode) & 0o077, 0)

    def test_original_does_not_get_mutated(self):
        R.prepare_for_export(REPORT)
        self.assertIn("latitude", json.dumps(REPORT))

    def test_raw_payload_stripped_unless_requested(self):
        rep = {"target": "x", "sections": {"rdap": {"raw_summary": {"a": 1}, "data": {"big": "payload"}}}}
        self.assertNotIn("big", json.dumps(R.prepare_for_export(rep)))
        self.assertIn("big", json.dumps(R.prepare_for_export(rep, include_raw=True)))

    def test_hostile_target_in_filename(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
            rep = dict(REPORT, target="../../etc/passwd\x00 <x>")
            for p in R.export_reports(rep, d).values():
                self.assertEqual(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(p)), os.path.abspath(d))


class Retention(unittest.TestCase):
    def make(self, d, name, age_days=0):
        for ext in (".json", ".txt"):
            p = os.path.join(d, name + ext); write(p, "x")
            t = time.time() - age_days * 86400; os.utime(p, (t, t))

    def test_list_delete_purge(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
            self.make(d, "atlas_a_20260101T000000Z", 40); self.make(d, "atlas_b_20260102T000000Z", 1)
            write(os.path.join(d, "notes.txt"), "mine")
            self.assertEqual(len(retention.list_reports(d)), 2)
            self.assertEqual(retention.purge_older_than(d, 30), 2)
            self.assertEqual([r["name"] for r in retention.list_reports(d)], ["atlas_b_20260102T000000Z"])
            self.assertEqual(retention.delete_report(d, "atlas_b_20260102T000000Z.json"), 2)
            self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(os.path.join(d, "notes.txt")))

    def test_path_traversal_rejected(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
            sub = os.path.join(d, "reports"); os.mkdir(sub)
            victim = os.path.join(d, "atlas_x_20260101T000000Z.json"); write(victim, "keep")
            for bad in ("../atlas_x_20260101T000000Z", "..", "/etc/passwd", "atlas_x", "*", "atlas_../../x_20260101T000000Z"):
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError, msg=bad):
                    retention.delete_report(sub, bad)
            self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(victim))

    def test_symlink_escape_not_followed(self):
        if os.name != "posix":
            self.skipTest("posix only")
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
            sub = os.path.join(d, "r"); os.mkdir(sub)
            secret = os.path.join(d, "secret.json"); write(secret, "keep")
            os.symlink(secret, os.path.join(sub, "atlas_s_20260101T000000Z.json"))
            self.assertEqual(retention.delete_report(sub, "atlas_s_20260101T000000Z"), 0)
            self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(secret))

    def test_delete_all_and_missing_dir(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
            self.make(d, "atlas_a_20260101T000000Z"); self.assertEqual(retention.delete_all(d), 2)
        self.assertEqual(retention.list_reports("/nonexistent/dir"), [])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

tests/test_username.py285 lines
import json, re, unittest
from unittest import mock
from tests.helpers import FakeWeb, ns, patch_agent_network
from atlas.agent import AtlasAgent
from atlas.classifier import TargetType as T, classify, parse_forced_type
from atlas.modules.username_module import IDENTITY_WARNING, investigate_username
from atlas.sources import username_sources as us
from atlas.sources.username_sources import Platform, check_platform, validate_username

GH = Platform("Alpha", "developer", "https://alpha.example/{}")
MSG = Platform("Beta", "community", "https://beta.example/user?id={}", kind="message", absent_text="No such user.")
JS = Platform("Gamma", "community", "https://gamma.example/api?u={}", kind="json", profile_url="https://gamma.example/u/{}",
              exists_path=("users", 0, "id"), absent_path=("users", 0, "missing"))
FMT = Platform("Delta", "gaming", "https://delta.example/{}", pattern=r"^[a-z]{3}$")


class Validation(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_usernames(self):
        for ok in ("jdoe", "a", "john.smith_99", "A-b", "x" * 39):
            self.assertTrue(validate_username(ok)[0], ok)
        for bad in ("", "-x", ".x", "a b", "a/b", "../x", "a?b=c", "a#b", "a:b@c", "x" * 40, "é", "a\nb", "%2e", "a\x00"):
            self.assertFalse(validate_username(bad)[0], repr(bad))

    def test_classification(self):
        for s, norm in (("@jdoe_42", "jdoe_42"), ("john_smith99", "john_smith99"), ("@john.smith", "john.smith")):
            r = classify(s); self.assertEqual((r.target_type, r.normalised), (T.USERNAME, norm), s)
        for s, t in (("Microsoft", T.ORGANISATION), ("Tim Cook", T.PERSON), ("example.com", T.DOMAIN), ("8.8.8.8", T.IP),
                     ("Apple Inc", T.ORGANISATION), ("john.smith", T.DOMAIN)):
            self.assertEqual(classify(s).target_type, t, s)
        self.assertEqual(classify("jdoe", parse_forced_type("username")).target_type, T.USERNAME)
        self.assertEqual(classify("@jdoe", parse_forced_type("handle")).normalised, "jdoe")
        self.assertEqual(classify("a b", parse_forced_type("username")).target_type, T.UNKNOWN)

    def test_default_platform_list_policy(self):
        names = [p.name for p in us.PLATFORMS]
        self.assertEqual(len(names), len(set(names)))
        banned = re.compile(r"dating|adult|porn|tinder|grindr|onlyfans|whatsapp|telegram|signal|snapchat|bank|health", re.I)
        for p in us.PLATFORMS:
            self.assertTrue(p.url.startswith("https://"), p.name)
            self.assertEqual(p.url.count("{}"), 1, p.name)
            self.assertNotIn(p.category, us.EXCLUDED_CATEGORIES, p.name)
            self.assertFalse(banned.search(p.name + p.url), p.name)
            if p.kind == "message": self.assertTrue(p.absent_text, p.name)
            if p.kind == "json": self.assertTrue(p.exists_path or p.absent_path, p.name)
        manual = " ".join(n for n, _ in us.MANUAL_LINKS)
        self.assertIn("Instagram", manual)
        self.assertFalse({n for n, _ in us.MANUAL_LINKS} & set(names))        # blocked sites are never queried

    def test_url_building_quotes_username(self):
        self.assertEqual(us._fill("https://a.example/{}", "x.y_z-1"), "https://a.example/x.y_z-1")
        self.assertEqual(us._fill("https://a.example/{}", "a/b"), "https://a.example/a%2Fb")    # cannot add a path segment


class PlatformChecks(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_status_found_with_clean_control(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.add("https://alpha.example/jdoe", "<html>profile</html>")
            r = check_platform(GH, "jdoe")
        self.assertEqual((r["status"], r["control_check"]), ("found", "passed"))
        self.assertEqual(r["profile_url"], "https://alpha.example/jdoe")

    def test_body_is_capped_and_page_content_is_not_kept(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web, mock.patch.object(ns, "_single_request", wraps=web._single) as sp:
            web.add("https://alpha.example/jdoe", "<title>jdoe</title><p>bio: lives in Leeds</p>")
            r = check_platform(GH, "jdoe")
        calls = [c for c in sp.call_args_list if c[0][0].startswith("https://alpha.example/jdoe")]
        self.assertTrue(calls and all(c[0][5] <= 100_000 for c in calls))        # max_bytes cap
        self.assertNotIn("Leeds", json.dumps(r))

    def test_not_found(self):
        with FakeWeb():
            self.assertEqual(check_platform(GH, "jdoe")["status"], "not_found")

    def test_message_kind(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.add("https://beta.example/user?id=jdoe", "<p>karma: 5</p>")
            web.add("https://beta.example/user?id=nobody", "<p>No such user.</p>")
            self.assertEqual(check_platform(MSG, "jdoe")["status"], "found")
            self.assertEqual(check_platform(MSG, "nobody")["status"], "not_found")

    def test_json_kind_and_human_profile_url(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.add("https://gamma.example/api?u=jdoe", json.dumps({"users": [{"id": 7, "name": "jdoe"}]}), ctype="application/json")
            web.add("https://gamma.example/api?u=ghost", json.dumps({"users": [{"missing": ""}]}), ctype="application/json")
            web.add("https://gamma.example/api?u=weird", "not json", ctype="application/json")
            web.add("https://gamma.example/api?u=odd", json.dumps({"other": 1}), ctype="application/json")
            r = check_platform(JS, "jdoe")
            self.assertEqual((r["status"], r["profile_url"]), ("found", "https://gamma.example/u/jdoe"))
            self.assertEqual(check_platform(JS, "ghost")["status"], "not_found")
            self.assertEqual(check_platform(JS, "weird")["status"], "inconclusive")
            self.assertEqual(check_platform(JS, "odd")["status"], "inconclusive")

    def test_soft_404_platform_is_marked_unreliable_not_found(self):
        with FakeWeb(default=(200, {"content-type": "text/html"}, b"<html>app shell</html>")):
            r = check_platform(GH, "jdoe")
        self.assertEqual((r["status"], r["control_check"]), ("unreliable", "failed"))

    def test_inconclusive_and_errors(self):
        for code in (403, 429, 500, 503):
            with FakeWeb() as web:
                web.add("https://alpha.example/jdoe", "", status=code)
                self.assertEqual(check_platform(GH, "jdoe")["status"], "inconclusive", code)
        with FakeWeb() as web:                       # robots.txt fine, profile request itself fails -> error
            def fail_profile(url, *a):
                if url.endswith("/robots.txt"):
                    return web._single(url, *a)
                raise OSError("boom")
            with mock.patch.object(ns, "_single_request", side_effect=fail_profile):
                self.assertEqual(check_platform(GH, "jdoe")["status"], "error")
        with FakeWeb(), mock.patch.object(ns, "_single_request", side_effect=OSError("boom")):
            # nothing is reachable, so robots.txt cannot be verified either: skipped, never assumed allowed
            self.assertEqual(check_platform(GH, "jdoe")["status"], "skipped_robots")

    def test_redirect_to_other_page_is_inconclusive_but_case_canonicalisation_is_fine(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.add("https://alpha.example/jdoe", "", 302, headers={"location": "/login"})
            web.add("https://alpha.example/login", "login page")
            self.assertEqual(check_platform(GH, "jdoe")["status"], "inconclusive")
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.add("https://alpha.example/JDoe", "", 301, headers={"location": "/jdoe"})
            web.add("https://alpha.example/jdoe", "profile")
            self.assertEqual(check_platform(GH, "JDoe")["status"], "found")

    def test_robots_disallow_means_no_profile_request(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.add("https://alpha.example/robots.txt", "User-agent: *\nDisallow: /\n", ctype="text/plain")
            web.add("https://alpha.example/jdoe", "profile")
            r = check_platform(GH, "jdoe")
            self.assertEqual(r["status"], "skipped_robots")
            self.assertNotIn(("GET", "https://alpha.example/jdoe"), web.requests)

    def test_incompatible_format_makes_no_request(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            r = check_platform(FMT, "jdoe_42")
            self.assertEqual(r["status"], "skipped_format"); self.assertEqual(web.requests, [])

    def test_offline(self):
        ns.set_offline(True)
        try:
            self.assertEqual(check_platform(GH, "jdoe")["status"], "offline")
        finally:
            ns.set_offline(False)

    def test_never_contacts_non_public_hosts(self):
        with FakeWeb(resolver=lambda h: ["10.0.0.1"]) as web:
            self.assertEqual(check_platform(GH, "jdoe")["status"], "error"); self.assertEqual(web.requests, [])


class ModuleReport(unittest.TestCase):
    PLATS = [GH, MSG, JS, FMT, Platform("Epsilon", "creative", "https://eps.example/{}")]

    def web(self):
        web = FakeWeb()
        web.__enter__(); self.addCleanup(web.__exit__, None, None, None)
        web.add("https://alpha.example/jdoe", "p")
        web.add("https://beta.example/user?id=jdoe", "p")
        web.add("https://eps.example/jdoe", "", status=429)
        return web

    def test_report_structure_and_honest_statuses(self):
        web = self.web()
        r = investigate_username("@jdoe", platforms=self.PLATS)
        self.assertEqual((r["target"], r["target_type"]), ("jdoe", "USERNAME"))
        found = {a["platform"]: a for a in r["accounts"]["found"]}
        self.assertEqual(set(found), {"Alpha", "Beta"})
        for a in found.values():
            self.assertEqual(a["identity_confidence"], "UNVERIFIED"); self.assertEqual(a["existence_confidence"], "MEDIUM")
            self.assertTrue(a["profile_url"].startswith("https://"))
        s = r["sections"]["scan_summary"]
        self.assertEqual((s["matches"], s["not_found"], s["skipped"], s["inconclusive_or_error"]), (2, 1, 1, 1))
        self.assertEqual(r["confidence_summary"]["identity_linkage"]["level"], "UNVERIFIED")
        self.assertTrue(any("does NOT show" in l for l in r["limitations"]))
        self.assertTrue(all(f["source"] for f in r["key_findings"]))
        self.assertTrue(any("Epsilon" in l for l in r["limitations"]))                # inconclusive named

    def test_manual_links_listed_but_never_fetched(self):
        web = self.web()
        r = investigate_username("jdoe", platforms=self.PLATS)
        urls = [h["url"] for h in r["search_hints"]]
        self.assertTrue(any("instagram.com/jdoe" in u for u in urls))
        fetched = " ".join(u for _, u in web.requests)
        for host in ("instagram", "x.com", "tiktok", "facebook", "linkedin", "reddit", "youtube"):
            self.assertNotIn(host, fetched)

    def test_no_match_is_reported_honestly(self):
        with FakeWeb():
            r = investigate_username("jdoe", platforms=self.PLATS)
        self.assertEqual(r["accounts"]["found"], [])
        self.assertIn("No match", r["key_findings"][0]["finding"])
        self.assertEqual(r["confidence_summary"]["account_existence"]["level"], "LOW")

    def test_stop_at_first(self):
        web = self.web()
        r = investigate_username("jdoe", platforms=[GH] + [Platform(f"P{i}", "x", f"https://p{i}.example/{{}}") for i in range(20)],
                                 stop_at_first=True, max_workers=1)
        self.assertEqual(len(r["accounts"]["found"]), 1)
        self.assertTrue(r["sections"]["scan_summary"]["stopped_at_first_match"])
        self.assertLess(r["accounts"]["checked"], 21)
        self.assertTrue(any("stopped at the first match" in l.lower() for l in r["limitations"]))

    def test_one_crashing_platform_does_not_sink_the_scan(self):
        web = self.web()
        real = us.check_platform
        def flaky(p, u):
            if p.name == "Beta": raise RuntimeError("boom")
            return real(p, u)
        with mock.patch("atlas.modules.username_module.check_platform", side_effect=flaky):
            r = investigate_username("jdoe", platforms=self.PLATS)
        self.assertEqual([a["platform"] for a in r["accounts"]["found"]], ["Alpha"])
        self.assertTrue(any(x["platform"] == "Beta" for x in r["accounts"]["inconclusive"]))

    def test_invalid_username_makes_no_requests(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            for bad in ("a b", "../etc", "", "x" * 50):
                r = investigate_username(bad, platforms=self.PLATS)
                self.assertIn("Invalid username", r["error"])
            self.assertEqual(web.requests, [])

    def test_offline(self):
        ns.set_offline(True)
        try:
            with mock.patch.object(ns, "_single_request", side_effect=AssertionError("network used")):
                r = investigate_username("jdoe")
        finally:
            ns.set_offline(False)
        self.assertEqual(r["accounts"]["status"], "offline"); self.assertEqual(r["accounts"]["found"], [])

    def test_no_profile_content_is_stored(self):
        web = self.web()
        web.add("https://alpha.example/jdoe", "<h1>Jane Doe</h1> bio: lives in Leeds, email jane@x.y")
        blob = json.dumps(investigate_username("jdoe", platforms=[GH]))
        for banned in ("Jane Doe", "Leeds", "jane@x.y"):
            self.assertNotIn(banned, blob)


class AgentAndCLI(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_agent_flow_with_default_platforms_and_export(self):
        import tempfile, os
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d, FakeWeb() as web:
            web.add("https://github.com/jdoe_42", "x")      # GitHub rejects underscores -> skipped_format; try a valid one
            web.add("https://codeberg.org/jdoe_42", "profile")
            r = AtlasAgent(output_dir=d).investigate("@jdoe_42", html=True)
            self.assertEqual(r["target_type"], "USERNAME")
            self.assertEqual([a["platform"] for a in r["accounts"]["found"]], ["Codeberg"])
            gh = next(x for x in r["accounts"]["skipped"] if x["platform"] == "GitHub")
            self.assertEqual(gh["status"], "skipped_format")
            self.assertNotIn("images", r); self.assertNotIn("location", r)
            self.assertEqual(r["accounts"]["platform_list"], "built-in list")
            txt = open(r["export"]["txt"], encoding="utf-8").read()
            self.assertIn("MATCH – Codeberg", txt); self.assertIn("same person", txt)
            self.assertIn("username_account_enumeration", r["legal_assessment"]["activity_classification"])
            html = open(r["export"]["html"], encoding="utf-8").read()
            self.assertNotIn("<script", html.lower()); self.assertIn("https://codeberg.org/jdoe_42", html)


    def test_cli_and_interactive(self):
        import contextlib, io, tempfile
        import main as m
        def run(argv):
            out = io.StringIO()
            with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out):
                try: code = m.main(argv)
                except SystemExit as e: code = e.code
            return code, out.getvalue()
        self.addCleanup(lambda: ns.set_offline(False))
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.add("https://codeberg.org/jdoe", "p")
            code, out = run(["username", "jdoe", "--no-save", "--no-report", "--stop-at-first"])
            self.assertEqual(code, 0); self.assertIn("Username presence", out); self.assertIn("https://codeberg.org/jdoe", out)
            self.assertIn("Same username", out); self.assertIn("instagram.com/jdoe", out)
            code, out = run(["@jdoe", "--no-save", "--no-report"])
            self.assertIn("Classified as USERNAME", out)
        code, out = run(["username", "bad name", "--no-save"]); self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        code, out = run(["username", "--offline", "jdoe", "--no-save", "--no-report"]); self.assertEqual(code, 0)
        self.assertIn("nothing can be said", out); self.assertNotIn("No matches", out)
        lines = iter(["username", "username jdoe --stop-at-first", "exit"])
        with mock.patch("builtins.input", side_effect=lambda *a: next(lines)), contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()) as o, \
             FakeWeb():
            m.run_interactive(m.AtlasAgent(output_dir=tempfile.mkdtemp(), save=False))
        self.assertIn("Usage: username", o.getvalue()); self.assertIn("Username presence", o.getvalue())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

tests/test_username_accuracy.py303 lines
import contextlib, io, json, os, re, tempfile, unittest
from unittest import mock
from tests.helpers import FakeWeb, ns, write
from atlas.modules.username_module import investigate_username, run_selftest
from atlas.sources import username_sources as us
from atlas.sources.username_sources import Platform, check_platform, load_platforms, parse_platform, selftest_platform

P = Platform("Alpha", "developer", "https://alpha.example/{}")
CTRL = iter([])


def fixed_controls(*names):
    it = iter(names)
    return mock.patch.object(us, "control_name", side_effect=lambda p=None: next(it))


def html(title="", h1="", body="p"):
    return f"<html><head><title>{title}</title></head><body><h1>{h1}</h1>{body}</body></html>"


class Loader(unittest.TestCase):
    def load(self, rows, as_dict=True):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
            path = os.path.join(d, "p.json"); write(path, json.dumps({"platforms": rows} if as_dict else rows))
            return load_platforms(path)

    def test_valid_entry_and_defaults(self):
        ps, w = self.load([{"name": "Good", "category": "Developer", "url": "https://good.example/{}"}])
        self.assertEqual((len(ps), w), (1, []))
        p = ps[0]
        self.assertEqual((p.kind, p.category, p.absent_codes), ("status", "developer", (404, 410)))

    def test_bad_entries_are_skipped_with_reasons(self):
        rows = [
            {"name": "ok", "category": "x", "url": "https://ok.example/{}"},
            {"name": "http", "category": "x", "url": "http://plain.example/{}"},
            {"name": "noplaceholder", "category": "x", "url": "https://a.example/"},
            {"name": "two", "category": "x", "url": "https://a.example/{}/{}"},
            {"name": "ip", "category": "x", "url": "https://10.0.0.1/{}"},
            {"name": "date", "category": "Dating", "url": "https://d.example/{}"},
            {"name": "adult", "category": "adult", "url": "https://d.example/{}"},
            {"name": "msg", "category": "messaging", "url": "https://d.example/{}"},
            {"name": "m", "category": "x", "url": "https://m.example/{}", "kind": "message"},
            {"name": "j", "category": "x", "url": "https://j.example/{}", "kind": "json"},
            {"name": "re", "category": "x", "url": "https://r.example/{}", "pattern": "("},
            {"name": "kind", "category": "x", "url": "https://k.example/{}", "kind": "execute"},
            {"name": "codes", "category": "x", "url": "https://c.example/{}", "absent_codes": [200]},
            {"name": "pu", "category": "x", "url": "https://p.example/{}", "profile_url": "javascript:{}"},
            {"name": "ok", "category": "x", "url": "https://dup.example/{}"},
            "not an object", {"category": "x"},
        ]
        ps, w = self.load(rows)
        self.assertEqual([p.name for p in ps], ["ok"])
        self.assertEqual(len(w), len(rows) - 1)
        joined = " ".join(w)
        for needle in ("https://", "placeholder", "IP address", "dating", "adult", "messaging", "absent_text", "exists_path",
                       "duplicate"):
            self.assertIn(needle, joined, needle)

    def test_top_level_list_accepted_and_unreadable_files_reported(self):
        ps, w = self.load([{"name": "A", "category": "x", "url": "https://a.example/{}"}], as_dict=False)
        self.assertEqual(len(ps), 1)
        self.assertEqual(load_platforms("/nonexistent/p.json")[0], [])
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
            bad = os.path.join(d, "b.json"); write(bad, "{not json")
            ps, w = load_platforms(bad); self.assertEqual(ps, []); self.assertTrue(w)
            big = os.path.join(d, "big.json"); write(big, "x" * 1_100_000)
            ps, w = load_platforms(big); self.assertEqual(ps, []); self.assertIn("larger than 1 MB", w[0])
            wrong = os.path.join(d, "w.json"); write(wrong, json.dumps({"platforms": "nope"}))
            self.assertEqual(load_platforms(wrong)[0], [])

    def test_platform_cap(self):
        rows = [{"name": f"P{i}", "category": "x", "url": f"https://p{i}.example/{{}}"} for i in range(350)]
        ps, w = self.load(rows)
        self.assertEqual(len(ps), us.MAX_PLATFORMS); self.assertTrue(any("first 300" in x for x in w))


class ShippedList(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_policy_and_quality(self):
        ps = us.PLATFORMS
        self.assertEqual(us._BUILTIN_WARNINGS, [])
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(ps), 70)
        names = [p.name for p in ps]; self.assertEqual(len(names), len(set(names)))
        banned = re.compile(r"dating|adult|porn|tinder|grindr|onlyfans|whatsapp|telegram|signal|snapchat|discord|bank|health", re.I)
        for p in ps:
            self.assertTrue(p.url.startswith("https://") and p.url.count("{}") == 1, p.name)
            self.assertNotIn(p.category, us.EXCLUDED_CATEGORIES, p.name)
            self.assertFalse(banned.search(p.name + p.url), p.name)
            if p.known_good and p.pattern:
                self.assertRegex(p.known_good, p.pattern, p.name)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(sum(1 for p in ps if p.known_good), 15)
        blocked = {n for n, _ in us.MANUAL_LINKS}
        self.assertFalse(blocked & set(names))
        from urllib.parse import urlparse
        hosts = {urlparse(p.url.replace("{}", "u")).hostname for p in ps}
        for site in ("instagram.com", "x.com", "twitter.com", "tiktok.com", "facebook.com", "linkedin.com", "reddit.com"):
            self.assertFalse([h for h in hosts if h == site or h.endswith("." + site)], site)

    def test_control_names_fit_every_platform(self):
        for p in us.PLATFORMS:
            for _ in range(5):
                n = us.control_name(p)
                self.assertTrue(re.match(p.pattern or ".", n), (p.name, n))
        self.assertNotEqual(us.control_name(P), us.control_name(P))


class Accuracy(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_soft_404_pages_are_not_matches(self):
        for title, h1 in (("Page not found", ""), ("", "404"), ("Oops – this page doesn't exist", ""), ("No such user", ""),
                          ("Profile unavailable", ""), ("jdoe", "Sorry, we couldn't find that page")):
            with FakeWeb() as web:
                web.add("https://alpha.example/jdoe", html(title, h1))
                r = check_platform(P, "jdoe")
            self.assertEqual(r["status"], "not_found", (title, h1))
            self.assertIn("soft 404", r["detail"])

    def test_normal_pages_are_not_flagged_as_soft_404(self):
        for title in ("jdoe (@jdoe) · Alpha", "Notes and nothing", "Knotted – a knitting site", "Foundation"):
            with FakeWeb() as web, fixed_controls("c1", "c2"):
                web.add("https://alpha.example/jdoe", html(title))
                self.assertEqual(check_platform(P, "jdoe")["status"], "found", title)

    def test_confidence_is_graded_by_evidence(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web, fixed_controls("c1", "c2"):
            web.add("https://alpha.example/jdoe", html("jdoe's profile"))
            r = check_platform(P, "jdoe")
        self.assertEqual((r["existence_confidence"], r["control_check"], r["username_in_page"]), ("HIGH", "passed", True))
        self.assertIn("username appears", r["detail"])
        with FakeWeb() as web, fixed_controls("c1", "c2"):
            web.add("https://alpha.example/jdoe", html("Alpha"))
            r = check_platform(P, "jdoe")
        self.assertEqual((r["existence_confidence"], r["username_in_page"]), ("MEDIUM", False))

    def test_username_in_json_body_counts(self):
        chess = Platform("Chess", "gaming", "https://api.chess.example/player/{}")
        with FakeWeb() as web, fixed_controls("c1", "c2"):
            web.add("https://api.chess.example/player/jdoe", json.dumps({"username": "jdoe"}), ctype="application/json")
            self.assertEqual(check_platform(chess, "jdoe")["existence_confidence"], "HIGH")

    def test_either_control_looking_found_makes_the_platform_unreliable(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web, fixed_controls("c1", "c2"):
            web.add("https://alpha.example/jdoe", html("jdoe"))
            web.add("https://alpha.example/c2", html("anything"))                 # second control ALSO "exists"
            r = check_platform(P, "jdoe")
        self.assertEqual((r["status"], r["control_check"]), ("unreliable", "failed"))

    def test_inconclusive_control_lowers_confidence_but_keeps_the_match(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web, fixed_controls("c1", "c2"):
            web.add("https://alpha.example/jdoe", html("jdoe"))
            web.add("https://alpha.example/c2", "", status=403)
            r = check_platform(P, "jdoe")
        self.assertEqual((r["status"], r["control_check"], r["existence_confidence"]), ("found", "inconclusive", "LOW"))

    def test_two_control_requests_are_made_only_for_matches(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web, fixed_controls("c1", "c2"):
            web.add("https://alpha.example/jdoe", html("jdoe"))
            check_platform(P, "jdoe")
            self.assertEqual(sorted(u.rsplit("/", 1)[1] for _, u in web.requests if "robots" not in u), ["c1", "c2", "jdoe"])
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            check_platform(P, "nobody")
            self.assertEqual([u for _, u in web.requests if "robots" not in u], ["https://alpha.example/nobody"])

    def test_transient_failure_is_retried_but_long_retry_after_is_respected(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web, fixed_controls("c1", "c2"):
            web.sequence("https://alpha.example/jdoe", [(503, {}, b""), (200, {"content-type": "text/html"}, html("jdoe").encode())])
            self.assertEqual(check_platform(P, "jdoe")["status"], "found")
            self.assertEqual(web.sleeps, [1.5])
        with FakeWeb() as web:
            web.sequence("https://alpha.example/jdoe", [(429, {"retry-after": "120"}, b""), (200, {}, b"")])
            self.assertEqual(check_platform(P, "jdoe")["status"], "inconclusive")
            self.assertEqual(web.sleeps, [])

    def test_custom_absent_codes_and_empty_json(self):
        bsky = Platform("Bsky", "social", "https://api.bsky.example/profile?actor={}", kind="json", exists_path=("handle",),
                        absent_codes=(400, 404))
        gitlab = Platform("GL", "developer", "https://gl.example/users?username={}", kind="json", absent_if_empty=True,
                          exists_path=(0, "id"))
        with FakeWeb() as web, fixed_controls("c1", "c2", "c1", "c2"):
            web.add("https://api.bsky.example/profile?actor=ghost", json.dumps({"error": "Profile not found"}), status=400)
            self.assertEqual(check_platform(bsky, "ghost")["status"], "not_found")
            web.add("https://api.bsky.example/profile?actor=jdoe", json.dumps({"handle": "jdoe.bsky.social"}))
            web.add("https://api.bsky.example/profile?actor=c1", "{}", status=400)
            web.add("https://api.bsky.example/profile?actor=c2", "{}", status=400)
            self.assertEqual(check_platform(bsky, "jdoe")["status"], "found")
            web.add("https://gl.example/users?username=ghost", "[]")
            self.assertEqual(check_platform(gitlab, "ghost")["status"], "not_found")
            web.add("https://gl.example/users?username=jdoe", json.dumps([{"id": 5}]))
            web.add("https://gl.example/users?username=c1", "[]"); web.add("https://gl.example/users?username=c2", "[]")
            self.assertEqual(check_platform(gitlab, "jdoe")["status"], "found")

    def test_page_text_is_never_stored(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web, fixed_controls("c1", "c2"):
            web.add("https://alpha.example/jdoe", html("jdoe", "Jane Doe", "bio: lives in Leeds, jane@x.y"))
            blob = json.dumps(check_platform(P, "jdoe"))
        for banned in ("Jane Doe", "Leeds", "jane@x.y"):
            self.assertNotIn(banned, blob)


KG = Platform("Kg", "developer", "https://kg.example/{}", known_good="official")


class SelfTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def verdict(self, p, setup, ctrl="neg"):
        with FakeWeb() as web, fixed_controls(ctrl):
            setup(web)
            return selftest_platform(p)

    def test_healthy(self):
        r = self.verdict(KG, lambda w: w.add("https://kg.example/official", html("official")))
        self.assertEqual((r["verdict"], r["negative"], r["positive"]), ("healthy", "absent", "exists"))

    def test_stale_rule_when_known_good_account_is_reported_absent(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.verdict(KG, lambda w: None)["verdict"], "stale_rule")

    def test_unreliable_when_random_name_exists(self):
        r = self.verdict(KG, lambda w: w.add("https://kg.example/neg", html("anything")))
        self.assertEqual(r["verdict"], "unreliable")

    def test_blocked_and_unreachable(self):
        def robots(w): w.add("https://kg.example/robots.txt", "User-agent: *\nDisallow: /\n", ctype="text/plain")
        self.assertEqual(self.verdict(KG, robots)["verdict"], "blocked_by_robots")
        self.assertEqual(self.verdict(KG, lambda w: w.add("https://kg.example/neg", "", status=403))["verdict"], "unreachable_or_blocked")

    def test_negative_only_when_no_known_good_account(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.verdict(P, lambda w: None)["verdict"], "ok_negative_only")

    def test_positive_inconclusive(self):
        r = self.verdict(KG, lambda w: w.add("https://kg.example/official", "", status=429))
        self.assertEqual(r["verdict"], "positive_inconclusive")

    def test_run_selftest_summary_recommendations_and_offline(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d, FakeWeb() as web:
            path = os.path.join(d, "p.json")
            write(path, json.dumps({"platforms": [
                {"name": "Good", "category": "x", "url": "https://good.example/{}", "known_good": "official"},
                {"name": "Stale", "category": "x", "url": "https://stale.example/{}", "known_good": "official"},
                {"name": "Soft", "category": "x", "url": "https://soft.example/{}"},
                {"name": "Broken", "category": "dating", "url": "https://b.example/{}"}]}))
            web.add("https://good.example/official", html("official"))
            web.route("https://soft.example/", html("anything"), ctype="text/html")
            res = run_selftest(platforms_path=path)
        self.assertEqual(res["summary"], {"healthy": 1, "stale_rule": 1, "unreliable": 1})
        self.assertEqual(len(res["recommendations"]), 2)
        self.assertTrue(any("dating" in w for w in res["warnings"]))
        ns.set_offline(True)
        try:
            self.assertEqual(run_selftest()["status"], "offline")
        finally:
            ns.set_offline(False)


class ModuleAndCLI(unittest.TestCase):
    def write_list(self, d, rows):
        path = os.path.join(d, "mine.json"); write(path, json.dumps({"platforms": rows})); return path

    def test_custom_platform_list_end_to_end(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d, FakeWeb() as web, fixed_controls("c1", "c2"):
            path = self.write_list(d, [{"name": "Mine", "category": "x", "url": "https://mine.example/{}"},
                                       {"name": "Bad", "category": "adult", "url": "https://bad.example/{}"}])
            web.add("https://mine.example/jdoe", html("jdoe"))
            r = investigate_username("jdoe", platforms_path=path)
        self.assertEqual([a["platform"] for a in r["accounts"]["found"]], ["Mine"])
        self.assertIn("custom list", r["accounts"]["platform_list"])
        self.assertTrue(any("adult" in l for l in r["limitations"]))                  # skipped entry reported
        self.assertFalse([u for _, u in web.requests if "bad.example" in u])

    def test_empty_or_missing_custom_list_is_an_error_not_a_silent_fallback(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d, FakeWeb() as web:
            r = investigate_username("jdoe", platforms_path=os.path.join(d, "missing.json"))
            self.assertIn("No usable platforms", r["error"])
            r = investigate_username("jdoe", platforms_path=self.write_list(d, []))
            self.assertIn("No usable platforms", r["error"])
            self.assertEqual(web.requests, [])

    def test_confidence_summary_reflects_best_evidence(self):
        with FakeWeb() as web, fixed_controls("c1", "c2"):
            web.add("https://alpha.example/jdoe", html("jdoe"))
            r = investigate_username("jdoe", platforms=[P])
        self.assertEqual(r["confidence_summary"]["account_existence"]["level"], "HIGH")
        self.assertEqual(r["confidence_summary"]["identity_linkage"]["level"], "UNVERIFIED")

    def test_cli_selftest_and_platforms_option(self):
        import main as m
        def run(argv):
            out = io.StringIO()
            with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out):
                try: code = m.main(argv)
                except SystemExit as e: code = e.code
            return code, out.getvalue()
        self.addCleanup(lambda: ns.set_offline(False))
        code, out = run(["selftest", "--offline"]); self.assertEqual(code, 0)
        self.assertIn("Platform rule health check", out); self.assertIn("Offline mode", out)
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d, FakeWeb() as web, fixed_controls("c1", "c2"):
            path = self.write_list(d, [{"name": "Mine", "category": "x", "url": "https://mine.example/{}"}])
            web.add("https://mine.example/jdoe", html("jdoe"))
            code, out = run(["username", "jdoe", "--platforms", path, "--no-save", "--no-report"])
        self.assertEqual(code, 0); self.assertIn("https://mine.example/jdoe", out)
        self.assertNotIn("Instagram_", out)

CHANGELOG.md103 lines
# Changelog

## 3.0.0  – "lean, wider, more accurate"

### Removed (a full 2.1 snapshot was kept separately)
- VPN manager (`vpn` commands, tkinter window, platform detection), public-image discovery and the local gallery, and the approximate-location module.
  IP geolocation remains as network information inside IP reports. Reports now have 10 sections (was 12).

### Added – wider public reach (all keyless)
- **Wikidata** for people and organisations: disambiguation (candidate lists instead of guesses), community-recorded **official website**, occupation and employer (professional facts only).
- **Internet Archive** web history for domains (first/last capture, months with captures).
- **Related domains** from certificate co-listing (shared CDN certificates excluded) and **organisation certificate search** (`crt.sh O=`), both labelled LOW-confidence leads.
- **Provider inference** from DNS (email, DNS and sending services).
- **Username search: 79 platforms** in an editable `atlas/data/platforms.json`, `--platforms FILE` for your own list (validated; dating/adult/messaging/health/finance refused), more mastodon instances, Bluesky, GitLab, Codeforces, Minecraft, blogging/creative/gaming sites.

### Added – accuracy
- Username: soft-404 page detection, **two** control checks, username-in-page signal, graded HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW confidence, per-platform `absent_codes`, empty-JSON detection, and **`selftest`** (negative control + known-good accounts) to find stale/unreliable rules.
- Network: bounded retry for transient failures (429/502/503/504, connection errors); `Retry-After` above 5 s is respected by not retrying.
- IP: **two-provider geolocation cross-check** (ipinfo.io + ipwho.is over HTTPS) with explicit agreement/disagreement and confidence derived from it; registry-vs-geolocation country check.
- Organisations: candidate websites are **verified against the site's own name** (whole title segment for one-word names); unrelated look-alikes are labelled as such; more candidate TLDs (.co.uk, .org, .net); Wikidata site preferred over guessing.

### Changed
- Username page reads are capped at 100 KB and used only for two booleans (nothing stored).
- Test suite: 183 offline tests (removed-feature tests dropped; new tests for every addition).

### Fixed (found by the new tests)
- Organisation module: a local import shadowed `urlparse`; Wikidata sites are now fetched at their recorded URL.
- One-word organisation names accepted any page containing that word ("Example Widgets" for "Example Corp").


## 2.1.0

### Added
- **Username presence checks** (`username <name>`, `@name`, or a bare handle such as `john_smith99`; `--type username`; `--stop-at-first`).
  ATLAS requests ONE public profile/API URL per platform for the exact username (28 mainstream developer, community, creative and gaming platforms)
  and reports where a public profile exists.
  - **Control check:** when a name looks "found", a random name that cannot exist is tested on the same platform; if that also looks found the
    platform is reported as *unreliable* instead of producing a false match.
  - **Existence only:** no profile content is read or stored; status checks download no response body.
  - **Same username ≠ same person:** every match is labelled identity UNVERIFIED and the warning appears in the terminal, every report and the limitations.
  - robots.txt is honoured (platforms that disallow automated access are listed as skipped); usernames are validated per platform before any request;
    no spelling variants are tried; no dating, adult, messaging, health or finance platforms are included.
  - Platforms that block automated access (Instagram, X, TikTok, Facebook, LinkedIn, Reddit, YouTube, Threads, Pinterest) are never queried;
    manual links are printed for you to open yourself.
  - Per-platform outcomes: found / not found / unreliable / inconclusive (blocked, rate-limited, redirected) / skipped (robots, format) / error.
- New files: `sources/username_sources.py`, `modules/username_module.py`, `tests/test_username.py` (27 tests; 186 in total).

### Changed
- Reports for IP and username targets now say images are "Not applicable" (instead of "not run").
- Classifier: `@handle` and single tokens containing a digit or underscore are treated as usernames (low confidence; override with `--type`).

## 2.0.0

### Added
- **Public image discovery** (`images <target>`, `--no-images`, `--gallery`): finds image *references* via Open Graph, Twitter Card,
  `link rel=image_src` and JSON-LD; header-only validation (type allow-list, 8 MB cap, no SVG); licence/attribution hints;
  every image labelled **verified / probable / unverified** with a reason; images are linked, never downloaded or re-hosted.
- **Local HTML image gallery** (`gallery` command / `--gallery`), opened with `webbrowser` + `pathlib`; no JavaScript, strict CSP.
- **Approximate location module** (`location <target>`): coarse IP network location (LOW), hosting-network location for domains,
  and organisation-level published locations from JSON-LD (locality/region/country only). Each finding carries source, date accessed,
  precision, confidence (HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW/UNVERIFIED) and limitations. Standard message when nothing is verified.
- **VPN Manager** (`vpn status|connect|disconnect|gui`): adapters for Windows (PowerShell + `rasdial`), Linux (NetworkManager `nmcli`)
  and macOS (`scutil --nc`); terminal dashboard and optional tkinter window; explicit permission before any change;
  status verified against the OS after each action.
- **Person module upgrade**: Wikipedia summary (public endpoint) and user-supplied official pages (`--source URL`, max 5) →
  biography, roles, affiliations, education, achievements with per-source reliability; ambiguity detection; identity confidence
  capped below HIGH; published-interview limitation stated honestly.
- **IP module upgrade**: category detection (private, loopback, link-local, multicast, reserved, unspecified, documentation, CGNAT),
  IPv4-mapped IPv6, hosting/data-centre indicators, ASN, uncertainty warnings, zero network traffic for non-public addresses.
- **Report system**: 12-section structure shared by TXT / HTML / JSON; per-finding sources; source-reliability table;
  `--html`, `--no-save`, `--raw`; owner-only file permissions.
- **Retention controls**: `reports list|delete <name|all>|purge <days>`, `--list-reports`, `--delete-reports`, `--purge-older-than`.
- **Offline mode** (`--offline`, `offline on|off`): no sockets, no DNS, no lookups of any kind.
- **robots.txt support** (it was promised in 1.x but not implemented): checked before page and image access; unverifiable ⇒ not fetched.
- New files: `utils/network_safety.py`, `utils/html_gallery.py`, `utils/platform_detection.py`, `utils/source_reliability.py`,
  `utils/retention.py`, `sources/image_discovery.py`, `sources/location_sources.py`, `sources/page_metadata.py`,
  `sources/wikimedia.py`, `modules/image_module.py`, `modules/location_module.py`, `modules/vpn_module.py`, `vpn_gui.py`, `tests/`.

### Changed
- **Standard library only.** Removed `requests`, `dnspython`, `rich`, `beautifulsoup4`. ATLAS now includes a strict stdlib DNS client
  (UDP+TCP fallback, compression-loop protection, ID/question validation) and plain ANSI colouring.
- **Data minimisation:** geolocation no longer requests or keeps latitude/longitude/postcodes; exports and renders scrub those keys.
- HTTP layer rewritten on `http.client`: validated-IP connection pinning (anti DNS-rebinding), per-hop redirect validation,
  port/scheme/credential restrictions, body-size and total-time caps.
- CLI: `investigate`, `images`, `location`, `vpn`, `reports`; options may appear before or between target words (`parse_intermixed_args`).
- Terminal output strips control characters from all text (blocks escape-sequence injection from remote pages).
- Geolocation prefers HTTPS (`ipinfo.io`); cleartext `ip-api.com` is a flagged fallback only.

### Fixed (carried from 1.1.0 and new)
- Classifier: whole-word organisation matching, URL credentials/ports/IPv6, IP-host URLs, quoted names; credentials redacted from reports.
- Certificate-transparency filter accepted look-alikes (`evilexample.com`).
- Blocklist "query refused" (127.255.255.x) was reported as a listing; IPv6 support; defunct SORBS removed.
- RDAP registry fallbacks used only for matching TLDs; contact fields redacted from exports.
- DNS client raised `UnicodeError` instead of `DNSError` for over-long labels (found by the new tests).
- Report links swallowed a trailing `]`; `report` command rendered unscrubbed data.
- `--source` pointing at a private IP literal is now rejected up front and reported.

SECURITY_AND_LEGAL.md101 lines
# ATLAS – Security Restrictions, Privacy & Legal Limitations

ATLAS is a **passive OSINT research tool**. The restrictions below are implemented in code and covered by automated tests, not only stated here.

## 1. What ATLAS will not do

| Prohibited activity | Reason |
|---------------------|--------|
| Unauthorised access to systems; exploitation; attacks | Computer Misuse Act 1990 (UK) and equivalent laws |
| Password guessing, credential stuffing/theft | Same + ethical boundary |
| Bypassing authentication, paywalls, access controls or **robots.txt** | Legal, terms of service, good practice |
| Access to private accounts or non-public databases | Legal and ethical |
| Denial-of-service or resource exhaustion | Illegal and unethical |
| Live tracking of individuals | Privacy / data protection |
| Discovery of private residential locations or home addresses | UK GDPR / DPA 2018, safety |
| Collection of private personal data (personal phones, private accounts, credentials, family details) | UK GDPR / DPA 2018 |
| Linking usernames/accounts to a real-world person; trying username variants; storing profile content | Privacy; shared usernames are common, so attribution is unreliable and harmful when wrong |
| Facial recognition or identifying people from images | Privacy, accuracy, ethics |
| Reading Wikidata/Wikipedia personal properties (birth data, family, residence) | Privacy – only professional facts are requested |

ATLAS also cannot "find everything on the internet": it only uses public, permitted, keyless sources and reports what it could not check.

## 2. Technical safeguards (what the code does)

All outbound traffic passes through `atlas/utils/network_safety.py::safe_request`:

- **SSRF protection:** only `http`/`https`; no embedded credentials; ports limited to 80/443/8080/8443; every address a host resolves to must be globally routable (private, loopback, link-local, multicast, reserved, CGNAT and documentation ranges are refused).
- **DNS-rebinding protection:** the connection is made to the *already validated IP*.
- **Unsafe redirects:** max 5 hops, each re-validated; redirects to `file://` or internal hosts are refused.
- **robots.txt:** honoured for web pages and username platforms. If it cannot be verified (5xx, unreachable, offline) ATLAS treats it as *no automated access*.
- **Rate limiting & limits:** per-host delay, timeouts, 30 s total deadline, 1 MB body cap (200 KB robots.txt, 100 KB for username checks).
- **Bounded retries:** at most the requested number of repeats for connection errors and HTTP 429/502/503/504; a `Retry-After` above 5 s is **respected by not retrying** – ATLAS never works around rate limits.
- **Unsafe HTML rendering:** HTML reports contain **no JavaScript**, use `default-src 'none'`, escape every dynamic value, emit only http(s) links with `rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow"`.
- **Terminal safety:** every printed string has control characters (incl. ESC) stripped before colour is applied, so page content cannot inject terminal escape sequences.
- **Malformed responses:** DNS packets are validated (ID, question, bounds, compression loops); HTML/JSON parse failures never abort a report; platform lists are validated entry by entry.
- **Path traversal:** report names are matched against a strict pattern, resolved inside the reports directory, symlinks are not followed outside it, and only ATLAS-named files are deleted.
- **Sensitive data in reports:** vCard phone/email/address from RDAP are always redacted; latitude/longitude/postcode keys are scrubbed at export and render time; raw payloads omitted unless `--raw`; URL credentials are redacted.
- **Credentials:** ATLAS never asks for, accepts, stores or logs credentials; `--source` URLs containing credentials are rejected.
- **Logging:** WARNING by default; `-v` is opt-in. **Local storage:** owner-only permissions (`0600` files, `0700` directory on POSIX).

## 3. Network endpoints used (what needs the internet)

No API keys, paid services or third-party packages are used. Public endpoints:

| Purpose | Endpoint(s) | Notes |
|---------|-------------|-------|
| IP registration | `rdap.org`, `rdap.arin.net`, `rdap.db.ripe.net`, `rdap.apnic.net`, `rdap.lacnic.net`, `rdap.afrinic.net` | public RDAP |
| Domain registration | `rdap.org`, `rdap.verisign.com` (.com/.net), `rdap.nic.uk` (.uk) | public RDAP |
| Coarse IP geolocation | `ipinfo.io`, `ipwho.is` (both HTTPS, cross-checked); fallback `ip-api.com` (**cleartext HTTP**) | coordinates/postcodes discarded or not requested |
| Subdomains, related domains, organisation certificates | `crt.sh` (`q=` and `O=` searches) | Certificate Transparency; shared CDN certificates excluded from "related" |
| IP reputation | `zen.spamhaus.org`, `bl.spamcop.net` via your system DNS resolver | DNS blocklists |
| DNS records | your system resolvers (`/etc/resolv.conf`) | Windows: A/AAAA via the OS resolver |
| Websites | the investigated site, its `robots.txt` | one page |
| Web history | `web.archive.org` (CDX API) | capture dates only |
| Public figures / organisations | `en.wikipedia.org` REST API, `www.wikidata.org` API | professional facts only |
| Username presence | one public profile/API URL per platform listed in `atlas/data/platforms.json`, plus each host's `robots.txt` | existence only |
| User-supplied pages | URLs you pass with `--source` (max 5) | same safety rules |

`--offline` disables **all** of the above (no sockets, no DNS). Search-engine links printed for people/organisations are for *you* to open; ATLAS never fetches them.

## 4. Username presence checks – boundaries

- **Scope:** whether a public profile page exists for the *exact* username. Page text is read transiently (first 100 KB) only to compute two booleans (soft-404 and username-in-title); **nothing from it is stored**.
- **Platform policy:** mainstream developer, community, creative and gaming platforms. Dating, adult, messaging, health and finance categories are excluded – in the shipped list **and** in any custom `--platforms` file (such entries are skipped and reported). Platforms that restrict automated access are never queried; their URLs are printed for manual use.
- **Accuracy:** per-platform format validation; soft-404 detection; two control checks; graded confidence; `selftest` to find stale rules. Ambiguous results (blocked, rate-limited, redirected) are *inconclusive*, never matches.
- **Identity:** a shared username is not evidence of a shared owner. Every match is identity UNVERIFIED; the warning appears in the terminal, every report and the limitations.
- **Purpose:** appropriate uses include checking your own handle or brand, authorised security assessments and proportionate public-interest research – not locating, monitoring or harassing an individual. Platform terms may restrict automated checks; compliance is your responsibility.

## 5. Privacy defaults

- Person research uses Wikipedia/Wikidata professional facts and pages **you** supply; ambiguous names are listed as candidates, never merged; identity confidence is capped below HIGH.
- IP geolocation is described as an approximate *network* location and cross-checked; disagreement is reported as unreliable.
- **Retention controls:** `reports list|delete <name|all>|purge <days>`, `--list-reports`, `--delete-reports`, `--purge-older-than DAYS`, and `--no-save` to write nothing. Bulk deletion asks for confirmation and refuses in non-interactive sessions.
- Reports from ATLAS 1.0 may contain approximate coordinates/postcodes; delete them if not needed.

## 6. Legal classification guidance (not formal advice)

Every report contains a structured legal assessment:

- **Generally permitted public research** – RDAP, DNS, Certificate Transparency, Internet Archive capture dates, passive observation of publicly reachable pages (robots.txt honoured), DNS-based blocklist queries.
- **Generally permitted with caveats** – approximate geolocation of a public IP (can be personal data in context).
- **Requires careful assessment / a lawful basis** – research focused on a living individual; username presence checks.
- **Potentially restricted or regulated** – bulk/automated scraping of registries or social platforms.
- **Not permitted by the system** – unauthorised access, credential testing, private-data collection, facial recognition.

**Using public information does not automatically make an investigation lawful.** Legality depends on the jurisdiction, the **purpose**, the information involved, how it is collected, stored and used, and the circumstances.

### UK instruments considered

- **UK GDPR and Data Protection Act 2018** – information relating to an identifiable living person is personal data *even if public* (a name with a role, an IP address or a username can qualify). You need a lawful basis, a specific purpose, minimisation, accuracy, storage limitation and security; special-category data has extra protection and is not sought. Consider a DPIA for systematic person-focused work.
- **Computer Misuse Act 1990** – unauthorised access to computer material is an offence. ATLAS only requests publicly reachable resources, but *you* are responsible for how you use results.
- **PECR** – relevant if findings are used for electronic marketing.

## 7. Responsible use

For defensive security research, education, legitimate OSINT and journalistic or academic research using public sources. You are responsible for compliance with applicable law and with the terms of any service you query.

## 8. Reporting issues

If you find a way ATLAS could perform non-passive or non-public research, expose a residential location, attribute accounts to a person, or write outside its reports directory, treat it as a security defect: avoid that path and fix or report it.

examples/README.md37 lines
# Example reports

`examples/` holds sample outputs; `reports/` (created at runtime) holds your own exports.

## Original examples (ATLAS 1.0, live data at the time)

`example_ip_8.8.8.8.txt`, `example_domain_example.com.txt`, `example_org_Microsoft.txt`

These predate ATLAS 2/3. Version 1.0 reports could contain approximate coordinates and postcodes from IP geolocation; ATLAS 3 never collects or prints them.
Reports in `reports/` produced by 1.0 were left untouched – delete them with `python main.py reports delete <name>` if you do not need them.

## Offline examples (ATLAS 3.0, generated with `--offline`, no live data)

- `example_offline_private_ip_10.0.0.1.txt` – a private address: classified, no external lookups, why.
- `example_offline_person_Jane_Roe.txt` – a person lookup with nothing verifiable: the conservative "no information located" behaviour.
- `example_offline_username_jdoe_42.txt` – a username scan in offline mode: it says nothing can be concluded, and lists manual-lookup links.
- `example_offline_domain_report.html` – the HTML report format (no scripts, escaped, strict CSP).

Regenerate them with:

```bash
python main.py -t 10.0.0.1 --offline --no-report
python main.py investigate Jane Roe --offline --no-report
python main.py username jdoe_42 --offline --no-report
python main.py example.org --offline --html --no-report
```

Live runs need an internet connection:

```bash
python main.py -t 8.8.8.8
python main.py investigate example.com
python main.py investigate "Example Corp"
python main.py username jdoe_42
python main.py selftest            # check the username platform rules against the real sites
```

LEGAL & ETHICAL NOTES (NOT FORMAL LEGAL ADVICE)
------------------------------------------------------------------------
  • Querying public RDAP, DNS, and free geolocation services for an IP address is generally considered passive public research.
  • Do not attempt to map an IP to a specific individual or private residence without lawful authority.

  • Under UK GDPR / DPA 2018, IP addresses can be personal data in certain contexts. Processing must have a lawful basis if personal data is involved.
