------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Identity assessment   : no information located
  Explanation           : No public professional information could be matched automatically. ATLAS does not build dossiers on private individuals; supply an official page with --source <URL> if the person is a public figure.

  Published interviews: ATLAS has no search-engine access, so published interviews are not discovered automatically. Use the search links below and verify each result manually.

------------------------------------------------------------------------
6. SOURCES CONSULTED
------------------------------------------------------------------------
  • No external sources returned data.
  Recommended public search starting points (manual use):
  • Professional / public web presence: https://duckduckgo.com/?q=Jane+Roe
  • News coverage and published interviews: https://news.google.com/search?q=Jane+Roe+interview
  • Academic / publication search (if applicable): https://scholar.google.com/scholar?q=Jane+Roe
  • Official biography heuristic: https://duckduckgo.com/?q=Jane+Roe+official+biography

------------------------------------------------------------------------
7. SOURCE RELIABILITY
------------------------------------------------------------------------
  • No sources to rate.

------------------------------------------------------------------------
8. CONFIDENCE ASSESSMENTS
------------------------------------------------------------------------
  • overall: VERY LOW / UNVERIFIED (score 0.1) — No public professional information could be matched automatically. ATLAS does not build dossiers on private individuals; supply an official page with --source <URL> if the person is a public figure.

------------------------------------------------------------------------
9. LIMITATIONS
------------------------------------------------------------------------
  • Offline mode: Wikipedia and source pages were not fetched.
  • Automated person research is deliberately limited; results are leads to verify, not a dossier.
  • Identity matching is by name only unless an official page links the facts together.
  • Published interviews cannot be discovered automatically (no search-engine access).
  • Public figures still have privacy rights; purpose and proportionality matter.

------------------------------------------------------------------------
10. PRIVACY AND LEGAL CONSIDERATIONS
------------------------------------------------------------------------
  • Under UK GDPR and the Data Protection Act 2018, information relating to an identifiable living individual is personal data.
  • Even public information may be personal data and need a lawful basis, a stated purpose and proportionate handling.
  • Special category data (health, politics, religion, etc.) has additional protections and is not sought by ATLAS.
  • Using publicly available information does not by itself make an investigation lawful; legality depends on purpose, the information, how it is processed and the circumstances.
  • The Computer Misuse Act 1990 prohibits unauthorised access to computer material; ATLAS only requests public pages.
  • This is not formal legal advice.
  Operational boundary: ATLAS only assists with publicly available information relevant to a person's professional or public identity. It will not search for or report private addresses, personal phone numbers, private email accounts, passwords, family details, or non-public social media, and it does not build dossiers on private individuals.
  public rdap dns ct lookups: Generally permitted public research — RDAP, DNS and Certificate Transparency are designed as public data sources.
  passive http observation: Generally permitted public research — Requests to public pages honour robots.txt; a single request is visible in the site's logs.
  geolocation of public ip: Generally permitted public research (with caveats) — Approximate network location only. An IP can be personal data in context.
  blocklist reputation checks: Generally permitted public research — Standard passive DNS-based blocklist queries; indicators only.
  automated scraping of registries or social: Potentially restricted or regulated — May breach terms or data-protection rules. ATLAS avoids this.
  unauthorised access or credential testing: Not permitted by the system — Prohibited under the Computer Misuse Act 1990 and equivalent laws.
  collection of private personal data: Not permitted by the system — ATLAS does not seek private addresses, phones, accounts, credentials or residential locations.
  facial recognition or reverse image search: Not permitted by the system — Not implemented; ATLAS never identifies people from images.
  person focused research: Requires careful assessment; a lawful basis is needed — Information about an identifiable living individual is personal data even when public; UK GDPR principles (purpose limitation, minimisation, accuracy, storage limitation) apply.

  This report may contain personal data. It is stored locally with owner-only permissions. Delete it when no longer needed (`reports delete <name>`, `reports purge <days>`), keep it only for the stated purpose, and do not share it without a lawful basis.
  This assessment is provided for transparency only and is not formal legal advice. Using publicly available information does not automatically make an investigation lawful: the position depends on jurisdiction, the purpose of processing, the data involved, how it is collected and used, and the circumstances. Seek qualified legal advice for your use case.

10. PRIVACY AND LEGAL CONSIDERATIONS
------------------------------------------------------------------------
  • A username can be personal data under UK GDPR / DPA 2018 when it relates to an identifiable person; you need a lawful basis and a specific purpose.
  • Suitable purposes include checking your own handle or brand for impersonation, authorised security assessments and proportionate public-interest research. Do not use this to locate, monitor or harass an individual.
  • Platform terms of service may restrict automated checks; ATLAS honours robots.txt and queries one public URL per platform, but you remain responsible for compliance.
  • Using public information does not by itself make an investigation lawful; legality depends on purpose, the information, how it is processed and the circumstances.
  • This is not formal legal advice.
  Operational boundary: ATLAS only checks whether a public profile page exists for the EXACT username on a curated list of mainstream platforms that permit automated access. It does not read profile content, log in, bypass access controls, try spelling variants, or link accounts to a real-world person.
  public rdap dns ct lookups: Generally permitted public research — RDAP, DNS and Certificate Transparency are designed as public data sources.
  passive http observation: Generally permitted public research — Requests to public pages honour robots.txt; a single request is visible in the site's logs.
  geolocation of public ip: Generally permitted public research (with caveats) — Approximate network location only. An IP can be personal data in context.
  blocklist reputation checks: Generally permitted public research — Standard passive DNS-based blocklist queries; indicators only.
  automated scraping of registries or social: Potentially restricted or regulated — May breach terms or data-protection rules. ATLAS avoids this.
  unauthorised access or credential testing: Not permitted by the system — Prohibited under the Computer Misuse Act 1990 and equivalent laws.
  collection of private personal data: Not permitted by the system — ATLAS does not seek private addresses, phones, accounts, credentials or residential locations.
  facial recognition or reverse image search: Not permitted by the system — Not implemented; ATLAS never identifies people from images.
  username account enumeration: Requires careful assessment; purpose and proportionality matter — Checks only whether a public profile page exists for an exact username on platforms that permit automated access (robots.txt honoured). A username can be personal data; do not use results to identify, locate, monitor or harass an individual.

  This report may contain personal data. It is stored locally with owner-only permissions. Delete it when no longer needed (`reports delete <name>`, `reports purge <days>`), keep it only for the stated purpose, and do not share it without a lawful basis.
  This assessment is provided for transparency only and is not formal legal advice. Using publicly available information does not automatically make an investigation lawful: the position depends on jurisdiction, the purpose of processing, the data involved, how it is collected and used, and the circumstances. Seek qualified legal advice for your use case.
